1→"""CLI entry point: parse args, load shared context, dispatch command handlers."""
     2→

     9→from desloppify.app.cli_support.parser import create_parser as _create_parser
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.registry import get_command_handlers
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import LangResolutionError, resolve_lang
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import CommandRuntime
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path

    17→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize

    19→from desloppify.core.runtime_state import runtime_scope
    20→from desloppify.languages import available_langs
    21→from desloppify.state import load_state
    22→
    23→_DETECTOR_NAMES: list[str] | None = None
    24→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    25→# Backward-compatible test patch hook; runtime path resolution uses get_project_root().
    26→PROJECT_ROOT = get_project_root()
    27→_PROJECT_ROOT_SENTINEL = PROJECT_ROOT
    28→
    29→
    30→def _get_detector_names() -> list[str]:
    31→    """Return cached detector names, computing on first access."""
    32→    global _DETECTOR_NAMES
    33→    if _DETECTOR_NAMES is None:
    34→        _DETECTOR_NAMES = detector_names()
    35→    return _DETECTOR_NAMES
    36→
    37→
    38→def _invalidate_detector_names_cache() -> None:
    39→    """Invalidate detector-name cache when runtime registrations change."""
    40→    global _DETECTOR_NAMES
    41→    _DETECTOR_NAMES = None
    42→
    43→
    44→on_detector_registered(_invalidate_detector_names_cache)
    45→
    46→def create_parser():
    47→    """Return the top-level argparse parser."""
    48→    return _create_parser(langs=available_langs(), detector_names=_get_detector_names())
    49→
    50→
    51→def _apply_persisted_exclusions(args, config: dict):
    52→    """Merge CLI --exclude with persisted config.exclude and apply globally."""
    53→    cli_exclusions = getattr(args, "exclude", None) or []
    54→    persisted = config.get("exclude", [])
    55→    combined = list(cli_exclusions) + [e for e in persisted if e not in cli_exclusions]
    56→    if not combined:
    57→        return
    58→    set_exclusions(combined)
    59→    if cli_exclusions:
    60→        print(
    61→            colorize(f"  Excluding: {', '.join(combined)}", "dim"),
    62→            file=sys.stderr,
    63→        )
    64→        return
    65→    print(
    66→        colorize(
    67→            f"  Excluding (from config): {', '.join(combined)}", "dim"
    68→        ),
    69→        file=sys.stderr,
    70→    )
    71→
    72→
    73→def _resolve_default_path(args) -> None:
    74→    """Fill args.path from detected language or default source path.
    75→
    76→    For the review command, the last scan path (stored in state) is used as the
    77→    default so that ``desloppify review --prepare`` works on the same scope as
    78→    the preceding scan even when the project files are not under ``src/``.
    79→    """
    80→    if getattr(args, "path", None) is not None:
    81→        return
    82→    runtime_root = (
    83→        PROJECT_ROOT.resolve()
    84→        if PROJECT_ROOT is not _PROJECT_ROOT_SENTINEL and isinstance(PROJECT_ROOT, Path)
    85→        else get_project_root()
    86→    )
    87→    if getattr(args, "command", None) == "review":
    88→        try:
    89→            state_file = state_path(args)
    90→            if state_file:
    91→                saved = load_state(state_file)
    92→                saved_path = saved.get("scan_path")
    93→                if saved_path:
    94→                    args.path = str((runtime_root / saved_path).resolve())
    95→                    return
    96→        except (OSError, KeyError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError) as exc:
    97→            logger.debug("Failed to resolve default path from saved state: %s", exc)
    98→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    99→    if lang:
   100→        args.path = str(runtime_root / lang.default_src)
   101→    else:
   102→        args.path = str(get_default_path())
   103→
   104→
   105→def _load_shared_runtime(args) -> None:
   106→    """Load config/state and attach shared objects to parsed args."""
   107→    config = load_config()
   108→
   109→    state_file = state_path(args)
   110→    state = load_state(state_file)
   111→    _apply_persisted_exclusions(args, config)
   112→
   113→    args.runtime = CommandRuntime(config=config, state=state, state_path=state_file)
   114→
   115→
   116→def _resolve_handler(command: str):
   117→    return get_command_handlers()[command]
   118→
   119→
   120→def _handle_help_command(args, parser) -> None:
   121→    """Handle explicit help command when present in parser config."""
   122→    topic = list(getattr(args, "topic", []) or [])
   123→    try:
   124→        parser.parse_args([*topic, "--help"])
   125→    except SystemExit:
   126→        return
   127→
   128→
   129→def main() -> None:
   130→    # Ensure Unicode output works on Windows terminals (cp1252 etc.)
   131→    for stream in (sys.stdout, sys.stderr):
   132→        if hasattr(stream, "reconfigure"):
   133→            try:
   134→                stream.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
   135→            except (AttributeError, OSError):
   136→                logger.debug(
   137→                    "Skipping stream reconfigure for %s (not supported)",
   138→                    getattr(stream, "name", "<stream>"),
   139→                )
   140→
   141→    parser = create_parser()
   142→    args = parser.parse_args()
   143→    if args.command == "help":
   144→        _handle_help_command(args, parser)
   145→        return
   146→
   147→    try:
   148→        with runtime_scope():
   149→            _resolve_default_path(args)
   150→            _load_shared_runtime(args)
   151→
   152→            handler = _resolve_handler(args.command)
   153→            handler(args)
   154→    except LangResolutionError as exc:
   155→        print(colorize(f"  {exc.message}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   156→        sys.exit(1)
   157→    except KeyboardInterrupt:
   158→        print("\nInterrupted.")
   159→        sys.exit(1)
   160→
   161→
   162→if __name__ == "__main__":
   163→    main()
   164→

    29→USAGE_EXAMPLES = """
    30→core workflow:
    31→  scan       Run detectors, update state, show diff
    32→  status     Score dashboard with dimension health
    33→  next       Show next highest-priority item to work on
    34→  plan       Living plan: prioritize, cluster, skip, done, annotate
    35→
    36→investigation:
    37→  show       Dig into findings by file/dir/detector/ID
    38→  tree       Annotated codebase tree (zoom with --focus)
    39→  detect     Run a single detector directly (bypass state)
    40→
    41→maintenance:
    42→  fix        Auto-fix mechanical issues
    43→  ignore     Suppress findings matching a pattern
    44→  exclude    Exclude path pattern from scanning
    45→  move       Move file/dir and update import references
    46→  review     Holistic subjective review (LLM-based)
    47→
    48→setup & admin:
    49→  zone       Show/set zone classifications
    50→  config     Project configuration
    51→  viz        Interactive HTML treemap
    52→  langs      List language plugins
    53→  dev        Developer utilities
    54→  update-skill  Install/update agent skill document
    55→
    56→examples:
    57→  desloppify scan
    58→  desloppify scan --skip-slow --profile ci
    59→  desloppify plan                        # full prioritized markdown
    60→  desloppify plan queue                  # compact table of all items
    61→  desloppify next --count 10             # top 10 queue items
    62→  desloppify show src/components/Modal.tsx
    63→  desloppify plan done "unused::src/foo.tsx::React" \\
    64→    --note "removed import" --attest "I have actually ..."
    65→  desloppify review --run-batches --parallel --scan-after-import
    66→"""
    67→
    68→
    69→class _NoAbbrevArgumentParser(argparse.ArgumentParser):
    70→    """Argparse parser variant that disables long-option abbreviation."""
    71→
    72→    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
    73→        kwargs.setdefault("allow_abbrev", False)
    74→        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
    75→
    76→
    77→def _cli_version_string() -> str:
    78→    """Return the best available CLI version label."""
    79→    try:
    80→        return f"desloppify {get_version('desloppify')}"
    81→    except PackageNotFoundError:
    82→        return "desloppify (version unknown)"
    83→
    84→
    85→def create_parser(*, langs: list[str], detector_names: list[str]) -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    86→    """Build top-level CLI parser with all subcommands."""
    87→    lang_help = ", ".join(langs) if langs else "registered languages"
    88→
    89→    parser = _NoAbbrevArgumentParser(
    90→        prog="desloppify",
    91→        description="Desloppify — codebase health tracker",
    92→        epilog=USAGE_EXAMPLES,
    93→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
    94→    )
    95→    parser.add_argument(
    96→        "--lang",
    97→        type=str,
    98→        default=None,
    99→        help=f"Language to scan ({lang_help}). Auto-detected if omitted.",
   100→    )
   101→    parser.add_argument(
   102→        "--exclude",
   103→        action="append",
   104→        default=None,
   105→        metavar="PATTERN",
   106→        help="Path pattern to exclude (component/prefix match; repeatable)",
   107→    )
   108→    parser.add_argument(
   109→        "--version",
   110→        "-V",
   111→        action="version",
   112→        version=_cli_version_string(),
   113→    )
   114→    sub = parser.add_subparsers(
   115→        dest="command",
   116→        required=True,
   117→        parser_class=_NoAbbrevArgumentParser,
   118→    )
   119→    _add_scan_parser(sub)
   120→    _add_status_parser(sub)
   121→    _add_tree_parser(sub)
   122→    _add_show_parser(sub)
   123→    _add_next_parser(sub)
   124→    _add_ignore_parser(sub)
   125→    _add_exclude_parser(sub)
   126→    _add_fix_parser(sub, langs)
   127→    _add_plan_parser(sub)
   128→    _add_viz_parser(sub)
   129→    _add_detect_parser(sub, detector_names)
   130→    _add_move_parser(sub)
   131→    _add_review_parser(sub)
   132→    _add_zone_parser(sub)
   133→    _add_config_parser(sub)
   134→    _add_dev_parser(sub)
   135→    _add_langs_parser(sub)
   136→    _add_update_skill_parser(sub)
   137→    return parser
   138→
   139→
   140→__all__ = ["USAGE_EXAMPLES", "create_parser"]
   141→

1→"""fix command: auto-fix mechanical issues with fixer registry and pipeline."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands._show_terminal import show_fix_dry_run_samples
     9→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import FixResult
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    11→
    12→from .apply_flow import (
    13→    _apply_and_report,
    14→    _detect,
    15→    _print_fix_summary,
    16→    _report_dry_run,
    17→    _warn_uncommitted_changes,
    18→)
    19→from .options import _load_fixer
    20→from .review_flow import _cmd_fix_review
    21→
    22→
    23→def cmd_fix(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    24→    """Auto-fix mechanical issues."""
    25→    fixer_name = args.fixer
    26→    if fixer_name == "review":
    27→        _cmd_fix_review(args)
    28→        return
    29→
    30→    dry_run = getattr(args, "dry_run", False)
    31→    path = Path(args.path)
    32→
    33→    lang, fixer = _load_fixer(args, fixer_name)
    34→
    35→    if not dry_run:
    36→        _warn_uncommitted_changes()
    37→    entries = _detect(fixer, path)
    38→    if not entries:
    39→        print(colorize(f"No {fixer.label} found.", "green"))
    40→        return
    41→
    42→    raw = fixer.fix(entries, dry_run=dry_run)
    43→    if isinstance(raw, FixResult):
    44→        results = raw.entries
    45→        skip_reasons = raw.skip_reasons
    46→    else:
    47→        results = raw
    48→        skip_reasons = {}
    49→    total_items = sum(len(r["removed"]) for r in results)
    50→    total_lines = sum(r.get("lines_removed", 0) for r in results)
    51→    _print_fix_summary(fixer, results, total_items, total_lines, dry_run)
    52→
    53→    if dry_run and results:
    54→        show_fix_dry_run_samples(entries, results)
    55→
    56→    if not dry_run:
    57→        _apply_and_report(
    58→            args,
    59→            path,
    60→            fixer,
    61→            fixer_name,
    62→            entries,
    63→            results,
    64→            total_items,
    65→            lang,
    66→            skip_reasons,
    67→        )
    68→    else:
    69→        _report_dry_run(args, fixer_name, entries, results, total_items)
    70→    print()
    71→
```

1→<!-- desloppify-begin -->
     2→<!-- desloppify-skill-version: 2 -->
     3→---
     4→name: desloppify
     5→description: >
     6→  Codebase health scanner and technical debt tracker. Use when the user asks
     7→  about code quality, technical debt, dead code, large files, god classes,
     8→  duplicate functions, code smells, naming issues, import cycles, or coupling
     9→  problems. Also use when asked for a health score, what to fix next, or to
    10→  create a cleanup plan. Supports 28 languages.
    11→allowed-tools: Bash(desloppify *)
    12→---
    13→
    14→# Desloppify
    15→
    16→## 1. Your Job
    17→
    18→Maximise the **strict score** honestly. Your main cycle: **scan → plan → execute → rescan**. Follow the scan output's **INSTRUCTIONS FOR AGENTS** — don't substitute your own analysis.
    19→
    20→**Don't be lazy.** Do large refactors and small detailed fixes with equal energy. If it takes touching 20 files, touch 20 files. If it's a one-line change, make it. No task is too big or too small — fix things properly, not minimally.
    21→
    22→## 2. The Workflow
    23→
    24→Three phases, repeated as a cycle.
    25→
    26→### Phase 1: Scan and review — understand the codebase
    27→
    28→```bash
    29→desloppify scan --path .       # analyse the codebase
    30→desloppify status              # check scores — are we at target?
    31→```
    32→
    33→The scan will tell you if subjective dimensions need review. Follow its instructions. To trigger a review manually:
    34→```bash
    35→desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import
    36→```
    37→
    38→### Phase 2: Plan — decide what to work on
    39→
    40→After reviews, triage stages and plan creation appear as queue items in `next`. Complete them in order:
    41→```bash
    42→desloppify next                                        # shows the next workflow step
    43→desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "themes and root causes..."
    44→desloppify plan triage --stage reflect --report "comparison against completed work..."
    45→desloppify plan triage --stage organize --report "summary of priorities..."
    46→desloppify plan triage --complete --strategy "execution plan..."
    47→```
    48→
    49→Then shape the queue. **The plan shapes everything `next` gives you** — don't skip this step.
    50→
    51→```bash
    52→desloppify plan                          # see the full ordered queue
    53→desloppify plan move <pat> top           # reorder — what unblocks the most?
    54→desloppify plan cluster create <name>    # group related issues to batch-fix
    55→desloppify plan focus <cluster>          # scope next to one cluster
    56→desloppify plan skip <pat>              # defer — hide from next
    57→```
    58→
    59→More plan commands:
    60→```bash
    61→desloppify plan move <cluster> top       # move all cluster members at once
    62→desloppify plan move <a> <b> top         # mix clusters + findings in one move
    63→desloppify plan move <pat> before -t X   # position relative to another item/cluster
    64→desloppify plan cluster move a,b top     # move multiple clusters as one block
    65→desloppify plan done <pat>               # mark complete
    66→desloppify plan reopen <pat>             # reopen
    67→```
    68→
    69→### Phase 3: Execute — grind the queue to completion
    70→
    71→Trust the plan and execute. Don't rescan mid-queue — finish the queue first.
    72→
    73→**Branch first.** Create a dedicated branch for health work — never commit directly to main:
    74→```bash
    75→git checkout -b desloppify/code-health    # or desloppify/<focus-area>
    76→```
    77→
    78→**Set up commit tracking.** If you have a PR, link it for auto-updated descriptions:
    79→```bash
    80→desloppify config set commit_pr 42        # PR number for auto-updates
    81→```
    82→
    83→**The loop:**
    84→```
    85→1. desloppify next              ← what to fix next
    86→2. Fix the issue in code
    87→3. Resolve it (next shows you the exact command including required attestation)
    88→4. When you have a logical batch, commit:
    89→   git add <files> && git commit -m "desloppify: fix 3 deferred_import findings"
    90→5. Record the commit:
    91→   desloppify plan commit-log record      # moves findings uncommitted → committed, updates PR
    92→6. Push periodically:
    93→   git push -u origin desloppify/code-health
    94→7. Repeat until the queue is empty
    95→```
    96→
    97→Score may temporarily drop after fixes — cascade effects are normal, keep going.
    98→If `next` suggests an auto-fixer, run `desloppify fix <fixer> --dry-run` to preview, then apply.
    99→
   100→**When the queue is clear, go back to Phase 1.** New issues will surface, cascades will have resolved, priorities will have shifted. This is the cycle.
   101→
   102→### Other useful commands
   103→
   104→```bash
   105→desloppify next --count 5                         # top 5 priorities
   106→desloppify next --cluster <name>                  # drill into a cluster
   107→desloppify show <pattern>                         # filter by file/detector/ID
   108→desloppify show --status open                     # all open findings
   109→desloppify plan skip --permanent "<id>" --note "reason" --attest "..." # accept debt
   110→desloppify exclude <path>                         # exclude a directory from scanning
   111→desloppify config show                            # show all config including excludes
   112→desloppify scan --path . --reset-subjective       # reset subjective baseline to 0
   113→```
   114→
   115→## 3. Reference
   116→
   117→### How scoring works
   118→
   119→Overall score = **40% mechanical** + **60% subjective**.
   120→
   121→- **Mechanical (40%)**: auto-detected issues — duplication, dead code, smells, unused imports, security. Fixed by changing code and rescanning.
   122→- **Subjective (60%)**: design quality review — naming, error handling, abstractions, clarity. Starts at **0%** until reviewed. The scan will prompt you when a review is needed.
   123→- **Strict score** is the north star: wontfix items count as open. The gap between overall and strict is your wontfix debt.
   124→- **Score types**: overall (lenient), strict (wontfix counts), objective (mechanical only), verified (confirmed fixes only).
   125→
   126→### Subjective reviews in detail
   127→
   128→- **Preferred**: `desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` — does everything in one command.
   129→- **Manual path**: `desloppify review --prepare` → review per dimension → `desloppify review --import file.json`.
   130→- Import first, fix after — import creates tracked state entries for correlation.
   131→- Integrity: reviewers score from evidence only. Scores hitting exact targets trigger auto-reset.
   132→- Even moderate scores (60-80) dramatically improve overall health.
   133→- Stale dimensions auto-surface in `next` — just follow the queue.
   134→
   135→### Commit tracking & branch workflow
   136→
   137→Work on a dedicated branch named `desloppify/<description>` (e.g., `desloppify/code-health`, `desloppify/fix-smells`). Never push health work directly to main.
   138→
   139→```bash
   140→desloppify config set commit_pr 42              # link to your PR
   141→desloppify plan commit-log                      # see uncommitted + committed status
   142→desloppify plan commit-log record               # record HEAD commit, update PR description
   143→desloppify plan commit-log record --note "why"  # with rationale
   144→desloppify plan commit-log record --only "smells::*"  # record specific findings only
   145→desloppify plan commit-log history              # show commit records
   146→desloppify plan commit-log pr                   # preview PR body markdown
   147→desloppify config set commit_tracking_enabled false  # disable guidance
   148→```
   149→
   150→After resolving findings as `fixed`, the tool shows uncommitted work, committed history, and a suggested commit message. After committing externally, run `record` to move findings from uncommitted to committed and auto-update the linked PR description.
   151→
   152→### Key concepts
   153→
   154→- **Tiers**: T1 auto-fix → T2 quick manual → T3 judgment call → T4 major refactor.
   155→- **Auto-clusters**: related findings are auto-grouped in `next`. Drill in with `next --cluster <name>`.
   156→- **Zones**: production/script (scored), test/config/generated/vendor (not scored). Fix with `zone set`.
   157→- **Wontfix cost**: widens the lenient↔strict gap. Challenge past decisions when the gap grows.
   158→- Score can temporarily drop after fixes (cascade effects are normal).
   159→
   160→## 4. Escalate Tool Issues Upstream
   161→
   162→When desloppify itself appears wrong or inconsistent:
   163→
   164→1. Capture a minimal repro (`command`, `path`, `expected`, `actual`).
   165→2. Open a GitHub issue in `peteromallet/desloppify`.
   166→3. If you can fix it safely, open a PR linked to that issue.
   167→4. If unsure whether it is tool bug vs user workflow, issue first, PR second.
   168→
   169→## Prerequisite
   170→
   171→`command -v desloppify >/dev/null 2>&1 && echo "desloppify: installed" || echo "NOT INSTALLED — run: pip install --upgrade git+https://github.com/peteromallet/desloppify.git"`
   172→
   173→<!-- desloppify-end -->
   174→
```

    11→class _DeprecatedAction(argparse.Action):

    13→
    14→    def __call__(self, parser, namespace, values, option_string=None):
    15→        print(

    18→        )
    19→        setattr(namespace, self.dest, values)

    22→class _DeprecatedBoolAction(argparse.Action):

    25→    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
    26→        kwargs.setdefault("nargs", 0)

1→## Comments and docs
     2→
     3→- **Keep** existing docstrings/JSDoc — only remove if clearly stale or wrong.
     4→- **Don't add** new docs to code you didn't write — unless matching an existing codebase pattern.
     5→
     6→## Claude Code Overlay
     7→
     8→Use Claude subagents for subjective scoring work. **Do not use `--runner codex`** — use Claude subagents exclusively.
     9→
    10→### Subjective review
    11→
    12→Run `desloppify review --prepare` first to generate review data, then use Claude subagents:
    13→
    14→1. **Prepare**: `desloppify review --prepare` — writes `query.json` and `.desloppify/review_packet_blind.json`.
    15→2. **Launch subagents**: Split the review across N parallel Claude subagents (one message, multiple Task calls). Each agent reviews a subset of dimensions.
    16→3. **Merge & import**: Merge agent outputs, then `desloppify review --import merged.json --manual-override --attest "Claude subagents ran blind reviews against review_packet_blind.json" --scan-after-import`.
    17→
    18→#### How to split dimensions across subagents
    19→
    20→- Read `dimension_prompts` from `query.json` — it lists all dimensions with their definitions and seed files.
    21→- Read `.desloppify/review_packet_blind.json` for the blind packet (no score targets, no anchoring data).
    22→- Group dimensions into 3-4 batches by theme (e.g., architecture, code quality, testing, conventions).
    23→- Launch one Task agent per batch. Each agent gets:
    24→  - The codebase path and list of dimensions to score
    25→  - The blind packet path to read
    26→  - The output JSON schema (assessments + findings)
    27→  - Instruction to score from code evidence only, not from targets
    28→- Each agent writes output to a separate file. Merge assessments (average overlapping dimension scores) and concatenate findings.
    29→- Import first, fix after — import creates tracked state entries for correlation.
    30→
    31→### Subagent rules
    32→
    33→1. Use the Task tool with `subagent_type: "general-purpose"` for review agents.
    34→2. Each agent must be context-isolated — do not pass conversation history or score targets.
    35→3. Agents must consume `.desloppify/review_packet_blind.json` (not full `query.json`) to avoid score anchoring.
    36→4. Score from evidence only; do not anchor to target thresholds. When mixed, score lower.
    37→5. Return machine-readable JSON matching the format in the base skill doc.
    38→6. `findings` MUST match the schema in the blind packet's `system_prompt`. Use `"findings": []` when no defects found.
    39→7. Import is fail-closed: invalid findings abort unless `--allow-partial` is passed.
    40→
    41→<!-- desloppify-overlay: claude -->
    42→<!-- desloppify-end -->
    43→

   101→class TestResolveFixerResults:
   102→    """_resolve_fixer_results marks matching findings as fixed."""
   103→
   104→    def _make_state_with_findings(self, *findings):
   105→        state = {"findings": {}}
   106→        for fid, status in findings:
   107→            state["findings"][fid] = {
   108→                "id": fid,
   109→                "status": status,
   110→                "detector": "unused",
   111→                "file": "a.ts",
   112→                "tier": 2,
   113→                "confidence": "high",
   114→                "summary": "test",
   115→                "note": None,
   116→            }
   117→        return state
   118→
   119→    def test_resolves_matching_open_findings(self, monkeypatch):
   120→        monkeypatch.setattr(fix_apply_mod, "rel", lambda p: p)
   121→
   122→        state = self._make_state_with_findings(
   123→            ("unused::a.ts::foo", "open"),
   124→            ("unused::a.ts::bar", "open"),
   125→        )
   126→        results = [{"file": "a.ts", "removed": ["foo"]}]
   127→        resolved = _resolve_fixer_results(state, results, "unused", "unused-imports")

   129→        assert state["findings"]["unused::a.ts::foo"]["status"] == "fixed"
   130→        assert state["findings"]["unused::a.ts::bar"]["status"] == "open"

## Scope Analysis: "fix" Command Rename to "autofix"

I've conducted a thorough search of the codebase to understand the scope of renaming the "fix" command to "autofix". Here are my findings:

### 1. **CLI Command Registration** (4 locations)

**Core entry points:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/cli_support/parser.py:42` - Usage example in USAGE_EXAMPLES: `fix        Auto-fix mechanical issues`

- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/cli_support/parser_groups_admin.py:448` - Parser definition: `sub.add_parser("fix", ...)`
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/registry.py:40` - Handler registration: `"fix": cmd_fix,`

**Help text locations:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/cli_support/parser_groups_admin.py:449` - Help text: `help="Auto-fix mechanical issues"`
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/cli_support/parser_groups_admin.py:452` - Argument help: `help="What to fix"`

### 2. **File/Module Structure** (4 files in command package)

All located under `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/fix/`:
- `cmd.py` - Main command handler `cmd_fix()`
- `apply_flow.py` - Application logic (`_apply_and_report`, `_detect`, etc.)
- `review_flow.py` - Review preparation flow `_cmd_fix_review()`
- `options.py` - Fixer loading helpers

### 3. **User-Facing Command References** (30+ locations)

**Documentation (SKILL.md):**

**Output/Narrative strings** (in code that generates user output):

- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_reporting_text.py:24` - Scan output text: `` `desloppify fix <fixer> --dry-run` ``

### 4. **Internal Function Names vs Command Name**

**Important distinction - these should NOT be renamed:**
- `cmd_fix()` - Command handler (internal function)
- `_cmd_fix_review()` - Review subcommand handler (internal function)
- `_add_fix_parser()` - Parser builder (internal function)
- Module name: `fix/` - Package directory (can stay as-is; internal)
- `is_auto_fix_command()` - Internal detection helper (uses "auto_fix" not "fix")
- Type string `"auto_fix"` in action types - This is an ACTION TYPE, separate from command name

**These refer to internal action type, NOT the command:**
- `action_type: "auto_fix"` (in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/core/registry.py`)
- `"auto_fix": "fix"` mapping (action type → help text)

### 5. **Summary: Count of Changes Required**

| Category | Count |
|----------|-------|
| CLI parser/registration | 4 |
| Help text strings | 2 |
| User-facing documentation | 1 (SKILL.md) |
| Output/narrative strings | ~7 |
| Test assertions with command strings | ~17 |
| **Total user-visible changes** | **~31** |

### 6. **Key Considerations for the Rename**

1. **Backward compatibility**: If you need to support the old command name, you'd need to add a deprecated alias in the parser.

2. **Internal function names**: The functions `cmd_fix`, `_cmd_fix_review`, `_add_fix_parser` can stay as-is since they're internal implementation details. Only the command name "fix" (as a CLI argument) needs to change to "autofix".

3. **Action type vs command**: The `action_type: "auto_fix"` string in the registry is separate and describes the ACTION type (fixable by automation), not the command name. This could stay as-is or be renamed separately.

4. **Commit messages**: The text in commit messages (`"auto-fixed by desloppify fix {fixer_name}"`) would need updating to maintain consistency.

5. **Tests**: All test cases that check for the command string would need updating (17+ locations).

6. **Module directory**: The `/fix/` package directory could stay as-is for internal organization, or be renamed to `/autofix/` for consistency.

1→"""CLI entry point: parse args, load shared context, dispatch command handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→import sys
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify.app.cli_support.parser import create_parser as _create_parser
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.registry import get_command_handlers
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import LangResolutionError, resolve_lang
    12→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import CommandError
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import CommandRuntime
    14→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
    15→from desloppify.core.registry import detector_names, on_detector_registered
    16→from desloppify.core.config import load_config
    17→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import set_exclusions
    18→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    19→from desloppify.core.paths_api import get_default_path, get_project_root
    20→from desloppify.core.runtime_state import runtime_scope
    21→from desloppify.languages import available_langs
    22→from desloppify.state import load_state
    23→
    24→_DETECTOR_NAMES: list[str] | None = None
    25→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    26→# Backward-compatible test patch hook; runtime path resolution uses get_project_root().
    27→PROJECT_ROOT = get_project_root()
    28→_PROJECT_ROOT_SENTINEL = PROJECT_ROOT
    29→
    30→
    31→def _get_detector_names() -> list[str]:
    32→    """Return cached detector names, computing on first access."""
    33→    global _DETECTOR_NAMES
    34→    if _DETECTOR_NAMES is None:
    35→        _DETECTOR_NAMES = detector_names()
    36→    return _DETECTOR_NAMES
    37→
    38→
    39→def _invalidate_detector_names_cache() -> None:
    40→    """Invalidate detector-name cache when runtime registrations change."""
    41→    global _DETECTOR_NAMES
    42→    _DETECTOR_NAMES = None
    43→
    44→
    45→on_detector_registered(_invalidate_detector_names_cache)
    46→
    47→def create_parser():
    48→    """Return the top-level argparse parser."""
    49→    return _create_parser(langs=available_langs(), detector_names=_get_detector_names())
    50→
    51→
    52→def _apply_persisted_exclusions(args, config: dict):
    53→    """Merge CLI --exclude with persisted config.exclude and apply globally."""
    54→    cli_exclusions = getattr(args, "exclude", None) or []
    55→    persisted = config.get("exclude", [])
    56→    combined = list(cli_exclusions) + [e for e in persisted if e not in cli_exclusions]
    57→    if not combined:
    58→        return
    59→    set_exclusions(combined)
    60→    if cli_exclusions:
    61→        print(
    62→            colorize(f"  Excluding: {', '.join(combined)}", "dim"),
    63→            file=sys.stderr,
    64→        )
    65→        return
    66→    print(
    67→        colorize(
    68→            f"  Excluding (from config): {', '.join(combined)}", "dim"
    69→        ),
    70→        file=sys.stderr,
    71→    )
    72→
    73→
    74→def _resolve_default_path(args) -> None:
    75→    """Fill args.path from detected language or default source path.
    76→
    77→    For the review command, the last scan path (stored in state) is used as the
    78→    default so that ``desloppify review --prepare`` works on the same scope as
    79→    the preceding scan even when the project files are not under ``src/``.
    80→    """
    81→    if getattr(args, "path", None) is not None:
    82→        return
    83→    runtime_root = (
    84→        PROJECT_ROOT.resolve()
    85→        if PROJECT_ROOT is not _PROJECT_ROOT_SENTINEL and isinstance(PROJECT_ROOT, Path)
    86→        else get_project_root()
    87→    )
    88→    if getattr(args, "command", None) == "review":
    89→        try:
    90→            state_file = state_path(args)
    91→            if state_file:
    92→                saved = load_state(state_file)
    93→                saved_path = saved.get("scan_path")
    94→                if saved_path:
    95→                    args.path = str((runtime_root / saved_path).resolve())
    96→                    return
    97→        except (OSError, KeyError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError) as exc:
    98→            logger.debug("Failed to resolve default path from saved state: %s", exc)
    99→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   100→    if lang:
   101→        args.path = str(runtime_root / lang.default_src)
   102→    else:
   103→        args.path = str(get_default_path())
   104→
   105→
   106→def _load_shared_runtime(args) -> None:
   107→    """Load config/state and attach shared objects to parsed args."""
   108→    config = load_config()
   109→
   110→    state_file = state_path(args)
   111→    state = load_state(state_file)
   112→    _apply_persisted_exclusions(args, config)
   113→
   114→    args.runtime = CommandRuntime(config=config, state=state, state_path=state_file)
   115→
   116→
   117→def _resolve_handler(command: str):
   118→    return get_command_handlers()[command]
   119→
   120→
   121→def _handle_help_command(args, parser) -> None:
   122→    """Handle explicit help command when present in parser config."""
   123→    topic = list(getattr(args, "topic", []) or [])
   124→    try:
   125→        parser.parse_args([*topic, "--help"])
   126→    except SystemExit:
   127→        return
   128→
   129→
   130→def main() -> None:
   131→    # Ensure Unicode output works on Windows terminals (cp1252 etc.)
   132→    for stream in (sys.stdout, sys.stderr):
   133→        if hasattr(stream, "reconfigure"):
   134→            try:
   135→                stream.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
   136→            except (AttributeError, OSError):
   137→                logger.debug(
   138→                    "Skipping stream reconfigure for %s (not supported)",
   139→                    getattr(stream, "name", "<stream>"),
   140→                )
   141→
   142→    parser = create_parser()
   143→    args = parser.parse_args()
   144→    if args.command == "help":
   145→        _handle_help_command(args, parser)
   146→        return
   147→
   148→    try:
   149→        with runtime_scope():
   150→            _resolve_default_path(args)
   151→            _load_shared_runtime(args)
   152→
   153→            handler = _resolve_handler(args.command)
   154→            handler(args)
   155→    except CommandError as exc:
   156→        print(colorize(f"  {exc.message}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   157→        sys.exit(exc.exit_code)
   158→    except LangResolutionError as exc:
   159→        print(colorize(f"  {exc.message}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   160→        sys.exit(1)
   161→    except KeyboardInterrupt:
   162→        print("\nInterrupted.")
   163→        sys.exit(1)
   164→
   165→
   166→if __name__ == "__main__":
   167→    main()
   168→

    28→
    29→USAGE_EXAMPLES = """
    30→core workflow:
    31→  scan       Run detectors, update state, show diff
    32→  status     Score dashboard with dimension health
    33→  next       Show next highest-priority item to work on
    34→  plan       Living plan: prioritize, cluster, skip, done, annotate
    35→
    36→investigation:
    37→  show       Dig into findings by file/dir/detector/ID
    38→  tree       Annotated codebase tree (zoom with --focus)
    39→  detect     Run a single detector directly (bypass state)
    40→
    41→maintenance:
    42→  autofix    Auto-fix mechanical issues
    43→  ignore     Suppress findings matching a pattern
    44→  exclude    Exclude path pattern from scanning
    45→  move       Move file/dir and update import references
    46→  review     Holistic subjective review (LLM-based)
    47→
    48→setup & admin:
    49→  zone       Show/set zone classifications
    50→  config     Project configuration
    51→  viz        Interactive HTML treemap
    52→  langs      List language plugins
    53→  dev        Developer utilities
    54→  update-skill  Install/update agent skill document
    55→
    56→examples:
    57→  desloppify scan
    58→  desloppify scan --skip-slow --profile ci
    59→  desloppify plan                        # full prioritized markdown
    60→  desloppify plan queue                  # compact table of all items
    61→  desloppify next --count 10             # top 10 queue items
    62→  desloppify show src/components/Modal.tsx
    63→  desloppify plan done "unused::src/foo.tsx::React" \\
    64→    --note "removed import" --attest "I have actually ..."
    65→  desloppify review --run-batches --parallel --scan-after-import
    66→"""
    67→
    68→
    69→class _NoAbbrevArgumentParser(argparse.ArgumentParser):
    70→    """Argparse parser variant that disables long-option abbreviation."""
    71→
    72→    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
    73→        kwargs.setdefault("allow_abbrev", False)
    74→        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
    75→
    76→
    77→def _cli_version_string() -> str:
    78→    """Return the best available CLI version label."""
    79→    try:
    80→        return f"desloppify {get_version('desloppify')}"
    81→    except PackageNotFoundError:
    82→        return "desloppify (version unknown)"
    83→
    84→
    85→def create_parser(*, langs: list[str], detector_names: list[str]) -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    86→    """Build top-level CLI parser with all subcommands."""
    87→    lang_help = ", ".join(langs) if langs else "registered languages"
    88→
    89→    parser = _NoAbbrevArgumentParser(
    90→        prog="desloppify",
    91→        description="Desloppify — codebase health tracker",
    92→        epilog=USAGE_EXAMPLES,
    93→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
    94→    )
    95→    parser.add_argument(
    96→        "--lang",
    97→        type=str,
    98→        default=None,
    99→        help=f"Language to scan ({lang_help}). Auto-detected if omitted.",
   100→    )
   101→    parser.add_argument(
   102→        "--exclude",
   103→        action="append",
   104→        default=None,
   105→        metavar="PATTERN",
   106→        help="Path pattern to exclude (component/prefix match; repeatable)",
   107→    )
   108→    parser.add_argument(
   109→        "--version",
   110→        "-V",
   111→        action="version",
   112→        version=_cli_version_string(),
   113→    )
   114→    sub = parser.add_subparsers(
   115→        dest="command",
   116→        required=True,
   117→        parser_class=_NoAbbrevArgumentParser,
   118→    )
   119→    _add_scan_parser(sub)
   120→    _add_status_parser(sub)
   121→    _add_tree_parser(sub)
   122→    _add_show_parser(sub)
   123→    _add_next_parser(sub)
   124→    _add_ignore_parser(sub)
   125→    _add_exclude_parser(sub)
   126→    _add_fix_parser(sub, langs)
   127→    _add_plan_parser(sub)
   128→    _add_viz_parser(sub)
   129→    _add_detect_parser(sub, detector_names)
   130→    _add_move_parser(sub)
   131→    _add_review_parser(sub)
   132→    _add_zone_parser(sub)
   133→    _add_config_parser(sub)
   134→    _add_dev_parser(sub)
   135→    _add_langs_parser(sub)
   136→    _add_update_skill_parser(sub)
   137→    return parser
   138→
   139→
   140→__all__ = ["USAGE_EXAMPLES", "create_parser"]
   141→

    47→def _add_scan_parser(sub) -> None:
    48→    p_scan = sub.add_parser(

    50→        help="Run all detectors, update state, show diff",
    51→        epilog="""\
    52→examples:
    53→  desloppify scan
    54→  desloppify scan --skip-slow
    55→  desloppify scan --profile ci
    56→  desloppify scan --force-resolve""",
    57→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
    58→    )
    59→    p_scan.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None, help="Project root directory (default: auto-detected)")
    60→    p_scan.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None, help="Path to state file")
    61→    p_scan.add_argument(
    62→        "--reset-subjective",
    63→        action="store_true",
    64→        help="Reset subjective measures to 0 before running scan",
    65→    )
    66→    p_scan.add_argument(
    67→        "--skip-slow", action="store_true", help="Skip slow detectors (dupes)"
    68→    )
    69→    p_scan.add_argument(
    70→        "--profile",
    71→        choices=["objective", "full", "ci"],
    72→        default=None,
    73→        help="Scan profile: objective, full, or ci",
    74→    )
    75→    p_scan.add_argument(
    76→        "--force-resolve",
    77→        action="store_true",
    78→        help="Bypass suspect-detector protection (use when a detector legitimately went to 0)",
    79→    )
    80→    p_scan.add_argument(
    81→        "--no-badge",
    82→        action="store_true",
    83→        help="Skip scorecard image generation (also: DESLOPPIFY_NO_BADGE=true)",
    84→    )

    99→    p_scan.add_argument(

   121→    p_tree.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None, help="Project root directory (default: auto-detected)")
   122→    p_tree.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None, help="Path to state file")

    56→
    57→def _print_scan_header(lang_label: str) -> None:
    58→    """Print the scan header line."""
    59→    print(colorize(f"\nDesloppify Scan{lang_label}\n", "bold"))
    60→
    61→
    62→def _print_scan_complete_banner() -> None:
    63→    """Print scan completion hint banner."""
    64→    lines = [
    65→        colorize("  Scan complete", "bold"),
    66→        colorize("  " + "─" * 50, "dim"),
    67→    ]
    68→    print("\n".join(lines))
    69→
    70→
    71→def _show_scan_visibility(noise, effective_include_slow: bool) -> None:
    72→    """Print fast-scan and noise budget visibility hints."""
    73→    if not effective_include_slow:
    74→        print(colorize("  * Fast scan — slow phases (duplicates) skipped", "yellow"))
    75→    if noise.budget_warning:
    76→        print(colorize(f"  * {noise.budget_warning}", "yellow"))
    77→    if noise.hidden_total:
    78→        print(
    79→            colorize(
    80→                f"  * {noise.hidden_total} findings hidden (showing {noise.noise_budget}/detector). "
    81→                "Use `desloppify show <detector>` to see all.",
    82→                "dim",
    83→            )
    84→        )
    85→
    86→
    87→def _show_coverage_preflight(runtime) -> None:
    88→    """Print preflight warnings when scan coverage confidence is reduced."""
    89→    warnings = getattr(runtime, "coverage_warnings", []) or []
    90→    if not isinstance(warnings, list) or not warnings:
    91→        return
    92→
    93→    for entry in warnings:
    94→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    95→            continue
    96→        summary = str(entry.get("summary", "")).strip()
    97→        impact = str(entry.get("impact", "")).strip()
    98→        remediation = str(entry.get("remediation", "")).strip()
    99→        detector = str(entry.get("detector", "")).strip() or "detector"
   100→
   101→        headline = summary or f"Coverage reduced for `{detector}`."
   102→        print(colorize(f"  * Coverage preflight: {headline}", "yellow"))
   103→        if impact:
   104→            print(colorize(f"    Repercussion: {impact}", "dim"))
   105→        if remediation:
   106→            print(colorize(f"    Fix: {remediation}", "dim"))
   107→
   108→
   109→def _print_plan_workflow_nudge(state: dict) -> None:
   110→    from desloppify.app.commands.scan.plan_nudge import print_plan_workflow_nudge
   111→
   112→    print_plan_workflow_nudge(state)
   113→
   114→
   115→def cmd_scan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   116→    """Run all detectors, update persistent state, show diff."""
   117→    scan_preflight_mod.scan_queue_preflight(args)
   118→    try:
   119→        runtime = prepare_scan_runtime(args)
   120→    except LangRuntimeOptionsError as exc:
   121→        lang_cfg = resolve_lang(args)
   122→        lang_name = lang_cfg.name if lang_cfg else "selected"
   123→        print_lang_runtime_options_error(exc, lang_name=lang_name)
   124→        raise SystemExit(2) from exc
   125→    except ScanStateContractError as exc:
   126→        print(colorize(f"  {exc}", "red"))
   127→        raise SystemExit(2) from exc
   128→    orchestrator = ScanOrchestrator(
   129→        runtime,
   130→        run_scan_generation_fn=run_scan_generation,
   131→        merge_scan_results_fn=merge_scan_results,
   132→        resolve_noise_snapshot_fn=resolve_noise_snapshot,
   133→        persist_reminder_history_fn=persist_reminder_history,
   134→    )
   135→    _print_scan_header(runtime.lang_label)
   136→    if runtime.reset_subjective_count > 0:
   137→        print(
   138→            colorize(
   139→                "  * Subjective reset "
   140→                f"{runtime.reset_subjective_count} subjective dimensions to 0",
   141→                "yellow",
   142→            )
   143→        )
   144→    if runtime.expired_manual_override_count > 0:
   145→        print(
   146→            colorize(
   147→                "  * Expired provisional manual-override assessments: "
   148→                f"{runtime.expired_manual_override_count} dimension(s) reset to 0. "
   149→                "Use trusted `review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` to replace them.",
   150→                "yellow",
   151→            )
   152→        )
   153→    _show_coverage_preflight(runtime)
   154→
   155→    findings, potentials, codebase_metrics = orchestrator.generate()
   156→    merge = orchestrator.merge(findings, potentials, codebase_metrics)
   157→    _print_scan_complete_banner()
   158→
   159→    noise = orchestrator.noise_snapshot()
   160→
   161→    target_value = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   162→
   163→    show_diff_summary(merge.diff)
   164→    show_score_delta(
   165→        runtime.state,
   166→        merge.prev_overall,
   167→        merge.prev_objective,
   168→        merge.prev_strict,
   169→        merge.prev_verified,
   170→        target_strict=target_value,
   171→    )
   172→    # Nudge: if plan_start_scores was just seeded, tell the agent about the lifecycle.
   173→    _print_plan_workflow_nudge(runtime.state)
   174→    _show_scan_visibility(noise, runtime.effective_include_slow)
   175→    show_scorecard_subjective_measures(runtime.state)
   176→    show_score_model_breakdown(runtime.state)
   177→
   178→    new_dim_scores = runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   179→    if new_dim_scores and merge.prev_dim_scores:
   180→        show_dimension_deltas(merge.prev_dim_scores, new_dim_scores)
   181→
   182→    warnings, narrative = show_post_scan_analysis(
   183→        merge.diff,
   184→        runtime.state,
   185→        runtime.lang,
   186→        target_strict_score=target_value,
   187→    )
   188→    orchestrator.persist_reminders(narrative)
   189→
   190→    write_query(
   191→        build_scan_query_payload(
   192→            runtime.state,
   193→            runtime.config,
   194→            runtime.profile,
   195→            merge.diff,
   196→            warnings,
   197→            narrative,
   198→            merge,
   199→            noise,
   200→        ),
   201→        query_file=query_file_path(),
   202→    )
   203→
   204→    badge_emit = emit_scorecard_badge(args, runtime.config, runtime.state)
   205→    if isinstance(badge_emit, tuple):
   206→        badge_path, _badge_result = badge_emit
   207→    else:  # Backward-compatible shape for monkeypatched tests.
   208→        badge_path = badge_emit
   209→    _print_llm_summary(runtime.state, badge_path, narrative, merge.diff)
   210→    auto_update_skill()
   211→
   212→
   213→__all__ = [
   214→    "cmd_scan",
   215→]
   216→

     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import json
     7→import logging
     8→
     9→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang

    35→    show_structural_areas,
    36→    show_subjective_followup,
    37→    show_tier_progress_table,
    38→    write_status_query,
    39→)
    40→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    41→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    42→    scorecard_dimensions_payload,
    43→)
    44→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    45→from desloppify.core.skill_docs import check_skill_version
    46→from desloppify.core.tooling import check_config_staleness
    47→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative import NarrativeContext, compute_narrative
    48→from desloppify.scoring import compute_health_breakdown
    49→
    50→
    51→def cmd_status(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    52→    """Show score dashboard."""
    53→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    54→    state = runtime.state
    55→    config = runtime.config
    56→
    57→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
    58→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    59→    scorecard_dims = scorecard_dimensions_payload(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    60→    subjective_measures = [row for row in scorecard_dims if row.get("subjective")]
    61→    suppression = state_mod.suppression_metrics(state)
    62→
    63→    if getattr(args, "json", False):
    64→        print(
    65→            json.dumps(
    66→                _status_json_payload(
    67→                    state,
    68→                    stats,
    69→                    dim_scores,
    70→                    scorecard_dims,
    71→                    subjective_measures,
    72→                    suppression,
    73→                ),
    74→                indent=2,
    75→            )
    76→        )
    77→        return
    78→
    79→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    80→        return
    81→
    82→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    83→    if skill_warning:
    84→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    85→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    86→    if config_warning:
    87→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    88→
    89→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    90→    by_tier = stats.get("by_tier", {})
    91→    target_strict_score = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    92→
    93→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    94→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
    95→
    96→    # Load living plan for plan-aware rendering and narrative
    97→    _plan = load_plan()
    98→    _plan_active = _plan if (
    99→        _plan.get("queue_order") or _plan.get("clusters")
   100→    ) else None
   101→
   102→    print_triage_guardrail_info(plan=_plan, state=state)
   103→
   104→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   105→        state,
   106→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="status", plan=_plan_active),
   107→    )
   108→    ignores = config.get("ignore", [])
   109→
   110→    _breakdown = _print_score_section(state, scores, _plan, target_strict_score)
   111→    print_scan_metrics(state)
   112→    print_open_scope_breakdown(state)
   113→    print_scan_completeness(state)
   114→
   115→    # Compute objective backlog once for consistent subjective actionability gating
   116→    from desloppify.engine.plan import compute_subjective_visibility
   117→    _policy = compute_subjective_visibility(state, target_strict=target_strict_score)
   118→    _objective_backlog = _policy.objective_count
   119→
   120→    if dim_scores:
   121→        show_dimension_table(state, dim_scores, objective_backlog=_objective_backlog)
   122→        reporting_dimensions_mod.show_score_model_breakdown(
   123→            state,
   124→            dim_scores=dim_scores,
   125→        )
   126→    else:
   127→        show_tier_progress_table(by_tier)
   128→
   129→    if dim_scores:
   130→        show_focus_suggestion(dim_scores, state, plan=_plan_active)
   131→        show_subjective_followup(
   132→            state,
   133→            dim_scores,
   134→            target_strict_score=target_strict_score,
   135→            objective_backlog=_objective_backlog,
   136→        )
   137→
   138→    show_review_summary(state)
   139→    show_structural_areas(state)
   140→
   141→    # Commit tracking reminder
   142→    try:
   143→        from desloppify.app.commands.next_parts.render import render_uncommitted_reminder
   144→        render_uncommitted_reminder(_plan_active)
   145→    except (ImportError, OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
   146→        pass
   147→
   148→    show_agent_plan(narrative, plan=_plan_active)
   149→
   150→    if narrative.get("headline"):
   151→        print(colorize(f"  -> {narrative['headline']}", "cyan"))
   152→        print()
   153→
   154→    if ignores:
   155→        show_ignore_summary(ignores, suppression)
   156→
   157→    review_age = config.get("review_max_age_days", 30)
   158→    if review_age != 30:
   159→        label = "never" if review_age == 0 else f"{review_age} days"
   160→        print(colorize(f"  Review staleness: {label}", "dim"))
   161→    print()
   162→
   163→    write_status_query(
   164→        state=state,
   165→        stats=stats,
   166→        by_tier=by_tier,
   167→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
   168→        scorecard_dims=scorecard_dims,
   169→        subjective_measures=subjective_measures,
   170→        suppression=suppression,
   171→        narrative=narrative,
   172→        ignores=ignores,
   173→        overall_score=scores.overall,
   174→        objective_score=scores.objective,
   175→        strict_score=scores.strict,
   176→        verified_strict_score=scores.verified,
   177→        plan=_plan_active,
   178→    )
   179→
   180→
   181→def _print_score_section(state, scores, plan, target_strict_score):
   182→    """Print score header: frozen plan-start or live score with queue breakdown."""
   183→    plan_start_strict = get_plan_start_strict(plan)
   184→    breakdown = None
   185→    queue_remaining = 0
   186→    if plan_start_strict is not None:
   187→        try:
   188→            breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   189→            queue_remaining = breakdown.queue_total
   190→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   191→            logging.debug("Plan-aware queue count failed: %s", exc)
   192→            queue_remaining = 0
   193→    if plan_start_strict is not None and queue_remaining > 0:
   194→        print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   195→            plan, queue_remaining, breakdown=breakdown,
   196→        )
   197→    else:
   198→        for line, style in score_summary_lines(
   199→            overall_score=scores.overall,
   200→            objective_score=scores.objective,
   201→            strict_score=scores.strict,
   202→            verified_strict_score=scores.verified,
   203→            target_strict=target_strict_score,
   204→        ):
   205→            print(colorize(line, style))
   206→        # Show queue breakdown even without frozen score
   207→        if breakdown is None:
   208→            try:
   209→                breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   210→            except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   211→                breakdown = None
   212→        if breakdown is not None and breakdown.queue_total > 0:
   213→            block = format_queue_block(breakdown)
   214→            for text, style in block:
   215→                print(colorize(text, style))
   216→    return breakdown
   217→
   218→
   219→def _status_json_payload(
   220→    state: dict,
   221→    stats: dict,
   222→    dim_scores: dict,
   223→    scorecard_dims: list[dict],
   224→    subjective_measures: list[dict],
   225→    suppression: dict,
   226→) -> dict:
   227→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   228→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   229→    open_scope = (
   230→        state_mod.open_scope_breakdown(findings, state.get("scan_path"))
   231→        if isinstance(findings, dict)
   232→        else None
   233→    )
   234→    return {
   235→        "overall_score": scores.overall,
   236→        "objective_score": scores.objective,
   237→        "strict_score": scores.strict,
   238→        "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,
   239→        "dimension_scores": dim_scores,
   240→        "score_breakdown": compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores) if dim_scores else None,
   241→        "scorecard_dimensions": scorecard_dims,
   242→        "subjective_measures": subjective_measures,
   243→        "potentials": state.get("potentials"),
   244→        "codebase_metrics": state.get("codebase_metrics"),
   245→        "stats": stats,
   246→        "open_scope": open_scope,
   247→        "suppression": suppression,
   248→        "scan_count": state.get("scan_count", 0),
   249→        "last_scan": state.get("last_scan"),
   250→    }
   251→
   252→__all__ = [
   253→    "cmd_status",
   254→    "show_dimension_table",
   255→    "show_focus_suggestion",
   256→    "show_ignore_summary",
   257→    "show_structural_areas",
   258→    "show_subjective_followup",
   259→]
   260→

    38→def _scorecard_subjective(
    39→    state: dict,
    40→    dim_scores: dict,
    41→) -> list[dict]:
    42→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    43→    return next_render_mod.scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
    44→
    45→
    46→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    47→    state: dict,
    48→    dim_scores: dict,
    49→    *,
    50→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    51→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    52→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    53→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    54→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    55→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    56→            continue
    57→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    58→        if strict_val < threshold:
    59→            low.append(
    60→                (
    61→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    62→                    strict_val,
    63→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    64→                )
    65→            )
    66→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    67→    return low
    68→
    69→
    70→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    71→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
    72→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    73→    state = runtime.state
    74→    config = runtime.config
    75→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    76→        return
    77→
    78→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    79→    if skill_warning:
    80→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    81→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    82→    if config_warning:
    83→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    84→
    85→    print_triage_guardrail_info(state=state)
    86→    _get_items(args, state, config)
    87→
    88→
    89→def _resolve_cluster_focus(
    90→    plan_data: dict | None,
    91→    *,
    92→    cluster_arg: str | None,
    93→    scope: str | None,
    94→) -> str | None:
    95→    effective_cluster = cluster_arg
    96→    if plan_data and not cluster_arg and not scope:
    97→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
    98→        if active_cluster:
    99→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   100→    return effective_cluster
   101→
   102→
   103→def _build_next_payload(
   104→    *,
   105→    queue: dict,
   106→    items: list[dict],
   107→    state: dict,
   108→    narrative: dict,
   109→    plan_data: dict | None,
   110→) -> dict:
   111→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(
   112→        queue, items, command="next", narrative=narrative, plan=plan_data
   113→    )
   114→    payload["overall_score"] = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   115→    payload["objective_score"] = state_mod.get_objective_score(state)
   116→    payload["strict_score"] = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   117→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   118→        state,
   119→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   120→    )
   121→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   122→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   123→    ]
   124→    return payload
   125→
   126→
   127→def _emit_requested_output(
   128→    args,
   129→    payload: dict,
   130→    items: list[dict],
   131→) -> bool:
   132→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   133→    if output_file:
   134→        if next_output_mod.write_output_file(
   135→            output_file,
   136→            payload,
   137→            len(items),
   138→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   139→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   140→        ):
   141→            return True
   142→        raise SystemExit(1)
   143→
   144→    output_format = getattr(args, "format", "terminal")
   145→    if next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(output_format, payload, items):
   146→        return True
   147→    return False
   148→
   149→
   150→def _plan_queue_context(
   151→    *,
   152→    state: dict,
   153→    plan_data: dict | None,
   154→) -> tuple[float | None, QueueBreakdown | None]:
   155→    plan_start_strict = get_plan_start_strict(plan_data)
   156→    try:
   157→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan_data)
   158→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   159→        breakdown = None
   160→    return plan_start_strict, breakdown
   161→
   162→
   163→def _merge_potentials_safe(raw_potentials: dict | None) -> dict | None:
   164→    try:
   165→        return merge_potentials(raw_potentials) or None
   166→    except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError):
   167→        return raw_potentials or None
   168→

   170→def _get_items(args, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
   171→    count = getattr(args, "count", 1) or 1
   172→    scope = getattr(args, "scope", None)
   173→    status = getattr(args, "status", "open")
   174→    group = getattr(args, "group", "item")
   175→    explain = bool(getattr(args, "explain", False))
   176→    cluster_arg = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
   177→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
   178→
   179→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
   180→
   181→    # Load the living plan
   182→    plan = load_plan()
   183→    plan_data: dict | None = None
   184→    if (
   185→        plan.get("queue_order")
   186→        or plan.get("overrides")
   187→        or plan.get("clusters")
   188→    ):
   189→        plan_data = plan
   190→
   191→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster if set and no explicit scope/cluster
   192→    effective_cluster = _resolve_cluster_focus(
   193→        plan_data,
   194→        cluster_arg=cluster_arg,
   195→        scope=scope,
   196→    )
   197→
   198→    queue = build_work_queue(
   199→        state,
   200→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   201→            count=count,
   202→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   203→            scope=scope,
   204→            status=status,
   205→            include_subjective=True,
   206→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   207→            explain=explain,
   208→            plan=plan_data,
   209→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   210→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   211→        ),
   212→    )
   213→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   214→
   215→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   216→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   217→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   218→        state,
   219→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next", plan=plan_data),
   220→    )
   221→
   222→    payload = _build_next_payload(
   223→        queue=queue,
   224→        items=items,
   225→        state=state,
   226→        narrative=narrative,
   227→        plan_data=plan_data,
   228→    )
   229→    write_query(payload)
   230→
   231→    if _emit_requested_output(args, payload, items):
   232→        return
   233→
   234→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   235→    findings_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   236→        state.get("findings", {}),
   237→        state.get("scan_path"),
   238→    )
   239→
   240→    # Extract frozen plan-start score and queue breakdown for lifecycle display
   241→    plan_start_strict, breakdown = _plan_queue_context(
   242→        state=state,
   243→        plan_data=plan_data,
   244→    )
   245→    queue_total = breakdown.queue_total if breakdown else 0
   246→
   247→    next_render_mod.render_queue_header(queue, explain)
   248→    strict_score = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   249→    if next_render_mod.show_empty_queue(
   250→        queue,
   251→        strict_score,
   252→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   253→        target_strict=target_strict,
   254→    ):
   255→        return
   256→
   257→    raw_potentials = state.get("potentials", {})
   258→    potentials = _merge_potentials_safe(raw_potentials)
   259→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   260→        items, dim_scores, findings_scoped, group=group, explain=explain,
   261→        potentials=potentials, plan=plan_data,
   262→        cluster_filter=effective_cluster,
   263→    )
   264→    next_render_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   265→    next_render_mod.render_uncommitted_reminder(plan_data)
   266→    next_render_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   267→        state,
   268→        dim_scores,
   269→        findings_scoped,

   271→        target_strict_score=target_strict,
   272→        queue_total=queue_total,
   273→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   274→        breakdown=breakdown,
   275→    )
   276→    print()

1→"""show command: dig into findings by file, directory, detector, or pattern."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.guardrails import print_triage_guardrail_info
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score import target_strict_score_from_config
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    13→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    14→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    15→from desloppify.core.skill_docs import check_skill_version
    16→from desloppify.core.tooling import check_config_staleness
    17→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    18→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative import NarrativeContext, compute_narrative
    19→
    20→from .concerns_view import _show_concerns
    21→from .dimension_views import (
    22→    _load_dimension_findings,
    23→    _render_clean_mechanical_dimension,
    24→    _render_no_matches,
    25→    _render_subjective_dimension,
    26→    _render_subjective_views_guide,
    27→)
    28→from .payload import ShowPayloadMeta, build_show_payload
    29→from .render import (
    30→    render_findings,
    31→    show_agent_plan,
    32→    show_subjective_followup,
    33→    write_show_output_file,
    34→)
    35→from .scope import load_matches, resolve_entity, resolve_noise, resolve_show_scope
    36→
    37→
    38→def cmd_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    39→    """Show all findings for a file, directory, detector, or pattern."""
    40→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    41→    state = runtime.state
    42→    config = runtime.config
    43→
    44→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    45→        return
    46→
    47→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    48→    if skill_warning:
    49→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    50→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    51→    if config_warning:
    52→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    53→
    54→    print_triage_guardrail_info(state=state)
    55→
    56→    pattern_raw = getattr(args, "pattern", "")
    57→    show_code = getattr(args, "code", False)
    58→    chronic = getattr(args, "chronic", False)
    59→    ok, pattern, status_filter, scope = resolve_show_scope(args)
    60→    if not ok or pattern is None:
    61→        return
    62→
    63→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    64→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
    65→    narrative = compute_narrative(
    66→        state,
    67→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="show"),
    68→    )
    69→
    70→    entity = resolve_entity(pattern, state)
    71→
    72→    if entity.kind == "special_view" and entity.pattern.strip().lower() == "concerns":
    73→        _show_concerns(state, lang_name)
    74→        return
    75→
    76→    if entity.kind == "dimension" and entity.is_subjective:
    77→        _render_subjective_dimension(state, config, entity, pattern_raw)
    78→        return
    79→
    80→    if entity.kind == "dimension" and not entity.is_subjective:
    81→        matches = _load_dimension_findings(state, entity, status_filter)
    82→        if not matches:
    83→            _render_clean_mechanical_dimension(state, entity)
    84→            return
    85→        pattern = entity.display_name
    86→    else:
    87→        matches = load_matches(
    88→            state, scope=scope, status_filter=status_filter, chronic=chronic
    89→        )
    90→
    91→    if not matches:
    92→        _render_no_matches(entity, pattern, status_filter, narrative, state, config)
    93→        return
    94→
    95→    no_budget = getattr(args, "no_budget", False)
    96→    (
    97→        surfaced_matches,
    98→        hidden_by_detector,
    99→        noise_budget,
   100→        global_noise_budget,
   101→        budget_warning,
   102→    ) = resolve_noise(
   103→        config,
   104→        matches,
   105→        no_budget=no_budget,
   106→    )
   107→    hidden_total = sum(hidden_by_detector.values())
   108→
   109→    payload = build_show_payload(
   110→        surfaced_matches,
   111→        pattern,
   112→        status_filter,
   113→        ShowPayloadMeta(
   114→            total_matches=len(matches),
   115→            hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   116→            noise_budget=noise_budget,
   117→            global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   118→        ),
   119→    )
   120→    write_query({"command": "show", **payload, "narrative": narrative})
   121→
   122→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   123→    if output_file:
   124→        if write_show_output_file(output_file, payload, len(surfaced_matches)):
   125→            return
   126→        raise SystemExit(1)
   127→
   128→    top = getattr(args, "top", 20) or 20
   129→    render_findings(
   130→        surfaced_matches,
   131→        pattern=pattern,
   132→        status_filter=status_filter,
   133→        show_code=show_code,
   134→        top=top,
   135→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   136→        hidden_total=hidden_total,
   137→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   138→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   139→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   140→    )
   141→    try:
   142→        plan = load_plan()
   143→        plan_active = plan if (plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters")) else None
   144→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   145→        plan_active = None
   146→    show_agent_plan(narrative, surfaced_matches, plan=plan_active)
   147→    show_subjective_followup(
   148→        state,
   149→        target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0),
   150→    )
   151→
   152→    _render_subjective_views_guide(entity)
   153→
   154→
   155→__all__ = ["cmd_show"]
   156→

1→"""detect command: run a single detector directly (bypass state tracking)."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import sys
     7→
     8→from desloppify import languages as lang_api
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang, resolve_lang_settings
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime_options import (
    12→    LangRuntimeOptionsError,
    13→    print_lang_runtime_options_error,
    14→    resolve_lang_runtime_options,
    15→)
    16→from desloppify.languages import runtime as lang_runtime
    17→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import CommandError
    18→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    19→
    20→
    21→def _resolve_detector_key(
    22→    detector: str, detect_commands: dict[str, object]
    23→) -> str | None:
    24→    """Resolve detector input to a command key."""
    25→    detector = detector.strip()
    26→    if detector in detect_commands:
    27→        return detector
    28→
    29→    normalized = detector.lower().replace("-", "_")
    30→    if normalized in detect_commands:
    31→        return normalized
    32→
    33→    denormalized = detector.lower().replace("_", "-")
    34→    if denormalized in detect_commands:
    35→        return denormalized
    36→
    37→    return None
    38→
    39→
    40→def cmd_detect(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    41→    """Run a single detector directly (bypass state tracking)."""
    42→    detector_input = args.detector
    43→
    44→    # Resolve language (from --lang flag or auto-detection)
    45→    lang_cfg = resolve_lang(args)
    46→
    47→    if not lang_cfg:
    48→        langs = ", ".join(lang_api.available_langs()) or "registered language plugins"
    49→        raise CommandError(
    50→            f"No language specified. Use --lang <name> (available: {langs})."
    51→        )
    52→
    53→    # Validate detector name
    54→    detector = _resolve_detector_key(detector_input, lang_cfg.detect_commands)
    55→    if detector is None:
    56→        available = ", ".join(sorted(lang_cfg.detect_commands))
    57→        raise CommandError(
    58→            f"Unknown detector for {lang_cfg.name}: {detector_input}\n"
    59→            f"  Available: {available}"
    60→        )
    61→
    62→    # Set default thresholds for detectors that expect them
    63→    if getattr(args, "threshold", None) is None:
    64→        if detector == "large":
    65→            args.threshold = lang_cfg.large_threshold
    66→        elif detector == "dupes":
    67→            args.threshold = 0.8
    68→
    69→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    70→    lang_settings = resolve_lang_settings(runtime.config, lang_cfg)
    71→    try:
    72→        lang_options = resolve_lang_runtime_options(args, lang_cfg)
    73→    except LangRuntimeOptionsError as exc:
    74→        print_lang_runtime_options_error(exc, lang_name=lang_cfg.name)
    75→        raise CommandError(str(exc), exit_code=2) from exc
    76→    lang = lang_runtime.make_lang_run(
    77→        lang_cfg,
    78→        overrides=lang_runtime.LangRunOverrides(
    79→            runtime_settings=lang_settings,
    80→            runtime_options=lang_options,
    81→        ),
    82→    )
    83→    args.lang_runtime_options = dict(lang_options)
    84→    try:
    85→        lang.detect_commands[detector](args)
    86→    finally:
    87→        args.lang_runtime_options = None
    88→    if not getattr(args, "json", False):
    89→        scan_path = getattr(args, "path", ".") or "."
    90→        print(
    91→            colorize(f"\n  Next command: `desloppify scan --path {scan_path}`", "dim"),
    92→            file=sys.stderr,
    93→        )
    94→
