1→"""fix command: auto-fix mechanical issues with fixer registry and pipeline."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands._show_terminal import show_fix_dry_run_samples
     9→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import FixResult
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    11→
    12→from .apply_flow import (
    13→    _apply_and_report,
    14→    _detect,
    15→    _print_fix_summary,
    16→    _report_dry_run,
    17→    _warn_uncommitted_changes,
    18→)
    19→from .options import _load_fixer
    20→from .review_flow import _cmd_fix_review
    21→
    22→
    23→def cmd_fix(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    24→    """Auto-fix mechanical issues."""
    25→    fixer_name = args.fixer
    26→    if fixer_name == "review":
    27→        _cmd_fix_review(args)
    28→        return
    29→
    30→    dry_run = getattr(args, "dry_run", False)
    31→    path = Path(args.path)
    32→
    33→    lang, fixer = _load_fixer(args, fixer_name)
    34→
    35→    if not dry_run:
    36→        _warn_uncommitted_changes()
    37→    entries = _detect(fixer, path)
    38→    if not entries:
    39→        print(colorize(f"No {fixer.label} found.", "green"))
    40→        return
    41→
    42→    raw = fixer.fix(entries, dry_run=dry_run)
    43→    if isinstance(raw, FixResult):
    44→        results = raw.entries
    45→        skip_reasons = raw.skip_reasons
    46→    else:
    47→        results = raw
    48→        skip_reasons = {}
    49→    total_items = sum(len(r["removed"]) for r in results)
    50→    total_lines = sum(r.get("lines_removed", 0) for r in results)
    51→    _print_fix_summary(fixer, results, total_items, total_lines, dry_run)
    52→
    53→    if dry_run and results:
    54→        show_fix_dry_run_samples(entries, results)
    55→
    56→    if not dry_run:
    57→        _apply_and_report(
    58→            args,
    59→            path,
    60→            fixer,
    61→            fixer_name,
    62→            entries,
    63→            results,
    64→            total_items,
    65→            lang,
    66→            skip_reasons,
    67→        )
    68→    else:
    69→        _report_dry_run(args, fixer_name, entries, results, total_items)
    70→    print()
    71→

    23→def cmd_ignore_pattern(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    24→    """Add a pattern to the ignore list."""

    26→    if not validate_attestation(attestation):

    37→    config["needs_rescan"] = True
    38→    _save_config_or_exit(config)
    39→
    40→    removed = state_mod.remove_ignored_findings(state, args.pattern)
    41→    state.setdefault("attestation_log", []).append(
    42→        {
    43→            "timestamp": state.get("last_scan"),
    44→            "command": "ignore",
    45→            "pattern": args.pattern,
    46→            "attestation": attestation,
    47→            "affected": removed,
    48→        }
    49→    )
    50→    _save_state_or_exit(state, state_file)
    51→
    52→    print(colorize(f"Added ignore pattern: {args.pattern}", "green"))
    53→    if removed:

    55→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    56→    if config_warning:

    58→    show_score_with_plan_context(state, prev)

    62→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(

1→"""exclude command: add path patterns and clean stale queue/state entries."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import sys
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    11→from desloppify.core import config as config_mod
    12→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import CommandError
    13→from desloppify.core.file_paths import matches_exclusion
    14→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    15→from desloppify.core.tooling import check_config_staleness
    16→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    17→    load_plan,
    18→    plan_path_for_state,
    19→    purge_ids,
    20→    save_plan,
    21→)
    22→
    23→
    24→def _state_file_for_runtime(runtime) -> Path:
    25→    """Resolve the effective state file path for a command runtime."""
    26→    state_file = runtime.state_path
    27→    if isinstance(state_file, Path):
    28→        return state_file
    29→    return state_mod.STATE_FILE
    30→
    31→
    32→def _prune_excluded_findings(state: dict, pattern: str) -> list[str]:
    33→    """Drop findings whose file path matches a new exclusion pattern."""
    34→    findings = state.get("findings")
    35→    if not isinstance(findings, dict):
    36→        return []
    37→
    38→    removed_ids = [
    39→        finding_id
    40→        for finding_id, finding in findings.items()
    41→        if isinstance(finding, dict)
    42→        and matches_exclusion(str(finding.get("file", "")), pattern)
    43→    ]
    44→    for finding_id in removed_ids:
    45→        findings.pop(finding_id, None)
    46→    return removed_ids
    47→
    48→
    49→def _purge_removed_ids_from_plan(state_file: Path, removed_ids: list[str]) -> int:
    50→    """Remove pruned finding IDs from queue/skips/clusters in plan.json."""
    51→    if not removed_ids:
    52→        return 0
    53→    plan_file = plan_path_for_state(state_file)
    54→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
    55→    purged = purge_ids(plan, removed_ids)
    56→    if purged:
    57→        save_plan(plan, plan_file)
    58→    return purged
    59→
    60→
    61→def cmd_exclude(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    62→    """Add a path pattern to the exclude list and clean cached queue/state."""
    63→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    64→    config = runtime.config
    65→    state = runtime.state
    66→    state_file = _state_file_for_runtime(runtime)
    67→
    68→    config_mod.add_exclude_pattern(config, args.pattern)
    69→    config["needs_rescan"] = True
    70→    try:
    71→        config_mod.save_config(config)
    72→    except OSError as exc:
    73→        print_error(f"could not save config: {exc}")
    74→        sys.exit(1)
    75→
    76→    removed_ids: list[str] = []
    77→    plan_purged = 0
    78→    if state_file.exists():
    79→        removed_ids = _prune_excluded_findings(state, args.pattern)
    80→        if removed_ids:
    81→            try:
    82→                state_mod.save_state(state, state_file)
    83→            except OSError as exc:
    84→                print_error(f"could not save state: {exc}")
    85→                sys.exit(1)
    86→
    87→            try:
    88→                plan_purged = _purge_removed_ids_from_plan(state_file, removed_ids)
    89→            except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError, KeyError):
    90→                print(
    91→                    colorize("  Warning: could not update living plan.", "yellow"),
    92→                    file=sys.stderr,
    93→                )
    94→
    95→    print(colorize(f"Added exclude pattern: {args.pattern}", "green"))
    96→    if removed_ids:
    97→        print(f"  Removed {len(removed_ids)} matching findings from state.")
    98→        if plan_purged:
    99→            print(
   100→                colorize(
   101→                    f"  Plan updated: {plan_purged} item(s) removed from queue.",
   102→                    "dim",
   103→                )
   104→            )
   105→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
   106→    if config_warning:
   107→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
   108→
   109→
   110→__all__ = ["cmd_exclude"]
   111→

1→"""move command: move a file or directory and update all import references."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import sys
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify import languages as lang_mod
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.move.move_apply import apply_file_move
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.move.move_directory import run_directory_move
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.move.move_language import (
    13→    load_lang_move_module,
    14→    resolve_lang_for_file_move,
    15→    resolve_move_verify_hint,
    16→    supported_ext_hint,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.app.commands.move.move_planning import (
    19→    compute_replacements,
    20→    resolve_dest,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.app.commands.move.move_reporting import print_file_move_plan
    23→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import rel, resolve_path
    24→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    25→
    26→
    27→def cmd_move(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    28→    """Move a file or directory and update all import references."""
    29→    source_rel = args.source
    30→    source_abs = resolve_path(source_rel)
    31→    source_path = Path(source_abs)
    32→
    33→    if source_path.is_dir():
    34→        return _cmd_move_dir(args, source_abs)
    35→
    36→    if not source_path.is_file():
    37→        print(colorize(f"Source not found: {rel(source_abs)}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
    38→        sys.exit(1)
    39→
    40→    dest_abs = resolve_dest(source_rel, args.dest, resolve_path)
    41→    if Path(dest_abs).exists():
    42→        print(
    43→            colorize(f"Destination already exists: {rel(dest_abs)}", "red"),
    44→            file=sys.stderr,
    45→        )
    46→        sys.exit(1)
    47→
    48→    dry_run = getattr(args, "dry_run", False)
    49→
    50→    lang_name = resolve_lang_for_file_move(source_abs, args)
    51→    if not lang_name:
    52→        print(
    53→            colorize(
    54→                (
    55→                    "Cannot detect language. Use --lang or ensure file has one of: "
    56→                    f"{supported_ext_hint()}"
    57→                ),
    58→                "red",
    59→            ),
    60→            file=sys.stderr,
    61→        )
    62→        sys.exit(1)
    63→
    64→    lang = lang_mod.get_lang(lang_name)
    65→    move_mod = load_lang_move_module(lang_name)
    66→
    67→    scan_path = Path(resolve_path(lang.default_src))
    68→    importer_changes, self_changes = compute_replacements(
    69→        move_mod,
    70→        source_abs,
    71→        dest_abs,
    72→        lang.build_dep_graph(scan_path),
    73→    )
    74→
    75→    print_file_move_plan(source_abs, dest_abs, importer_changes, self_changes)
    76→    if dry_run:
    77→        print(colorize("  Dry run — no files modified.", "yellow"))
    78→        return
    79→
    80→    apply_file_move(source_abs, dest_abs, importer_changes, self_changes)
    81→
    82→    print(colorize("  Done.", "green"))
    83→    verify_hint = resolve_move_verify_hint(move_mod)
    84→    if verify_hint:
    85→        print(colorize(f"  Run `{verify_hint}` to verify.", "dim"))
    86→    print()
    87→
    88→
    89→def _cmd_move_dir(args, source_abs: str):
    90→    """Move a directory (package) and update all import references."""
    91→    run_directory_move(args, source_abs, resolve_path)
    92→

1→"""zone command: show/set/clear zone classifications."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.rendering import print_agent_plan
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
    12→from desloppify.core import config as config_mod
    13→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import CommandError
    14→from desloppify.engine.policy.zones import FileZoneMap, Zone
    15→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import rel
    16→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    17→
    18→
    19→def cmd_zone(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    20→    """Handle zone subcommands: show, set, clear."""
    21→    action = getattr(args, "zone_action", None)
    22→    if action in (None, "show"):
    23→        _zone_show(args)
    24→    elif action == "set":
    25→        _zone_set(args)
    26→    elif action == "clear":
    27→        _zone_clear(args)
    28→    else:
    29→        raise CommandError("Usage: desloppify zone {show|set|clear}")
    30→
    31→
    32→def _zone_show(args):
    33→    """Show zone classifications for all scanned files."""
    34→    state_file = state_path(args)
    35→    if not state_file.exists():
    36→        raise CommandError("No state file found — run a scan first.")
    37→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    38→    if not lang or not lang.file_finder:
    39→        raise CommandError("No language detected — run a scan first.")
    40→
    41→    path = Path(args.path)
    42→    overrides = command_runtime(args).config.get("zone_overrides", {})
    43→
    44→    files = lang.file_finder(path)
    45→    zone_map = FileZoneMap(
    46→        files, lang.zone_rules, rel_fn=rel, overrides=overrides or None
    47→    )
    48→
    49→    # Group files by zone
    50→    by_zone: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    51→    for f in sorted(files, key=lambda f: rel(f)):
    52→        zone = zone_map.get(f)
    53→        by_zone.setdefault(zone.value, []).append(f)
    54→
    55→    total = len(files)
    56→    print(colorize(f"\nZone classifications ({total} files)\n", "bold"))
    57→
    58→    for zone_val in ["production", "test", "config", "generated", "script", "vendor"]:
    59→        zone_files = by_zone.get(zone_val, [])
    60→        if not zone_files:
    61→            continue
    62→        print(colorize(f"  {zone_val} ({len(zone_files)} files)", "bold"))
    63→        for f in zone_files:
    64→            rp = rel(f)
    65→            is_override = rp in overrides
    66→            suffix = colorize(" (override)", "cyan") if is_override else ""
    67→            print(f"    {rp}{suffix}")
    68→        print()
    69→
    70→    if overrides:
    71→        print(colorize(f"  {len(overrides)} override(s) active", "dim"))
    72→    print(colorize("  Override: desloppify zone set <file> <zone>", "dim"))
    73→    print(colorize("  Clear:    desloppify zone clear <file>", "dim"))
    74→    print_agent_plan(
    75→        ["Fix misclassified files, then re-scan."],
    76→        next_command="desloppify scan",
    77→    )
    78→
    79→
    80→def _zone_set(args):
    81→    """Set a zone override for a file."""
    82→    filepath = args.zone_path
    83→    zone_value = args.zone_value
    84→
    85→    # Validate zone value
    86→    valid_zones = {z.value for z in Zone}
    87→    if zone_value not in valid_zones:
    88→        raise CommandError(
    89→            f"Invalid zone: {zone_value}. Valid: {', '.join(sorted(valid_zones))}"
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    normalized = rel(filepath)
    93→    config = command_runtime(args).config
    94→    config.setdefault("zone_overrides", {})[normalized] = zone_value
    95→    try:
    96→        config_mod.save_config(config)
    97→    except OSError as e:
    98→        raise CommandError(f"could not save config: {e}") from e
    99→    print(f"  Set {normalized} → {zone_value}")
   100→    print(colorize("  Run `desloppify scan` to apply.", "dim"))
   101→    print(colorize("  Next command: `desloppify scan`", "dim"))
   102→
   103→
   104→def _zone_clear(args):
   105→    """Clear a zone override for a file."""
   106→    filepath = args.zone_path
   107→
   108→    normalized = rel(filepath)
   109→    config = command_runtime(args).config
   110→    overrides = config.get("zone_overrides", {})
   111→    if normalized in overrides:
   112→        del overrides[normalized]
   113→        try:
   114→            config_mod.save_config(config)
   115→        except OSError as e:
   116→            raise CommandError(f"could not save config: {e}") from e
   117→        print(f"  Cleared override for {normalized}")
   118→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify scan` to apply.", "dim"))
   119→        print(colorize("  Next command: `desloppify scan`", "dim"))
   120→    else:
   121→        print(colorize(f"  No override found for {normalized}", "yellow"))
   122→

1→"""config command: show/set/unset project configuration."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     8→from desloppify.core.config import (
     9→    CONFIG_SCHEMA,
    10→    save_config,
    11→    set_config_value,
    12→    unset_config_value,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import CommandError
    15→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    16→
    17→
    18→def cmd_config(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    19→    """Handle config subcommands: show, set, unset."""
    20→    action = getattr(args, "config_action", None)
    21→    if action == "set":
    22→        _config_set(args)
    23→    elif action == "unset":
    24→        _config_unset(args)
    25→    else:
    26→        _config_show(args)
    27→
    28→
    29→def _config_show(args):
    30→    """Print all config keys with current values and descriptions."""
    31→    config = command_runtime(args).config
    32→
    33→    print(colorize("\n  Desloppify Configuration\n", "bold"))
    34→    for key, schema in CONFIG_SCHEMA.items():
    35→        value = config.get(key, schema.default)
    36→        is_default = value == schema.default
    37→
    38→        # Format display value
    39→        if schema.type is int and key.endswith("_days") and value == 0:
    40→            display = "never (0)"
    41→        elif isinstance(value, list):
    42→            display = ", ".join(value) if value else "(empty)"
    43→        elif isinstance(value, dict):
    44→            display = f"{len(value)} entries" if value else "(empty)"
    45→        else:
    46→            display = str(value)
    47→
    48→        default_tag = colorize(" (default)", "dim") if is_default else ""
    49→        print(f"  {key:<25} {display}{default_tag}")
    50→        print(colorize(f"  {'':25} {schema.description}", "dim"))
    51→    print()
    52→
    53→
    54→def _config_set(args):
    55→    """Set a config key to a value."""
    56→    config = command_runtime(args).config
    57→    key = args.config_key
    58→    value = args.config_value
    59→
    60→    try:
    61→        set_config_value(config, key, value)
    62→    except (KeyError, ValueError) as e:
    63→        raise CommandError(str(e)) from e
    64→
    65→    try:
    66→        save_config(config)
    67→    except OSError as e:
    68→        raise CommandError(f"could not save config: {e}") from e
    69→    display = config[key]
    70→    if isinstance(display, int) and key.endswith("_days") and display == 0:
    71→        display = "never (0)"
    72→    print(colorize(f"  Set {key} = {display}", "green"))
    73→
    74→
    75→def _config_unset(args):
    76→    """Reset a config key to its default."""
    77→    config = command_runtime(args).config
    78→    key = args.config_key
    79→
    80→    try:
    81→        unset_config_value(config, key)
    82→    except KeyError as e:
    83→        raise CommandError(str(e)) from e
    84→
    85→    try:
    86→        save_config(config)
    87→    except OSError as e:
    88→        raise CommandError(f"could not save config: {e}") from e
    89→    default = CONFIG_SCHEMA[key].default
    90→    print(colorize(f"  Reset {key} to default ({default})", "green"))
    91→

1→"""Developer utilities."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import ast
     7→import keyword
     8→import re
     9→
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.dev_scaffold_templates import build_scaffold_files
    11→from desloppify.core.text_api import PROJECT_ROOT
    12→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    13→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import CommandError
    14→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    15→
    16→
    17→def cmd_dev(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    18→    """Dispatch developer subcommands."""
    19→    action = getattr(args, "dev_action", None)
    20→    if action == "scaffold-lang":
    21→        try:
    22→            _cmd_scaffold_lang(args)
    23→        except ValueError as ex:
    24→            raise CommandError(str(ex)) from ex
    25→        return
    26→    raise CommandError("Unknown dev action. Use `desloppify dev scaffold-lang`.")
    27→
    28→
    29→def _normalize_lang_name(raw: str) -> str:
    30→    name = raw.strip().lower().replace("-", "_")
    31→    if not re.fullmatch(r"[a-z][a-z0-9_]*", name):
    32→        raise ValueError("language name must match [a-z][a-z0-9_]*")
    33→    if keyword.iskeyword(name):
    34→        raise ValueError(f"language name cannot be a Python keyword: {name}")
    35→    return name
    36→
    37→
    38→def _normalize_extensions(values: list[str] | None) -> list[str]:
    39→    out: list[str] = []
    40→    for val in values or []:
    41→        ext = val.strip()
    42→        if not ext:
    43→            continue
    44→        if not ext.startswith("."):
    45→            ext = "." + ext
    46→        if not re.fullmatch(r"\.[a-z0-9]+", ext):
    47→            raise ValueError(f"invalid extension: {val!r}")
    48→        out.append(ext)
    49→    if not out:
    50→        raise ValueError("at least one --extension is required")
    51→    seen: set[str] = set()
    52→    deduped: list[str] = []
    53→    for ext in out:
    54→        if ext not in seen:
    55→            seen.add(ext)
    56→            deduped.append(ext)
    57→    return deduped
    58→
    59→
    60→def _normalize_markers(values: list[str] | None) -> list[str]:
    61→    out: list[str] = []
    62→    for val in values or []:
    63→        marker = val.strip()
    64→        if marker and marker not in out:
    65→            out.append(marker)
    66→    return out
    67→
    68→
    69→def _class_name(lang_name: str) -> str:
    70→    return "".join(part.capitalize() for part in lang_name.split("_")) + "Config"
    71→
    72→
    73→def _template_files(
    74→    lang_name: str,
    75→    extensions: list[str],
    76→    markers: list[str],
    77→    default_src: str,
    78→) -> dict[str, str]:
    79→    class_name = _class_name(lang_name)
    80→    return build_scaffold_files(
    81→        lang_name=lang_name,
    82→        class_name=class_name,
    83→        extensions=extensions,
    84→        markers=markers,
    85→        default_src=default_src,
    86→    )
    87→
    88→
    89→def _render_array(items: list[str]) -> str:
    90→    return ", ".join(repr(x) for x in items)
    91→
    92→
    93→def _append_toml_array_item(text: str, key: str, value: str) -> str:
    94→    pattern = re.compile(
    95→        rf"(^\s*{re.escape(key)}\s*=\s*\[)(.*?)(\]\s*$)",
    96→        re.MULTILINE | re.DOTALL,
    97→    )
    98→    match = pattern.search(text)
    99→    if not match:
   100→        return text
   101→
   102→    raw = match.group(2).strip()
   103→    parsed = ast.literal_eval("[" + raw + "]") if raw else []
   104→    if not isinstance(parsed, list):
   105→        return text
   106→    if value in parsed:
   107→        return text
   108→
   109→    parsed.append(value)
   110→    replacement = f"{match.group(1)}{_render_array(parsed)}{match.group(3)}"
   111→    return text[: match.start()] + replacement + text[match.end() :]
   112→
   113→
   114→def _wire_pyproject(lang_name: str) -> bool:
   115→    pyproject_path = PROJECT_ROOT / "pyproject.toml"
   116→    if not pyproject_path.is_file():
   117→        return False
   118→
   119→    original = pyproject_path.read_text()
   120→    updated = original
   121→    updated = _append_toml_array_item(
   122→        updated,
   123→        "testpaths",
   124→        f"desloppify/languages/{lang_name}/tests",
   125→    )
   126→
   127→    if updated != original:
   128→        safe_write_text(pyproject_path, updated)
   129→        return True
   130→    return False
   131→
   132→
   133→def _cmd_scaffold_lang(args) -> None:
   134→    raw_name = getattr(args, "name", "")
   135→    lang_name = _normalize_lang_name(raw_name)
   136→    extensions = _normalize_extensions(getattr(args, "extension", None))
   137→    markers = _normalize_markers(getattr(args, "marker", None))
   138→    default_src = getattr(args, "default_src", "src") or "src"
   139→    force = bool(getattr(args, "force", False))
   140→    wire_pyproject = bool(getattr(args, "wire_pyproject", True))
   141→
   142→    lang_dir = PROJECT_ROOT / "desloppify" / "languages" / lang_name
   143→    if lang_dir.exists() and not force:
   144→        raise CommandError(
   145→            f"Language directory already exists: {lang_dir}. Use --force to overwrite."
   146→        )
   147→
   148→    files = _template_files(lang_name, extensions, markers, default_src)
   149→    for rel_path, content in files.items():
   150→        target = lang_dir / rel_path
   151→        target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   152→        if target.exists() and not force:
   153→            continue
   154→        safe_write_text(target, content)
   155→
   156→    wired = _wire_pyproject(lang_name) if wire_pyproject else False
   157→
   158→    print(colorize(f"Scaffolded language plugin: {lang_name}", "green"))
   159→    print(f"  Path: {lang_dir}")
   160→    print(f"  Extensions: {', '.join(extensions)}")
   161→    print(f"  Markers: {', '.join(markers) if markers else '(none)'}")
   162→    print(f"  pyproject.toml updated: {'yes' if wired else 'no'}")
   163→    print(
   164→        colorize(
   165→            "  Next: implement real phases/commands/detectors and run pytest.", "dim"
   166→        )
   167→    )
   168→

1→"""langs command: list all available language plugins with depth and tools."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import logging
     7→
     8→from desloppify.languages._framework import registry_state
     9→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import LangConfig
    10→from desloppify.languages._framework.discovery import load_all
    11→from desloppify.languages._framework.generic import SHARED_PHASE_LABELS
    12→from desloppify.languages._framework.resolution import make_lang_config
    13→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    14→
    15→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    16→
    17→_DEPTH_BARS = {
    18→    "full": 8,
    19→    "standard": 5,
    20→    "shallow": 3,
    21→    "minimal": 2,
    22→}
    23→
    24→_DEPTH_MAX = 8
    25→
    26→
    27→def _depth_bar(depth: str) -> str:
    28→    """Render a depth bar like ████░░░░."""
    29→    filled = _DEPTH_BARS.get(depth, 2)
    30→    empty = _DEPTH_MAX - filled
    31→    return "\u2588" * filled + "\u2591" * empty
    32→
    33→
    34→def _get_tool_labels(cfg: LangConfig) -> str:
    35→    """Extract tool labels from phases."""
    36→    if cfg.integration_depth == "full":
    37→        return "custom detectors"
    38→    labels = [p.label for p in cfg.phases if p.label not in SHARED_PHASE_LABELS]
    39→    suffix = " (auto-fix)" if cfg.fixers else ""
    40→    return (", ".join(labels) if labels else "none") + suffix
    41→
    42→
    43→def cmd_langs(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    44→    """List all available languages with depth and tool info."""
    45→    load_all()
    46→
    47→    configs: list[tuple[str, LangConfig]] = []
    48→    for name, obj in sorted(registry_state.all_items()):
    49→        if isinstance(obj, LangConfig):
    50→            configs.append((name, obj))
    51→        else:
    52→            try:
    53→                cfg = make_lang_config(name, obj)
    54→                configs.append((name, cfg))
    55→            except (TypeError, ValueError, KeyError, AttributeError) as exc:
    56→                logger.debug("Skipping unresolvable lang config %s: %s", name, exc)
    57→                continue
    58→
    59→    # Sort: full first, then standard, shallow, minimal; alphabetical within
    60→    depth_order = {"full": 0, "standard": 1, "shallow": 2, "minimal": 3}
    61→    configs.sort(key=lambda x: (depth_order.get(x[1].integration_depth, 9), x[0]))
    62→
    63→    # Column widths
    64→    name_width = max(len(name) for name, _ in configs) + 2 if configs else 14
    65→    name_width = max(name_width, 14)
    66→
    67→    print()
    68→    header = f"{'Language':<{name_width}}{'Depth':<14}Tools"
    69→    print(colorize(header, "bold"))
    70→    print("\u2500" * 60)
    71→
    72→    for name, cfg in configs:
    73→        depth = getattr(cfg, "integration_depth", "full")
    74→        bar = _depth_bar(depth)
    75→        tools = _get_tool_labels(cfg)
    76→        print(f"{name:<{name_width}}{bar}    {tools}")
    77→    print()
    78→

1→"""CLI entrypoint for review command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import sys
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    10→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import CommandError
    11→
    12→from .batch import _do_run_batches, do_import_run
    13→from .external import do_external_start, do_external_submit
    14→from .import_cmd import do_import, do_validate_import
    15→from .merge import do_merge
    16→from .preflight import review_rerun_preflight
    17→from .prepare import do_prepare
    18→
    19→
    20→def _enable_live_review_output() -> None:
    21→    """Best-effort: force line-buffered review output for non-TTY runners."""
    22→    for stream_name in ("stdout", "stderr"):
    23→        stream = getattr(sys, stream_name, None)
    24→        reconfigure = getattr(stream, "reconfigure", None)
    25→        if not callable(reconfigure):
    26→            continue
    27→        try:
    28→            reconfigure(line_buffering=True, write_through=True)
    29→        except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
    30→            _ = exc
    31→
    32→
    33→def _require_lang(lang) -> None:
    34→    if lang:
    35→        return
    36→    raise CommandError("Error: could not detect language. Use --lang.", exit_code=1)
    37→
    38→
    39→def _mode_flags(args: argparse.Namespace) -> tuple[list[bool], object, object]:
    40→    merge = bool(getattr(args, "merge", False))
    41→    run_batches = bool(getattr(args, "run_batches", False))
    42→    import_run_dir = bool(getattr(args, "import_run_dir", None))
    43→    external_start = bool(getattr(args, "external_start", False))
    44→    external_submit = bool(getattr(args, "external_submit", False))
    45→    import_file = getattr(args, "import_file", None)
    46→    validate_import_file = getattr(args, "validate_import_file", None)
    47→    import_mode = bool(import_file) and not external_submit
    48→    flags = [
    49→        merge,
    50→        run_batches,
    51→        import_run_dir,
    52→        external_start,
    53→        external_submit,
    54→        import_mode,
    55→        bool(validate_import_file),
    56→    ]
    57→    return flags, import_file, validate_import_file
    58→
    59→
    60→def _validate_mode_selection(
    61→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    62→    *,
    63→    mode_flags: list[bool],
    64→    import_file: object,
    65→) -> None:
    66→    if sum(1 for enabled in mode_flags if enabled) > 1:
    67→        raise CommandError(
    68→            "Error: choose one review mode per command "
    69→            "(--merge | --run-batches | --import-run | --external-start | --external-submit | --import | --validate-import).",
    70→            exit_code=1,
    71→        )
    72→
    73→    external_submit = bool(getattr(args, "external_submit", False))
    74→    if external_submit and not import_file:
    75→        raise CommandError(
    76→            "Error: --external-submit requires --import FILE.",
    77→            exit_code=2,
    78→        )
    79→    if external_submit and not getattr(args, "session_id", None):
    80→        raise CommandError(
    81→            "Error: --external-submit requires --session-id.",
    82→            exit_code=2,
    83→        )
    84→
    85→
    86→def _run_review_mode(
    87→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    88→    *,
    89→    runtime,
    90→    state,
    91→    lang,
    92→    state_file,
    93→    import_file,
    94→    validate_import_file,
    95→) -> None:
    96→    if bool(getattr(args, "merge", False)):
    97→        do_merge(args)
    98→        return
    99→    if bool(getattr(args, "run_batches", False)):
   100→        review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   101→        _do_run_batches(
   102→            args,
   103→            state,
   104→            lang,
   105→            state_file,
   106→            config=runtime.config,
   107→        )
   108→        return
   109→    import_run_dir = getattr(args, "import_run_dir", None)
   110→    if import_run_dir:
   111→        do_import_run(
   112→            import_run_dir,
   113→            state,
   114→            lang,
   115→            state_file,
   116→            config=runtime.config,
   117→            allow_partial=bool(getattr(args, "allow_partial", False)),
   118→            scan_after_import=bool(getattr(args, "scan_after_import", False)),
   119→            scan_path=str(getattr(args, "path", ".") or "."),
   120→        )
   121→        return
   122→    if bool(getattr(args, "external_start", False)):
   123→        review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   124→        do_external_start(
   125→            args,
   126→            state,
   127→            lang,
   128→            config=runtime.config,
   129→        )
   130→        return
   131→    if bool(getattr(args, "external_submit", False)):
   132→        do_external_submit(
   133→            import_file=str(import_file),
   134→            session_id=str(getattr(args, "session_id")),
   135→            state=state,
   136→            lang=lang,
   137→            state_file=state_file,
   138→            config=runtime.config,
   139→            allow_partial=bool(getattr(args, "allow_partial", False)),
   140→            scan_after_import=bool(getattr(args, "scan_after_import", False)),
   141→            scan_path=str(getattr(args, "path", ".") or "."),
   142→            dry_run=bool(getattr(args, "dry_run", False)),
   143→        )
   144→        return
   145→    if validate_import_file:
   146→        do_validate_import(
   147→            validate_import_file,
   148→            lang,
   149→            allow_partial=bool(getattr(args, "allow_partial", False)),
   150→            manual_override=bool(getattr(args, "manual_override", False)),
   151→            attested_external=bool(getattr(args, "attested_external", False)),
   152→            manual_attest=getattr(args, "attest", None),
   153→        )
   154→        return
   155→
   156→    if import_file:
   157→        do_import(
   158→            import_file,
   159→            state,
   160→            lang,
   161→            state_file,
   162→            config=runtime.config,
   163→            allow_partial=bool(getattr(args, "allow_partial", False)),
   164→            manual_override=bool(getattr(args, "manual_override", False)),
   165→            attested_external=bool(getattr(args, "attested_external", False)),
   166→            manual_attest=getattr(args, "attest", None),
   167→        )
   168→        return
   169→    review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   170→    do_prepare(args, state, lang, state_file, config=runtime.config)
   171→
   172→
   173→def cmd_review(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   174→    """Prepare or import subjective code review findings."""
   175→    _enable_live_review_output()
   176→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   177→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   178→    state = runtime.state
   179→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   180→    _require_lang(lang)
   181→
   182→    mode_flags, import_file, validate_import_file = _mode_flags(args)
   183→    _validate_mode_selection(
   184→        args,
   185→        mode_flags=mode_flags,
   186→        import_file=import_file,
   187→    )
   188→    _run_review_mode(
   189→        args,
   190→        runtime=runtime,
   191→        state=state,
   192→        lang=lang,
   193→        state_file=state_file,
   194→        import_file=import_file,
   195→        validate_import_file=validate_import_file,
   196→    )

1→"""update-skill command: install or update the desloppify skill document."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import urllib.error
     7→import urllib.request
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.path_io_api import safe_write_text
    10→from desloppify.core.paths_api import get_project_root
    11→from desloppify.core.skill_docs import (
    12→    SKILL_BEGIN,
    13→    SKILL_END,
    14→    SKILL_TARGETS,
    15→    SKILL_VERSION,
    16→    SKILL_VERSION_RE,
    17→    SkillInstall,
    18→    find_installed_skill,
    19→)
    20→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    21→
    22→_RAW_BASE = (
    23→    "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/peteromallet/desloppify/main/docs"
    24→)
    25→
    26→
    27→def _download(filename: str) -> str:
    28→    """Download a file from the desloppify docs directory on GitHub."""
    29→    url = f"{_RAW_BASE}/{filename}"
    30→    with urllib.request.urlopen(url, timeout=15) as resp:  # noqa: S310
    31→        return resp.read().decode("utf-8")
    32→
    33→
    34→def _build_section(skill_content: str, overlay_content: str | None) -> str:
    35→    """Assemble the complete skill section from downloaded parts."""
    36→    parts = [skill_content.rstrip()]
    37→    if overlay_content:
    38→        parts.append(overlay_content.rstrip())
    39→    return "\n\n".join(parts) + "\n"
    40→
    41→
    42→def _replace_section(file_content: str, new_section: str) -> str:
    43→    """Replace the desloppify section in a shared file, preserving surrounding content.
    44→
    45→    Uses first ``<!-- desloppify-begin -->`` and last ``<!-- desloppify-end -->``
    46→    so the overlay (which also has an end marker) is captured correctly.
    47→    """
    48→    begin = file_content.find(SKILL_BEGIN)
    49→    end = file_content.rfind(SKILL_END)
    50→    if begin == -1 or end == -1:
    51→        # No section markers — append (first install into existing shared file).
    52→        return file_content.rstrip() + "\n\n" + new_section
    53→
    54→    before = file_content[:begin]
    55→    after = file_content[end + len(SKILL_END):]
    56→    before = before.rstrip() + "\n\n" if before.strip() else ""
    57→    after = "\n" + after.lstrip("\n") if after.strip() else "\n"
    58→    return before + new_section + after
    59→
    60→
    61→def resolve_interface(
    62→    explicit: str | None = None,
    63→    install: SkillInstall | None = None,
    64→) -> str | None:
    65→    """Resolve which interface to update.
    66→
    67→    Uses the explicit argument if given, otherwise infers from an existing
    68→    install's overlay marker or file path.
    69→    """
    70→    if explicit:
    71→        return explicit.lower()
    72→
    73→    if install is None:
    74→        install = find_installed_skill()
    75→    if not install:
    76→        return None
    77→
    78→    if install.overlay:
    79→        return install.overlay.lower()
    80→
    81→    for name, (target, _overlay, _ded) in SKILL_TARGETS.items():
    82→        if target == install.rel_path:
    83→            return name
    84→    return None
    85→
    86→
    87→def update_installed_skill(interface: str) -> bool:
    88→    """Download and install the skill document for the given interface.
    89→
    90→    Returns True on success, False on failure. Prints status messages.
    91→    """
    92→    target_rel, overlay_name, dedicated = SKILL_TARGETS[interface]
    93→    target_path = get_project_root() / target_rel
    94→
    95→    print(colorize(f"Downloading skill document ({interface})...", "dim"))
    96→    try:
    97→        skill_content = _download("SKILL.md")
    98→        overlay_content = _download(f"{overlay_name}.md") if overlay_name else None
    99→    except (urllib.error.URLError, OSError) as exc:
   100→        print(colorize(f"Download failed: {exc}", "red"))
   101→        return False
   102→
   103→    if "desloppify-skill-version" not in skill_content:
   104→        print(colorize("Downloaded content doesn't look like a skill document.", "red"))
   105→        return False
   106→
   107→    new_section = _build_section(skill_content, overlay_content)
   108→
   109→    target_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   110→    if dedicated:
   111→        result = new_section
   112→    elif target_path.is_file():
   113→        existing = target_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
   114→        result = _replace_section(existing, new_section)
   115→    else:
   116→        result = new_section
   117→
   118→    safe_write_text(target_path, result)
   119→
   120→    version_match = SKILL_VERSION_RE.search(new_section)
   121→    version = version_match.group(1) if version_match else "?"
   122→    print(
   123→        colorize(
   124→            f"Updated {target_rel} (v{version}, tool expects v{SKILL_VERSION})",
   125→            "green",
   126→        )
   127→    )
   128→    return True
   129→
   130→
   131→def cmd_update_skill(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   132→    """Install or update the desloppify skill document."""
   133→    interface = resolve_interface(getattr(args, "interface", None))
   134→
   135→    if not interface:
   136→        print(colorize("No installed skill document found.", "yellow"))
   137→        print()
   138→        names = ", ".join(sorted(SKILL_TARGETS))
   139→        print(f"Install with: desloppify update-skill <{names}>")
   140→        return
   141→
   142→    if interface not in SKILL_TARGETS:
   143→        names = ", ".join(sorted(SKILL_TARGETS))
   144→        print(colorize(f"Unknown interface '{interface}'.", "red"))
   145→        print(f"Available: {names}")
   146→        return
   147→
   148→    update_installed_skill(interface)
   149→

    29→
    30→
    31→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    32→    """Generate the prioritized markdown plan (existing behavior)."""
    33→    # Auto-resolve the create-plan workflow item when plan runs
    34→    plan = load_plan()
    35→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in plan.get("queue_order", []):
    36→        purge_ids(plan, [WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID])
    37→        save_plan(plan)
    38→    _plan_output_impl(args)
    39→
    40→
    41→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    42→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    43→    plan = load_plan()
    44→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    45→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    46→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    47→    temp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "temporary")
    48→    perm_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "permanent")
    49→    fp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "false_positive")
    50→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    51→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    52→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    53→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    54→
    55→    # Count meaningful annotations (descriptions and notes)
    56→    described = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("description"))
    57→    noted = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("note"))
    58→
    59→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
    60→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
    61→    print(f"  Queue:            {ordered} items prioritized")
    62→    if total_skipped:
    63→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
    64→    else:
    65→        print(f"  Skipped:          0")
    66→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
    67→    if clusters:
    68→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
    69→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    70→            member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    71→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
    72→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
    73→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
    74→    if described or noted:
    75→        print(f"  Annotations:      {described} described, {noted} noted")
    76→    if active:
    77→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
    78→    if superseded:
    79→        print(f"  Disappeared:      {superseded} (resolved or removed since last scan)")
    80→
    81→    # Commit tracking summary
    82→    from desloppify.core.config import load_config as _load_config
    83→
    84→    _cfg = _load_config()
    85→    if _cfg.get("commit_tracking_enabled", True):
    86→        from desloppify.engine.plan import commit_tracking_summary as _ct_summary
    87→
    88→        ct = _ct_summary(plan)
    89→        if ct["total"] > 0:
    90→            pr_num = _cfg.get("commit_pr", 0)
    91→            pr_str = f"  PR: #{pr_num}" if pr_num else ""
    92→            print(
    93→                f"  Commit tracking:  {ct['uncommitted']} uncommitted, "
    94→                f"{ct['committed']} committed ({ct['total']} findings){pr_str}"
    95→            )
    96→
    97→
    98→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    99→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
   100→    plan = load_plan()
   101→    queue_len = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   102→    cluster_count = len(plan.get("clusters", {}))
   103→    reset_plan(plan)
   104→    append_log_entry(
   105→        plan, "reset", actor="user",
   106→        detail={"previous_queue_size": queue_len, "previous_cluster_count": cluster_count},
   107→    )
   108→    save_plan(plan)
   109→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
   110→
   111→
   112→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   113→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
   114→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
   115→
   116→    if plan_action is None:
   117→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
   118→        return
   119→
   120→    if plan_action == "show":
   121→        _cmd_plan_show(args)
   122→        return
   123→
   124→    if plan_action == "queue":
   125→        cmd_plan_queue(args)
   126→        return
   127→
   128→    if plan_action == "reset":
   129→        _cmd_plan_reset(args)
   130→        return
   131→
   132→    if plan_action == "move":
   133→        cmd_plan_move(args)
   134→        return
   135→
   136→    if plan_action in (
   137→        "describe", "note", "focus",
   138→        "skip", "unskip", "reopen", "done",
   139→    ):
   140→        dispatch = {
   141→            "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   142→            "done": cmd_plan_done,
   143→            "note": cmd_plan_note,
   144→            "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   145→            "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   146→            "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   147→            "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   148→        }
   149→        dispatch[plan_action](args)
   150→        return
   151→
   152→    if plan_action == "cluster":
   153→        cmd_cluster_dispatch(args)
   154→        return
   155→
   156→    if plan_action == "triage":
   157→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage_handlers import cmd_plan_triage
   158→
   159→        cmd_plan_triage(args)
   160→        return
   161→
   162→    if plan_action == "commit-log":
   163→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.commit_log_handlers import cmd_commit_log_dispatch
   164→
   165→        cmd_commit_log_dispatch(args)
   166→        return
   167→
   168→    print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   169→
   170→
   171→# Backwards-compatible alias
   172→cmd_plan_output = cmd_plan
   173→
   174→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]
   175→

1→"""Codebase treemap visualization with HTML output and LLM-readable tree text."""
     2→
     3→import argparse
     4→import json
     5→import logging
     6→import sys
     7→from collections import defaultdict
     8→from dataclasses import dataclass
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→from typing import Any
    11→
    12→from desloppify.app.output._viz_cmd_context import load_cmd_context
    13→from desloppify.app.output.tree_text import render_tree_lines
    14→from desloppify.core.file_paths import resolve_scan_file
    15→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import (
    16→    log_best_effort_failure,
    17→    print_write_error,
    18→    warn_best_effort,
    19→)
    20→from desloppify.core.output_contract import OutputResult
    21→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import rel, safe_write_text
    22→from desloppify.state import get_objective_score, get_overall_score, get_strict_score
    23→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    24→
    25→D3_CDN_URL = "https://d3js.org/d3.v7.min.js"
    26→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    27→_RECOVERABLE_LANG_RESOLUTION_ERRORS = (
    28→    ImportError,
    29→    ValueError,
    30→    TypeError,
    31→    AttributeError,
    32→    OSError,
    33→    RuntimeError,
    34→)
    35→
    36→
    37→__all__ = ["D3_CDN_URL", "cmd_viz", "cmd_tree"]
    38→
    39→
    40→def _resolve_visualization_lang(path: Path, lang):
    41→    """Resolve language config for visualization if not already provided."""
    42→    if lang:
    43→        return lang
    44→    from desloppify.languages import auto_detect_lang, get_lang
    45→
    46→    search_roots = [path if path.is_dir() else path.parent]
    47→    search_roots.extend(search_roots[0].parents)
    48→    warned = False
    49→    for root in search_roots:
    50→        try:
    51→            detected = auto_detect_lang(root)
    52→        except _RECOVERABLE_LANG_RESOLUTION_ERRORS as exc:
    53→            log_best_effort_failure(
    54→                logger,
    55→                f"auto-detect visualization language for {root}",
    56→                exc,
    57→            )
    58→            if not warned:
    59→                warned = True
    60→                warn_best_effort(
    61→                    "Could not auto-detect language plugins for visualization; "
    62→                    f"using fallback source discovery ({type(exc).__name__}: {exc})."
    63→                )
    64→            continue
    65→        if detected:
    66→            try:
    67→                return get_lang(detected)
    68→            except _RECOVERABLE_LANG_RESOLUTION_ERRORS as exc:
    69→                log_best_effort_failure(
    70→                    logger,
    71→                    f"load visualization language plugin '{detected}'",
    72→                    exc,
    73→                )
    74→                if not warned:
    75→                    warned = True
    76→                    warn_best_effort(
    77→                        "Visualization language plugin failed to load; using fallback source discovery "
    78→                        f"({type(exc).__name__}: {exc})."
    79→                    )
    80→                continue
    81→    return None
    82→
    83→
    84→def _fallback_source_files(path: Path) -> list[str]:
    85→    """Collect source files using extensions from all registered language plugins."""
    86→    from desloppify.core.discovery_api import find_source_files
    87→    from desloppify.languages import available_langs, get_lang
    88→
    89→    extensions: set[str] = set()
    90→    warned = False
    91→    for lang_name in available_langs():
    92→        try:
    93→            cfg = get_lang(lang_name)
    94→        except _RECOVERABLE_LANG_RESOLUTION_ERRORS as exc:
    95→            log_best_effort_failure(
    96→                logger,
    97→                f"load fallback visualization language plugin '{lang_name}'",
    98→                exc,
    99→            )
   100→            if not warned:
   101→                warned = True
   102→                warn_best_effort(
   103→                    "Some language plugins could not be loaded for visualization fallback; using available plugins only "
   104→                    f"({type(exc).__name__}: {exc})."
   105→                )
   106→            continue
   107→        extensions.update(cfg.extensions)
   108→    if not extensions:
   109→        return []
   110→    return find_source_files(path, sorted(extensions))
   111→
   112→
   113→def _collect_file_data(path: Path, lang=None) -> list[dict]:
   114→    """Collect LOC for all source files using the language's file finder."""
   115→    resolved_lang = _resolve_visualization_lang(path, lang)
   116→    if resolved_lang and resolved_lang.file_finder:
   117→        source_files = resolved_lang.file_finder(path)
   118→    else:
   119→        source_files = _fallback_source_files(path)
   120→    files = []
   121→    warned_read_failure = False
   122→    for filepath in source_files:
   123→        try:
   124→            p = resolve_scan_file(filepath, scan_root=path)
   125→            content = p.read_text()
   126→            loc = len(content.splitlines())
   127→            files.append(
   128→                {
   129→                    "path": rel(filepath),
   130→                    "abs_path": str(p.resolve()),
   131→                    "loc": loc,
   132→                }
   133→            )
   134→        except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
   135→            log_best_effort_failure(
   136→                logger, f"read visualization source file {filepath}", exc
   137→            )
   138→            if not warned_read_failure:
   139→                warned_read_failure = True
   140→                warn_best_effort(
   141→                    "Some visualization source files could not be read; output may be incomplete."
   142→                )
   143→            continue
   144→    return files
   145→
   146→
   147→def _build_tree(files: list[dict], dep_graph: dict, findings_by_file: dict) -> dict:
   148→    """Build nested tree structure for D3 treemap."""
   149→    root: dict = {"name": "src", "children": {}}
   150→
   151→    for f in files:
   152→        parts = f["path"].split("/")
   153→        # Skip leading 'src/' since root is already 'src'
   154→        if parts and parts[0] == "src":
   155→            parts = parts[1:]
   156→        node = root
   157→        for part in parts[:-1]:
   158→            if part not in node["children"]:
   159→                node["children"][part] = {"name": part, "children": {}}
   160→            node = node["children"][part]
   161→
   162→        filename = parts[-1]
   163→        resolved = f["abs_path"]
   164→        dep_entry = dep_graph.get(resolved, {"import_count": 0, "importer_count": 0})
   165→        file_findings = findings_by_file.get(f["path"], [])
   166→        open_findings = [ff for ff in file_findings if ff.get("status") == "open"]
   167→
   168→        node["children"][filename] = {
   169→            "name": filename,
   170→            "path": f["path"],
   171→            "loc": max(f["loc"], 1),  # D3 needs >0 values
   172→            "fan_in": dep_entry.get("importer_count", 0),
   173→            "fan_out": dep_entry.get("import_count", 0),
   174→            "findings_total": len(file_findings),
   175→            "findings_open": len(open_findings),
   176→            "finding_summaries": [ff.get("summary", "") for ff in open_findings[:20]],
   177→        }
   178→
   179→    # Convert children dicts to arrays (D3 format)
   180→    def to_array(node: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   181→        if "children" in node and isinstance(node["children"], dict):
   182→            children = list(node["children"].values())
   183→            for child in children:
   184→                to_array(child)
   185→            node["children"] = children
   186→            # Remove empty directories
   187→            node["children"] = [
   188→                c
   189→                for c in node["children"]
   190→                if "loc" in c or ("children" in c and c["children"])
   191→            ]
   192→
   193→    to_array(root)
   194→    return root
   195→
   196→
   197→def _build_dep_graph_for_path(path: Path, lang) -> dict:
   198→    """Build dependency graph using the resolved language plugin."""
   199→    resolved_lang = _resolve_visualization_lang(path, lang)
   200→    if resolved_lang and resolved_lang.build_dep_graph:
   201→        try:
   202→            return resolved_lang.build_dep_graph(path)
   203→        except (
   204→            OSError,
   205→            UnicodeDecodeError,
   206→            ValueError,
   207→            RuntimeError,
   208→            TypeError,
   209→        ) as exc:
   210→            log_best_effort_failure(logger, "build visualization dependency graph", exc)
   211→            warn_best_effort(
   212→                "Could not build visualization dependency graph; showing file-only view."
   213→            )
   214→    return {}
   215→
   216→
   217→def _findings_by_file(state: dict | None) -> dict[str, list]:
   218→    """Group findings from state by file path."""
   219→    result: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
   220→    if state and state.get("findings"):
   221→        for f in state["findings"].values():
   222→            result[f["file"]].append(f)
   223→    return result
   224→
   225→
   226→def _write_visualization_output(output: Path, html: str) -> OutputResult:
   227→    """Write visualization HTML to disk using the shared output-result contract."""
   228→    try:
   229→        safe_write_text(output, html)
   230→    except OSError as exc:
   231→        return OutputResult(
   232→            ok=False,
   233→            status="error",
   234→            message=str(exc),
   235→            error_kind="visualization_write_error",
   236→        )
   237→    return OutputResult(ok=True, status="written", message=f"wrote {output}")
   238→
   239→
   240→def generate_visualization(
   241→    path: Path, state: dict | None = None, output: Path | None = None, lang=None
   242→) -> tuple[str, OutputResult]:
   243→    """Generate an HTML treemap visualization and explicit output result."""
   244→    try:
   245→        files = _collect_file_data(path, lang)
   246→        dep_graph = _build_dep_graph_for_path(path, lang)
   247→        findings_by_file = _findings_by_file(state)
   248→        tree = _build_tree(files, dep_graph, findings_by_file)
   249→        # Escape </ to prevent </script> in filenames from breaking HTML
   250→        tree_json = json.dumps(tree).replace("</", r"<\/")
   251→
   252→        # Stats for header
   253→        total_files = len(files)
   254→        total_loc = sum(f["loc"] for f in files)
   255→        total_findings = sum(len(v) for v in findings_by_file.values())
   256→        open_findings = sum(
   257→            1 for fs in findings_by_file.values() for f in fs if f.get("status") == "open"
   258→        )
   259→        if state:
   260→            overall_score = get_overall_score(state)
   261→            objective_score = get_objective_score(state)
   262→            strict_score = get_strict_score(state)
   263→        else:
   264→            overall_score = objective_score = strict_score = None
   265→
   266→        def fmt_score(value):
   267→            return f"{value:.1f}" if isinstance(value, int | float) else "N/A"
   268→
   269→        replacements = {
   270→            "__D3_CDN_URL__": D3_CDN_URL,
   271→            "__TREE_DATA__": tree_json,
   272→            "__TOTAL_FILES__": str(total_files),
   273→            "__TOTAL_LOC__": f"{total_loc:,}",
   274→            "__TOTAL_FINDINGS__": str(total_findings),
   275→            "__OPEN_FINDINGS__": str(open_findings),
   276→            "__OVERALL_SCORE__": fmt_score(overall_score),
   277→            "__OBJECTIVE_SCORE__": fmt_score(objective_score),
   278→            "__STRICT_SCORE__": fmt_score(strict_score),
   279→        }
   280→        html = _get_html_template()
   281→        for placeholder, value in replacements.items():
   282→            html = html.replace(placeholder, value)
   283→    except OSError as exc:
   284→        return "", OutputResult(
   285→            ok=False,
   286→            status="error",
   287→            message=str(exc),
   288→            error_kind="visualization_generation_error",
   289→        )
   290→
   291→    if output:
   292→        write_result = _write_visualization_output(output, html)
   293→        if not write_result.ok:
   294→            message = write_result.message or "unknown write failure"
   295→            print_write_error(output, OSError(message), label="visualization")
   296→            return html, write_result
   297→        return html, write_result
   298→
   299→    return html, OutputResult(
   300→        ok=True,
   301→        status="not_requested",
   302→        message="visualization generated in memory only",
   303→    )
   304→
   305→
   306→def cmd_viz(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   307→    """Generate HTML treemap visualization."""
   308→    path, lang, state = load_cmd_context(args)
   309→    output = Path(getattr(args, "output", None) or ".desloppify/treemap.html")
   310→    print(colorize("Collecting file data and building dependency graph...", "dim"))
   311→    _, output_result = generate_visualization(path, state, output, lang=lang)
   312→    if output_result.status != "written":
   313→        message = output_result.message or "unknown write failure"
   314→        print(
   315→            colorize(
   316→                f"\nVisualization write failed ({output_result.status}): {output} ({message})",
   317→                "red",
   318→            ),
   319→            file=sys.stderr,
   320→        )
   321→        raise SystemExit(1)
   322→    print(colorize(f"\nTreemap written to {output}", "green"))
   323→    print(colorize(f"Open in browser: file://{output.resolve()}", "dim"))
   324→
   325→@dataclass
   326→class TreeTextOptions:
   327→    """Text tree rendering options."""
   328→    max_depth: int = 2
   329→    focus: str | None = None
   330→    min_loc: int = 0
   331→    sort_by: str = "loc"
   332→    detail: bool = False
   333→
   334→
   335→def generate_tree_text(
   336→    path: Path,
   337→    state: dict | None = None,
   338→    options: TreeTextOptions | None = None,
   339→    *,
   340→    lang=None,
   341→) -> str:
   342→    """Generate text-based annotated tree of the codebase."""
   343→    resolved_options = options or TreeTextOptions()
   344→    files = _collect_file_data(path, lang)
   345→    dep_graph = _build_dep_graph_for_path(path, lang)
   346→    tree = _build_tree(files, dep_graph, _findings_by_file(state))
   347→
   348→    root = tree
   349→    if resolved_options.focus:
   350→        parts = resolved_options.focus.strip("/").split("/")
   351→        if parts and parts[0] == "src":
   352→            parts = parts[1:]
   353→        for part in parts:
   354→            found = None
   355→            for child in root.get("children", []):
   356→                if child["name"] == part:
   357→                    found = child
   358→                    break
   359→            if found is None:
   360→                return f"Directory not found: {resolved_options.focus}"
   361→            root = found
   362→
   363→    lines = render_tree_lines(
   364→        root,
   365→        max_depth=resolved_options.max_depth,
   366→        min_loc=resolved_options.min_loc,
   367→        sort_by=resolved_options.sort_by,
   368→        detail=resolved_options.detail,
   369→    )
   370→    return "\n".join(lines)
   371→
   372→
   373→def cmd_tree(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   374→    """Print annotated codebase tree to terminal."""
   375→    path, lang, state = load_cmd_context(args)
   376→    print(
   377→        generate_tree_text(
   378→            path,
   379→            state,
   380→            options=TreeTextOptions(
   381→                max_depth=getattr(args, "depth", 2),
   382→                focus=getattr(args, "focus", None),
   383→                min_loc=getattr(args, "min_loc", 0),
   384→                sort_by=getattr(args, "sort", "loc"),
   385→                detail=getattr(args, "detail", False),
   386→            ),
   387→            lang=lang,
   388→        )
   389→    )
   390→
   391→
   392→def _get_html_template() -> str:
   393→    """Read the HTML treemap template from the external file."""
   394→    return (Path(__file__).parent / "_viz_template.html").read_text()
   395→

    46→def _plan_file_for_state(state_file: Path | None) -> Path | None:

    49→    return plan_path_for_state(state_file)
    50→
    51→
    52→def _snapshot_file(path: Path) -> str | None:
    53→    if not path.exists():
    54→        return None
    55→    return path.read_text()
    56→
    57→
    58→def _restore_file_snapshot(path: Path, snapshot: str | None) -> None:
    59→    if snapshot is None:
    60→        try:
    61→            path.unlink()
    62→        except FileNotFoundError:
    63→            return
    64→        return
    65→    safe_write_text(path, snapshot)

    68→def _save_plan_state_transactional(
    69→    *,
    70→    plan: dict,
    71→    plan_path: Path | None,
    72→    state_data: dict,
    73→    state_path_value: Path | None,
    74→) -> None:
    75→    """Persist plan+state together; rollback both files on partial write failure."""
    76→    effective_plan_path = _resolve_plan_file(plan_path)
    77→    effective_state_path = _resolve_state_file(state_path_value)
    78→    plan_snapshot = _snapshot_file(effective_plan_path)
    79→    state_snapshot = _snapshot_file(effective_state_path)
    80→
    81→    try:
    82→        state_mod.save_state(state_data, effective_state_path)
    83→        save_plan(plan, effective_plan_path)
    84→    except Exception:
    85→        _restore_file_snapshot(effective_state_path, state_snapshot)
    86→        _restore_file_snapshot(effective_plan_path, plan_snapshot)
    87→        raise
