```
   143→
   144→def cmd_plan_skip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   145→    """Skip findings — unified command for temporary/permanent/false-positive."""
   146→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   147→    state = runtime.state
   148→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   149→        return
   150→
   151→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   152→    reason: str | None = getattr(args, "reason", None)
   153→    review_after: int | None = getattr(args, "review_after", None)
   154→    permanent: bool = getattr(args, "permanent", False)
   155→    false_positive: bool = getattr(args, "false_positive", False)
   156→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
   157→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   158→
   159→    # Determine skip kind
   160→    if false_positive:
   161→        kind = "false_positive"
   162→    elif permanent:
   163→        kind = "permanent"
   164→    else:
   165→        kind = "temporary"
   166→
   167→    # Validate requirements for permanent/false_positive
   168→    if kind in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   169→        if not validate_attestation(attestation):
   170→            show_attestation_requirement(
   171→                "Permanent skip" if kind == "permanent" else "False positive",
   172→                attestation,
   173→                ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
   174→            )
   175→            return
   176→        if kind == "permanent" and not note:
   177→            print(
   178→                colorize("  --permanent requires --note to explain the decision.", "yellow"),
   179→                file=sys.stderr,
   180→            )
   181→            return
   182→
   183→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   184→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   185→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   186→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
   187→    if not finding_ids:
   188→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
   189→        return
   190→
   191→    # For permanent/false_positive: delegate to state layer for score impact
   192→    state_data: dict | None = None
   193→    if kind in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   194→        state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   195→        status = "wontfix" if kind == "permanent" else "false_positive"
   196→        for fid in finding_ids:
   197→            state_mod.resolve_findings(
   198→                state_data, fid, status, note or "", attestation=attestation
   199→            )
   200→
   201→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   202→    count = skip_items(
   203→        plan,
   204→        finding_ids,
   205→        kind=kind,
   206→        reason=reason,
   207→        note=note,
   208→        attestation=attestation,
   209→        review_after=review_after,
   210→        scan_count=scan_count,
   211→    )
   212→
   213→    # Log the skip action
   214→    append_log_entry(
   215→        plan,
   216→        "skip",
   217→        finding_ids=finding_ids,
   218→        actor="user",
   219→        note=note,
   220→        detail={"kind": kind, "reason": reason},
   221→    )
   222→    if state_data is not None:
   223→        _save_plan_state_transactional(
   224→            plan=plan,
   225→            plan_path=plan_file,
   226→            state_data=state_data,
   227→            state_path_value=state_file,
   228→        )
   229→    else:
   230→        save_plan(plan, plan_file)
   231→
   232→    label = {"temporary": "Skipped", "permanent": "Wontfixed", "false_positive": "Marked false positive"}
   233→    print(colorize(f"  {label[kind]} {count} item(s).", "green"))
   234→    if review_after:
   235→        print(colorize(f"  Will re-surface after {review_after} scan(s).", "dim"))
   236→
   237→
   238→def cmd_plan_unskip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   239→    """Unskip findings — bring back to queue."""
   240→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   241→    state = runtime.state
   242→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   243→        return
   244→
   245→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   246→
   247→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   248→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   249→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   250→    # For unskip we need to match against all statuses (skipped items may be wontfix/fp)
   251→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan, status_filter="all")
   252→    if not finding_ids:
   253→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
   254→        return
   255→
   256→    count, need_reopen = unskip_items(plan, finding_ids)
   257→    append_log_entry(
   258→        plan, "unskip", finding_ids=finding_ids, actor="user",
   259→        detail={"need_reopen": need_reopen},
   260→    )
   261→
   262→    # Reopen permanent/false_positive items in state
   263→    reopened: list[str] = []
   264→    if need_reopen:
   265→        state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   266→        for fid in need_reopen:
   267→            reopened.extend(state_mod.resolve_findings(state_data, fid, "open"))
   268→        _save_plan_state_transactional(
   269→            plan=plan,
   270→            plan_path=plan_file,
   271→            state_data=state_data,
   272→            state_path_value=state_file,
   273→        )
   274→        print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} finding(s) in state.", "dim"))
   275→    else:
   276→        save_plan(plan, plan_file)
   277→
   278→    print(colorize(f"  Unskipped {count} item(s) — back in queue.", "green"))
   279→
   280→
   281→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   282→# Reopen
   283→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   284→
   285→def cmd_plan_reopen(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   286→    """Reopen resolved findings from plan context."""
   287→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   288→
   289→    raw_state_path = state_path(args)
   290→    state_file = raw_state_path if isinstance(raw_state_path, Path) else Path(raw_state_path) if raw_state_path else None
   291→    state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   292→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   293→
   294→    reopened: list[str] = []
   295→    for pattern in patterns:
   296→        reopened.extend(
   297→            state_mod.resolve_findings(state_data, pattern, "open")
   298→        )
   299→
   300→    if not reopened:
   301→        print(colorize("  No resolved findings matching: " + " ".join(patterns), "yellow"))
   302→        return
   303→
   304→    # Remove from skipped if present, and ensure all reopened IDs are in queue
   305→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   306→
   307→    # Remove from commit tracking uncommitted list
   308→    from desloppify.engine.plan import purge_uncommitted_ids as _purge_uncommitted
   309→    _purge_uncommitted(plan, reopened)
   310→
   311→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   312→    count = 0
   313→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   314→    for fid in reopened:
   315→        if fid in skipped:
   316→            skipped.pop(fid)
   317→            count += 1
   318→        if fid not in order:
   319→            plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   320→            order.add(fid)
   321→            count += 1
   322→    append_log_entry(
   323→        plan, "reopen", finding_ids=reopened, actor="user",
   324→    )
   325→    _save_plan_state_transactional(
   326→        plan=plan,
   327→        plan_path=plan_file,
   328→        state_data=state_data,
   329→        state_path_value=state_file,
   330→    )
   331→
   332→    print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} finding(s).", "green"))
   333→    if count:
   334→        print(colorize(f"  Plan updated: items moved back to queue.", "dim"))
   335→
   336→
   337→_CLUSTER_INDIVIDUAL_THRESHOLD = 10
   338→
   339→
   340→def _check_cluster_guard(patterns: list[str], plan: dict, state: dict) -> bool:
   341→    """Return True if blocked by cluster guard, False if OK to proceed."""
   342→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   343→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   344→    for pattern in patterns:
   345→        if pattern in clusters:
   346→            cluster = clusters[pattern]
   347→            # Filter to alive findings only — stale IDs should not count
   348→            ids = [
   349→                fid for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   350→                if fid in findings and findings[fid].get("status") == "open"
   351→            ]
   352→            if len(ids) == 0:
   353→                print(colorize(
   354→                    f"\n  Cluster '{pattern}' is empty — add items before marking it done.\n",
   355→                    "yellow",
   356→                ))
   357→                print(colorize(
   358→                    f"  Use: desloppify plan cluster add {pattern} <finding-id>",
   359→                    "dim",
   360→                ))
   361→                return True  # blocked
   362→            if len(ids) <= _CLUSTER_INDIVIDUAL_THRESHOLD:
   363→                _print_cluster_guard(pattern, ids, state)
   364→                return True  # blocked
   365→    return False  # OK
   366→
   367→
   368→def _print_cluster_guard(cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str], state: dict) -> None:
   369→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   370→    print(colorize(
   371→        f"\n  Cluster '{cluster_name}' has {len(finding_ids)} item(s) — mark them done individually first:\n",
   372→        "yellow",
   373→    ))
   374→    for fid in finding_ids:
   375→        f = findings.get(fid, {})
   376→        summary = f.get("summary", "(no summary)")[:80]
   377→        detector = f.get("detector", "?")
   378→        print(f"    {fid}  [{detector}]  {summary}")
   379→    print(colorize(
   380→        f"\n  Use: desloppify resolve <id> --status fixed --note '...' --attest '...'",
   381→        "dim",
   382→    ))
   383→    print(colorize(
   384→        f"  Or mark each done: desloppify plan done <id> --note '...' --confirm\n",
   385→        "dim",
   386→    ))
   387→
   388→
   389→def _is_synthetic_id(fid: str) -> bool:
   390→    """Return True if the ID is a synthetic workflow/triage item, not a real finding."""
   391→    return fid.startswith("triage::") or fid.startswith("workflow::") or fid.startswith("subjective::")
   392→
   393→
   394→def _resolve_synthetic_ids(patterns: list[str]) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
   395→    """Separate synthetic IDs from real finding patterns.
   396→
   397→    Returns (synthetic_ids, remaining_patterns).
   398→    """
   399→    synthetic = [p for p in patterns if _is_synthetic_id(p)]
   400→    remaining = [p for p in patterns if not _is_synthetic_id(p)]
   401→    return synthetic, remaining
   402→
   403→
   404→def _blocked_triage_stages(plan: dict) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
   405→    """Return ``{stage_id: [blocked_by_ids]}`` for triage stages that can't run yet.
   406→
   407→    Uses the dependency graph and confirmed-stage metadata directly —
   408→    no state needed, no queue item construction.
   409→    """
   410→    from desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage_playbook import TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES
   411→    from desloppify.engine.plan import TRIAGE_IDS, TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS
   412→
   413→    order_set = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   414→    present = order_set & TRIAGE_IDS
   415→    if not present:
   416→        return {}
   417→
   418→    confirmed = set(plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}).get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   419→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   420→
   421→    blocked: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   422→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names):
   423→        if sid not in present or name in confirmed:
   424→            continue
   425→        deps = TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   426→        unmet = sorted(
   427→            f"triage::{dep}" for dep in deps
   428→            if f"triage::{dep}" in present and dep not in confirmed
   429→        )
   430→        if unmet:
   431→            blocked[sid] = unmet
   432→    return blocked
   433→
   434→
   435→def cmd_plan_done(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   436→    """Mark findings as fixed — delegates to cmd_resolve for rich UX."""
   437→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   438→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   439→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
   440→
   441→    # --confirm: auto-generate attestation from --note
   442→    if getattr(args, "confirm", False):
   443→        if not note:
   444→            print(colorize("  --confirm requires --note to describe what you did.", "red"))
   445→            return
   446→        attestation = f"I have actually {note} and I am not gaming the score."
   447→        args.attest = attestation
   448→
   449→    # Handle synthetic IDs (triage::*, workflow::*, subjective::*) directly
   450→    synthetic_ids, real_patterns = _resolve_synthetic_ids(patterns)
   451→    if synthetic_ids:
   452→        plan = load_plan()
   453→        # Validate triage dependency chain
   454→        blocked_map = _blocked_triage_stages(plan)
   455→        for sid in synthetic_ids:
   456→            if sid in blocked_map:
   457→                deps = ", ".join(b.replace("triage::", "") for b in blocked_map[sid])
   458→                print(colorize(f"  Cannot resolve {sid} — blocked by: {deps}", "red"))
   459→                print(colorize("  Complete those stages first, or use --force-resolve to override.", "dim"))
   460→                if not getattr(args, "force_resolve", False):
   461→                    return
   462→        purge_ids(plan, synthetic_ids)
   463→        append_log_entry(
   464→            plan, "done", finding_ids=synthetic_ids, actor="user", note=note,
   465→        )
   466→        save_plan(plan)
   467→        for sid in synthetic_ids:
   468→            print(colorize(f"  Resolved: {sid}", "green"))
   469→        if not real_patterns:
   470→            return
   471→        # Continue with remaining real patterns
   472→        patterns = real_patterns
   473→        args.patterns = patterns
   474→
   475→    # Validate note length
   476→    if not validate_note_length(note):
   477→        show_note_length_requirement(note)
   478→        return
   479→
   480→    # Pre-validate attestation before delegating (avoids stale hint in resolve)
   481→    if not validate_attestation(attestation):
   482→        show_attestation_requirement("Plan done", attestation, ATTEST_EXAMPLE)
   483→        return
   484→
   485→    # Cluster completion guard: block bulk-completing small clusters
   486→    try:
   487→        runtime = command_runtime(args)
   488→        state = runtime.state
   489→        plan = load_plan()
   490→        if _check_cluster_guard(patterns, plan, state):
   491→            return
   492→    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
   493→        plan = None
   494→
   495→    # Log the done action (best-effort)
   496→    try:
   497→        if plan is None:
   498→            plan = load_plan()
   499→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   500→        cluster_name = None
   501→        for p in patterns:
   502→            if p in clusters:
   503→                cluster_name = p
   504→                break
   505→        append_log_entry(
   506→            plan,
   507→            "done",
   508→            finding_ids=patterns,
   509→            cluster_name=cluster_name,
   510→            actor="user",
   511→            note=note,
   512→        )
   513→        save_plan(plan)
   514→    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError) as exc:
   515→        print(colorize(f"  Note: unable to append plan done log entry ({exc}).", "dim"))
   516→
   517→    # Deferred import: cmd_resolve has a heavy import chain that isn't needed
   518→    # for synthetic-ID handling above.
   519→    from desloppify.app.commands.resolve.cmd import cmd_resolve
   520→
   521→    # Build a Namespace that cmd_resolve expects
   522→    resolve_args = argparse.Namespace(
   523→        status="fixed",
   524→        patterns=patterns,
   525→        note=note,
   526→        attest=attestation,
   527→        confirm_batch_wontfix=False,
   528→        force_resolve=bool(getattr(args, "force_resolve", False)),
   529→        state=getattr(args, "state", None),
   530→        lang=getattr(args, "lang", None),
   531→        path=getattr(args, "path", None),
   532→        exclude=getattr(args, "exclude", None),
   533→    )
   534→
   535→    cmd_resolve(resolve_args)
   536→
   537→
   538→def cmd_plan_focus(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   539→    """Set or clear the active cluster focus."""
   540→    clear_flag = getattr(args, "clear", False)
   541→    cluster_name: str | None = getattr(args, "cluster_name", None)
   542→
   543→    plan = load_plan()
   544→    if clear_flag:
   545→        prev = plan.get("active_cluster")
   546→        clear_focus(plan)
   547→        append_log_entry(
   548→            plan, "focus", actor="user",
   549→            detail={"action": "clear", "previous": prev},
   550→        )
   551→        save_plan(plan)
   552→        print(colorize("  Focus cleared.", "green"))
   553→        return
   554→
   555→    if not cluster_name:
   556→        active = plan.get("active_cluster")
   557→        if active:
   558→            print(f"  Focused on: {active}")
   559→        else:
   560→            print("  No active focus.")
   561→        return
   562→
   563→    try:
   564→        set_focus(plan, cluster_name)
   565→    except ValueError as ex:
   566→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
   567→        return
   568→    append_log_entry(
   569→        plan, "focus", cluster_name=cluster_name, actor="user",
   570→        detail={"action": "set"},
   571→    )
   572→    save_plan(plan)
   573→    print(colorize(f"  Focused on: {cluster_name}", "green"))
   574→
   575→
   576→__all__ = [
   577→    "cmd_plan_describe",
   578→    "cmd_plan_done",
   579→    "cmd_plan_focus",
   580→    "cmd_plan_note",
   581→    "cmd_plan_reopen",
   582→    "cmd_plan_skip",
   583→    "cmd_plan_unskip",
   584→]
   585→
```

```
    59→def _cmd_cluster_add(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    60→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    61→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    62→        return

    77→        return
    78→
    79→    # Check for overlap with other manual clusters
    80→    member_set = set(finding_ids)
    81→    for other_name, other_cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    82→        if other_name == cluster_name or other_cluster.get("auto"):

    85→        if not other_ids:

    87→        overlap = member_set & other_ids

    91→                f"({len(overlap)}/{len(other_ids)} = {int(len(overlap)/len(other_ids)*100)}% overlap).",
    92→                "yellow",
    93→            ))
    94→
    95→    append_log_entry(
    96→        plan, "cluster_add", finding_ids=finding_ids, cluster_name=cluster_name, actor="user",
    97→    )
    98→    save_plan(plan)
    99→    print(colorize(f"  Added {count} item(s) to cluster {cluster_name}.", "green"))

   143→def _cmd_cluster_move(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   144→    raw_names: str = getattr(args, "cluster_names", "") or getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
   145→    cluster_names: list[str] = [n.strip() for n in raw_names.split(",") if n.strip()]

   151→

   156→            return
   157→
   158→    # Resolve cluster-name targets for before/after
   159→    target = resolve_target(plan, target, position)
   160→

   162→    if position in ("up", "down") and target is not None:
   163→        try:
   164→            offset = int(target)
   165→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   166→            print(colorize(f"  Invalid offset: {target}", "red"))
   167→            return
   168→        target = None
   169→
   170→    # Collect all member IDs from all clusters, preserving order, deduplicating

   179→    if not all_member_ids:

   181→        return

   183→    count = move_items(plan, all_member_ids, position, target=target, offset=offset)
   184→    append_log_entry(
   185→        plan, "cluster_move", cluster_name=",".join(cluster_names), actor="user",
   186→        detail={"position": position, "count": count},
   187→    )
   188→    save_plan(plan)
   189→    label = ", ".join(cluster_names)

   290→        print(colorize(f"  Stored {len(steps)} action step(s):", "dim"))
   291→        for i, step in enumerate(steps, 1):
   292→            clean = _LEADING_NUM_RE.sub('', step)
   293→            print(colorize(f"    {i}. {clean}", "dim"))
   294→        if len(steps) == 0:
   295→            print(colorize("  Warning: 0 steps stored. Did you forget the --steps values?", "yellow"))
   296→        elif len(steps) == 1 and len(steps[0]) > 100:
   297→            print(colorize("  Warning: only 1 step stored and it's quite long. Check shell quoting.", "yellow"))
   298→    cluster["user_modified"] = True
   299→
   300→    from desloppify.state import utc_now
   301→
   302→    cluster["updated_at"] = utc_now()
   303→    append_log_entry(
   304→        plan, "cluster_update", cluster_name=cluster_name, actor="user",
   305→        detail={"description": description, "steps": steps},
   306→    )
   307→    save_plan(plan)
   308→    print(colorize(f"  Updated cluster: {cluster_name}", "green"))
   309→
   310→
   311→def _cmd_cluster_merge(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   312→    """Merge source cluster into target cluster."""
   313→    source: str = getattr(args, "source", "")
   314→    target: str = getattr(args, "target", "")
   315→
   316→    plan = load_plan()
   317→    try:
   318→        added, source_ids = merge_clusters(plan, source, target)
   319→    except ValueError as ex:
   320→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
   321→        return
   322→
   323→    append_log_entry(
   324→        plan, "cluster_merge", finding_ids=source_ids,
   325→        cluster_name=target, actor="user",
   326→        detail={"source": source, "added": added},
   327→    )
   328→    save_plan(plan)
   329→    print(colorize(
   330→        f"  Merged cluster {source!r} into {target!r}: "
   331→        f"{added} finding(s) added, {len(source_ids)} total moved. Source deleted.",
   332→        "green",
   333→    ))
   334→
   335→
   336→def cmd_cluster_dispatch(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   337→    """Route cluster subcommands."""
   338→    cluster_action = getattr(args, "cluster_action", None)
   339→    dispatch = {
   340→        "create": _cmd_cluster_create,
   341→        "add": _cmd_cluster_add,
   342→        "remove": _cmd_cluster_remove,
   343→        "delete": _cmd_cluster_delete,
   344→        "move": _cmd_cluster_move,
   345→        "show": _cmd_cluster_show,
   346→        "list": _cmd_cluster_list,
   347→        "update": _cmd_cluster_update,
   348→        "merge": _cmd_cluster_merge,
   349→    }
   350→    handler = dispatch.get(cluster_action)
   351→    if handler is None:
   352→        _cmd_cluster_list(args)
   353→        return
   354→    handler(args)
   355→
   356→
   357→__all__ = ["cmd_cluster_dispatch"]
   358→
```

```
1→"""Compact queue table renderer for ``plan queue``."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.guardrails import print_triage_guardrail_info
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize, print_table
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan import compute_new_finding_ids, load_plan
    12→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    13→
    14→
    15→def _truncate(text: str, width: int) -> str:
    16→    if len(text) <= width:
    17→        return text
    18→    return text[: width - 1] + "\u2026"
    19→
    20→
    21→def _resolve_plan_context(plan: dict, cluster_filter: str | None) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None]:
    22→    plan_data: dict | None = None
    23→    if plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("overrides") or plan.get("clusters"):
    24→        plan_data = plan
    25→
    26→    effective_cluster = cluster_filter
    27→    if plan_data and not cluster_filter:
    28→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
    29→        if active_cluster:
    30→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
    31→    return plan_data, effective_cluster
    32→
    33→
    34→def _print_queue_header(
    35→    *,
    36→    items: list[dict],
    37→    include_skipped: bool,
    38→    plan: dict,
    39→    plan_data: dict | None,
    40→    new_count: int = 0,
    41→) -> None:
    42→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
    43→        QueueBreakdown,
    44→        format_queue_headline,
    45→    )
    46→
    47→    total = len(items)
    48→    skipped_count = sum(1 for it in items if it.get("plan_skipped"))
    49→    non_skipped = total - skipped_count
    50→    plan_skipped_total = len(plan.get("skipped", {})) if plan_data else 0
    51→
    52→    # Count subjective items in the visible list
    53→    subjective = sum(
    54→        1 for it in items if it.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
    55→    )
    56→
    57→    # Count plan-ordered items (minus skipped)
    58→    plan_ordered = 0
    59→    if plan_data:
    60→        queue_order = plan_data.get("queue_order", [])
    61→        skipped_ids = set(plan_data.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    62→        plan_ordered = sum(1 for fid in queue_order if fid not in skipped_ids)
    63→
    64→    breakdown = QueueBreakdown(
    65→        queue_total=non_skipped,
    66→        plan_ordered=plan_ordered,
    67→        skipped=plan_skipped_total,
    68→        subjective=subjective,
    69→    )
    70→    headline = format_queue_headline(breakdown)
    71→    new_suffix = f"  ({new_count} new this scan)" if new_count > 0 else ""
    72→    print(colorize(f"\n  {headline}{new_suffix}", "bold"))
    73→
    74→    focus = plan.get("active_cluster") if plan_data else None
    75→    if focus:
    76→        print(colorize(f"  Focus: {focus}", "cyan"))
    77→
    78→    if include_skipped or skipped_count != 0:
    79→        return
    80→    if not plan_skipped_total:
    81→        return
    82→    print(
    83→        colorize(
    84→            f"  ({plan_skipped_total} skipped item{'s' if plan_skipped_total != 1 else ''}"
    85→            " hidden — use --include-skipped)",
    86→            "dim",
    87→        )
    88→    )
    89→
    90→
    91→def _queue_display_items(items: list[dict], *, top: int) -> list[dict]:
    92→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
    93→        return items[:top]
    94→    return items
    95→
    96→
    97→def _build_rows(display_items: list[dict], new_ids: set[str] | None = None) -> list[list[str]]:
    98→    rows: list[list[str]] = []
    99→    _new = new_ids or set()
   100→    for idx, item in enumerate(display_items, 1):
   101→        pos = str(idx)
   102→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   103→
   104→        if kind == "workflow_stage":
   105→            blocked = item.get("is_blocked", False)
   106→            blocked_tag = " [blocked]" if blocked else ""
   107→            conf_str = "—"
   108→            detector = "planning"
   109→            summary = f"[TRIAGE] {item.get('summary', '')}{blocked_tag}"
   110→            cluster_name = ""
   111→        elif kind == "workflow_action":
   112→            conf_str = "—"
   113→            detector = "workflow"
   114→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   115→            cluster_name = ""
   116→        elif kind == "cluster":
   117→            conf_str = item.get("confidence", "high")
   118→            member_count = item.get("member_count", 0)
   119→            detector = item.get("detector", "cluster")
   120→            new_in_cluster = sum(
   121→                1 for m in item.get("members", [])
   122→                if m.get("id") in _new
   123→            )
   124→            new_tag = f" (+{new_in_cluster} new)" if new_in_cluster else ""
   125→            summary = f"[{member_count} items{new_tag}] {item.get('summary', '')}"
   126→            cluster_name = item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id", ""))
   127→        else:
   128→            conf_str = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   129→            detector = item.get("detector", "")
   130→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   131→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   132→            cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "") if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else ""
   133→
   134→        prefix = "* " if item.get("id") in _new else ""
   135→        suffix = " [skip]" if item.get("plan_skipped") else ""
   136→        summary_display = _truncate(prefix + summary, 48) + suffix
   137→        rows.append([pos, conf_str, detector, summary_display, cluster_name])
   138→    return rows
   139→
   140→
   141→def cmd_plan_queue(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   142→    """Render a compact table of all upcoming queue items."""
   143→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   144→    state = runtime.state
   145→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   146→        return
   147→
   148→    top = getattr(args, "top", 30)
   149→    cluster_filter = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
   150→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
   151→
   152→    plan = load_plan()
   153→    print_triage_guardrail_info(plan=plan, state=state)
   154→    plan_data, effective_cluster = _resolve_plan_context(plan, cluster_filter)
   155→
   156→    queue = build_work_queue(
   157→        state,
   158→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   159→            count=None,
   160→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   161→            status="open",
   162→            include_subjective=True,
   163→            plan=plan_data,
   164→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   165→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   166→            collapse_clusters=True,
   167→        ),
   168→    )
   169→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   170→
   171→    sort_by = getattr(args, "sort", "priority")
   172→    all_new_ids: set[str] = queue.get("new_ids", set())
   173→    # Merge review-based new finding IDs (since last triage)
   174→    review_new_ids = compute_new_finding_ids(plan, state)
   175→    all_new_ids = all_new_ids | review_new_ids
   176→    item_ids = {it.get("id") for it in items}
   177→    new_ids = all_new_ids & item_ids
   178→
   179→    if sort_by == "recent":
   180→        items = sorted(items, key=lambda it: it.get("first_seen", ""), reverse=True)
   181→
   182→    _print_queue_header(
   183→        items=items,
   184→        include_skipped=include_skipped,
   185→        plan=plan,
   186→        plan_data=plan_data,
   187→        new_count=len(new_ids),
   188→    )
   189→
   190→    if not items:
   191→        print(colorize("\n  Queue is empty.", "green"))
   192→        return
   193→
   194→    # Determine which items to show
   195→    display_items = _queue_display_items(items, top=top)
   196→    headers = ["#", "Confidence", "Detector", "Summary", "Cluster"]
   197→    rows = _build_rows(display_items, new_ids=new_ids)
   198→
   199→    print()
   200→    widths = [4, 4, 12, 50, 16]
   201→    print_table(headers, rows, widths=widths)
   202→
   203→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
   204→        remaining = len(items) - top
   205→        print(colorize(
   206→            f"\n  ... and {remaining} more (use --top 0 to show all)", "dim"
   207→        ))
   208→    print()
   209→
   210→
   211→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_queue"]
   212→
```

```
1→"""Plan move subcommand handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.plan._resolve import resolve_ids_from_patterns
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan import append_log_entry, load_plan, move_items, save_plan
    12→
    13→
    14→def resolve_target(plan: dict, target: str | None, position: str) -> str | None:
    15→    """Resolve a cluster name used as a before/after target to a member ID."""
    16→    if target is None:
    17→        return None
    18→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    19→    if target not in clusters:
    20→        return target
    21→    member_ids = clusters[target].get("finding_ids", [])
    22→    if not member_ids:
    23→        return target
    24→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    25→    member_set = set(member_ids)
    26→    ordered = [fid for fid in order if fid in member_set]
    27→    if not ordered:
    28→        return member_ids[0]
    29→    return ordered[0] if position == "before" else ordered[-1]
    30→
    31→
    32→def cmd_plan_move(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    33→    """Move findings to a position in the queue."""
    34→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    35→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    36→        return
    37→
    38→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    39→    position: str = getattr(args, "position", "top")
    40→    target: str | None = getattr(args, "target", None)
    41→
    42→    if position in ("before", "after") and target is None:
    43→        print(colorize(f"  '{position}' requires --target (-t). Example: plan move <pat> {position} -t <id>", "red"))
    44→        return
    45→    if position in ("up", "down") and target is None:
    46→        print(colorize(f"  '{position}' requires --target (-t) with an integer offset. Example: plan move <pat> {position} -t 3", "red"))
    47→        return
    48→
    49→    plan = load_plan()
    50→
    51→    target = resolve_target(plan, target, position)
    52→
    53→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
    54→    if not finding_ids:
    55→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
    56→        return
    57→
    58→    offset: int | None = None
    59→    if position in ("up", "down") and target is not None:
    60→        try:
    61→            offset = int(target)
    62→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
    63→            print(colorize(f"  Invalid offset: {target}", "red"))
    64→            return
    65→        target = None
    66→
    67→    count = move_items(plan, finding_ids, position, target=target, offset=offset)
    68→    append_log_entry(
    69→        plan, "move", finding_ids=finding_ids, actor="user",
    70→        detail={"position": position, "target": target, "offset": offset},
    71→    )
    72→    save_plan(plan)
    73→    print(colorize(f"  Moved {count} item(s) to {position}.", "green"))
    74→
    75→
    76→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_move", "resolve_target"]
    77→
```

```
1→"""plan command: generate prioritized markdown plan from state.
     2→
     3→This module preserves the original import path. The full plan command
     4→(with subcommands) lives in ``desloppify.app.commands.plan.cmd``.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import argparse
    10→
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.rendering import print_agent_plan
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    14→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import warn_best_effort
    15→from desloppify.engine import planning as planning_mod
    16→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    17→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    18→from desloppify.core.tooling import check_config_staleness
    19→
    20→
    21→def cmd_plan_output(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    22→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state."""
    23→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    24→    state = runtime.state
    25→
    26→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    27→        return
    28→
    29→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(runtime.config)
    30→    if config_warning:
    31→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    32→
    33→    plan_md = planning_mod.generate_plan_md(state)
    34→    next_command = "desloppify next --count 20"
    35→
    36→    output = getattr(args, "output", None)
    37→    if output:
    38→        try:
    39→            safe_write_text(output, plan_md)
    40→            print(colorize(f"Plan written to {output}", "green"))
    41→            print_agent_plan(
    42→                ["Inspect and execute the generated plan."],
    43→                next_command=next_command,
    44→            )
    45→        except OSError as e:
    46→            warn_best_effort(f"Could not write plan to {output}: {e}")
    47→    else:
    48→        print(plan_md)
    49→        print()
    50→        print_agent_plan(
    51→            ["Start from the top-ranked action in this plan."],
    52→            next_command=next_command,
    53→        )
    54→
    55→
    56→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_output"]
    57→
```

```
1→"""Review preparation flow for `desloppify autofix review`."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path

     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod

    19→def _print_subjective_integrity_refresh_hints(state: dict) -> None:
    20→    unassessed_dims = subjective_integrity_mod.unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
    21→        state.get("dimension_scores", {})
    22→    )
    23→    scoped_findings = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
    24→        state.get("findings", {}),
    25→        state.get("scan_path"),
    26→    )
    27→    _coverage_total, _reasons, holistic_reasons = (
    28→        subjective_integrity_mod.subjective_review_open_breakdown(scoped_findings)
    29→    )
    30→    holistic_open = sum(holistic_reasons.values())
    31→    if not (unassessed_dims or holistic_open > 0):
    32→        return
    33→
    34→    print(colorize("  Subjective integrity still needs refresh.", "yellow"))
    35→    if unassessed_dims:
    36→        rendered = ", ".join(unassessed_dims[:3])
    37→        if len(unassessed_dims) > 3:
    38→            rendered = f"{rendered}, +{len(unassessed_dims) - 3} more"
    39→        print(colorize(f"    Unassessed (0% placeholder): {rendered}", "dim"))
    40→    if holistic_open > 0:
    41→        print(
    42→            colorize(
    43→                f"    Holistic stale/missing signals: {holistic_open}", "dim"
    44→            )
    45→        )
    46→    print(

    50→        )
    51→    )
    52→    print(
    53→        colorize(

    55→            "dim",

    57→    )

    66→def _print_dimension_prompts(data: dict) -> None:

    99→
   100→def _print_review_agent_plan() -> None:

   102→    print(colorize("\n  AGENT PLAN (run in order):", "yellow"))
```

```
    46→
    47→
    48→def cmd_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    49→    """Resolve finding(s) matching one or more patterns."""
    50→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)
    51→    _validate_resolve_inputs(args, attestation)
    52→
    53→    if args.status == "fixed":
    54→        note = getattr(args, "note", None)
    55→        if not validate_note_length(note):
    56→            show_note_length_requirement(note)
    57→            return
    58→
    59→    state_file = state_path(args)
    60→    state = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
    61→
    62→    if _check_queue_order_guard(state, args.patterns, args.status):
    63→        return
    64→
    65→    if args.status == "fixed":
    66→        require_triage_current_or_exit(
    67→            state=state,
    68→            bypass=bool(getattr(args, "force_resolve", False)),
    69→            attest=getattr(args, "attest", "") or "",
    70→        )
    71→
    72→    _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation(
    73→        state,
    74→        args,
    75→        attestation=attestation,
    76→        resolve_all_patterns_fn=_resolve_all_patterns,
    77→    )
    78→    prev = _previous_score_snapshot(state)
    79→    prev_subjective_scores = {
    80→        str(dim): (coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0)
    81→        for dim, payload in (state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}).items()
    82→        if isinstance(dim, str)
    83→    }
    84→
    85→    all_resolved = _resolve_all_patterns(state, args, attestation=attestation)
    86→    if not all_resolved:
    87→        status_label = "resolved" if args.status == "open" else "open"
    88→        print(colorize(f"No {status_label} findings matching: {' '.join(args.patterns)}", "yellow"))
    89→        return
    90→
    91→    _save_state_or_exit(state, state_file)
    92→
    93→    plan = None
    94→    try:
    95→        if has_living_plan():
    96→            plan = load_plan()
    97→            purged = purge_ids(plan, all_resolved)
    98→            append_log_entry(
    99→                plan,
   100→                "resolve",
   101→                finding_ids=all_resolved,
   102→                actor="user",
   103→                note=getattr(args, "note", None),
   104→                detail={"status": args.status, "attestation": attestation},
   105→            )
   106→            # Commit tracking: add to uncommitted on fix, remove on reopen
   107→            if args.status == "fixed":
   108→                add_uncommitted_findings(plan, all_resolved)
   109→            elif args.status == "open":
   110→                purge_uncommitted_ids(plan, all_resolved)
   111→            if purged:
   112→                save_plan(plan)
   113→                print(colorize(f"  Plan updated: {purged} item(s) removed from queue.", "dim"))
   114→            else:
   115→                save_plan(plan)
   116→    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
   117→        print(colorize("  Warning: could not update living plan.", "yellow"), file=sys.stderr)
   118→
   119→    _print_resolve_summary(status=args.status, all_resolved=all_resolved)
   120→    _print_wontfix_batch_warning(
   121→        state,
   122→        status=args.status,
   123→        resolved_count=len(all_resolved),
   124→    )
   125→    show_score_with_plan_context(state, prev)
   126→    render_commit_guidance(state, plan, all_resolved, args.status)
   127→    _print_subjective_reset_hint(
   128→        args=args,
   129→        state=state,
   130→        all_resolved=all_resolved,
   131→        prev_subjective_scores=prev_subjective_scores,
   132→    )
   133→
   134→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   135→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   136→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   137→        state,
   138→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="resolve"),
   139→    )
   140→    if narrative.get("milestone"):
   141→        print(colorize(f"  → {narrative['milestone']}", "green"))
   142→
   143→    next_command = _print_next_command(state)
   144→    _write_resolve_query_entry(
   145→        ResolveQueryContext(
   146→            patterns=args.patterns,
   147→            status=args.status,
   148→            resolved=all_resolved,
   149→            next_command=next_command,
   150→            prev_overall=prev.overall,
   151→            prev_objective=prev.objective,
   152→            prev_strict=prev.strict,
   153→            prev_verified=prev.verified,
   154→            attestation=attestation,
   155→            narrative=narrative,
   156→            state=state,
   157→        )
   158→    )
   159→

   161→__all__ = ["_check_queue_order_guard", "cmd_ignore_pattern", "cmd_resolve"]
```

```
1→"""State-application helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query_best_effort as _write_query_best_effort
     9→from desloppify.core.output_contract import OutputResult
    10→from desloppify.engine.plan import has_living_plan, load_plan
    11→
    12→from .selection import ResolveQueryContext
    13→
    14→
    15→def _try_expand_cluster(pattern: str) -> list[str] | None:
    16→    """If pattern matches a cluster name, return its finding IDs."""
    17→    try:
    18→        if not has_living_plan():
    19→            return None
    20→        plan = load_plan()
    21→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(pattern)
    22→        if cluster and cluster.get("finding_ids"):
    23→            return list(cluster["finding_ids"])
    24→    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError) as exc:
    25→        _ = exc
    26→    return None
    27→
    28→
    29→def _resolve_all_patterns(
    30→    state: dict,
    31→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    32→    *,
    33→    attestation: str | None,
    34→) -> list[str]:
    35→    all_resolved: list[str] = []
    36→    for pattern in args.patterns:
    37→        # Check if pattern is a cluster name — expand to member IDs
    38→        cluster_ids = _try_expand_cluster(pattern)
    39→        if cluster_ids:
    40→            for fid in cluster_ids:
    41→                resolved = state_mod.resolve_findings(
    42→                    state,
    43→                    fid,
    44→                    args.status,
    45→                    args.note,
    46→                    attestation=attestation,
    47→                )
    48→                all_resolved.extend(resolved)
    49→            continue
    50→
    51→        resolved = state_mod.resolve_findings(
    52→            state,
    53→            pattern,
    54→            args.status,
    55→            args.note,
    56→            attestation=attestation,
    57→        )
    58→        all_resolved.extend(resolved)
    59→    return all_resolved
    60→
    61→
    62→def write_query(payload: dict) -> OutputResult:
    63→    """Backward-compatible resolve query writer seam."""
    64→    return _write_query_best_effort(
    65→        payload,
    66→        context="resolve query payload update",
    67→    )
    68→
    69→
    70→def _write_resolve_query_entry(context: ResolveQueryContext) -> None:
    71→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(context.state)
    72→    write_query(
    73→        {
    74→            "command": "resolve",
    75→            "patterns": context.patterns,
    76→            "status": context.status,
    77→            "resolved": context.resolved,
    78→            "count": len(context.resolved),
    79→            "next_command": context.next_command,
    80→            "overall_score": scores.overall,
    81→            "objective_score": scores.objective,
    82→            "strict_score": scores.strict,
    83→            "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,
    84→            "prev_overall_score": context.prev_overall,
    85→            "prev_objective_score": context.prev_objective,
    86→            "prev_strict_score": context.prev_strict,
    87→            "prev_verified_strict_score": context.prev_verified,
    88→            "attestation": context.attestation,
    89→            "narrative": context.narrative,
    90→        }
    91→    )
    92→
```

```
1→"""Shared persistence helpers for resolve command handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     6→from desloppify.core import config as config_mod
     7→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import CommandError
     8→
     9→
    10→def _save_state_or_exit(state: dict, state_file: str) -> None:
    11→    """Persist state with a consistent CLI error boundary."""
    12→    try:
    13→        state_mod.save_state(state, state_file)
    14→    except OSError as exc:
    15→        raise CommandError(f"could not save state: {exc}") from exc
    16→
    17→
    18→def _save_config_or_exit(config: dict) -> None:
    19→    """Persist config with a consistent CLI error boundary."""
    20→    try:
    21→        config_mod.save_config(config)
    22→    except OSError as exc:
    23→        raise CommandError(f"could not save config: {exc}") from exc
    24→
    25→
    26→__all__ = ["_save_config_or_exit", "_save_state_or_exit"]
    27→
```

```
1→"""CLI handlers for ``plan commit-log`` subcommand family."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import sys
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     9→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    10→    append_log_entry,
    11→    commit_tracking_summary,
    12→    filter_finding_ids_by_pattern,
    13→    generate_pr_body,
    14→    get_uncommitted_findings,
    15→    load_plan,
    16→    record_commit,
    17→    save_plan,
    18→)
    19→
    20→
    21→def _cmd_commit_log_status(plan: dict) -> None:
    22→    """Show full commit-log status: uncommitted, committed, git context."""
    23→    from desloppify.core.git_context import detect_git_context
    24→
    25→    git = detect_git_context()
    26→    summary = commit_tracking_summary(plan)
    27→
    28→    print(colorize("  Commit Tracking Status", "bold"))
    29→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
    30→
    31→    if git.available:
    32→        print(f"  Branch:  {git.branch or '(detached)'}")
    33→        print(f"  HEAD:    {git.head_sha or '?'}")
    34→        if git.has_uncommitted:
    35→            print(colorize("  Working tree has uncommitted changes", "yellow"))
    36→    else:
    37→        print(colorize("  Git: not available", "dim"))
    38→
    39→    from desloppify.core.config import load_config
    40→
    41→    config = load_config()
    42→    pr = config.get("commit_pr", 0)
    43→    if pr:
    44→        print(f"  PR:      #{pr}")
    45→
    46→    uncommitted = get_uncommitted_findings(plan)
    47→    print(f"\n  Uncommitted:  {summary['uncommitted']} finding(s)")
    48→    for fid in uncommitted[:10]:
    49→        print(f"    {fid}")
    50→    if len(uncommitted) > 10:
    51→        print(f"    ... and {len(uncommitted) - 10} more")
    52→
    53→    commit_log = plan.get("commit_log", [])
    54→    committed_count = sum(len(r.get("finding_ids", [])) for r in commit_log)
    55→    print(f"  Committed:    {committed_count} finding(s) in {len(commit_log)} commit(s)")
    56→
    57→    if not uncommitted and not commit_log:
    58→        print(colorize("\n  No commit tracking data yet.", "dim"))
    59→        print(colorize("  Resolve findings with `desloppify resolve` or `desloppify plan done` to start.", "dim"))
    60→
    61→
    62→def _cmd_commit_log_record(args: argparse.Namespace, plan: dict) -> None:
    63→    """Record a commit: capture HEAD, move uncommitted → committed, update PR."""
    64→    from desloppify.core.git_context import detect_git_context, update_pr_body
    65→
    66→    sha = getattr(args, "sha", None)
    67→    branch = getattr(args, "branch", None)
    68→    note = getattr(args, "note", None)
    69→    only_patterns: list[str] | None = getattr(args, "only", None)
    70→
    71→    # Auto-detect from git if not overridden
    72→    if not sha or not branch:
    73→        git = detect_git_context()
    74→        if git.available:
    75→            sha = sha or git.head_sha
    76→            branch = branch or git.branch
    77→        elif not sha:
    78→            print(colorize("  Cannot detect HEAD. Use --sha to specify.", "red"))
    79→            return
    80→
    81→    # Determine which findings to record
    82→    uncommitted = get_uncommitted_findings(plan)
    83→    if not uncommitted:
    84→        print(colorize("  No uncommitted findings to record.", "yellow"))
    85→        return
    86→
    87→    if only_patterns:
    88→        finding_ids = filter_finding_ids_by_pattern(uncommitted, only_patterns)
    89→        if not finding_ids:
    90→            print(colorize("  No uncommitted findings match --only patterns.", "yellow"))
    91→            return
    92→    else:
    93→        finding_ids = None  # record all
    94→
    95→    record = record_commit(
    96→        plan,
    97→        sha=sha,
    98→        branch=branch,
    99→        finding_ids=finding_ids,
   100→        note=note,
   101→    )
   102→    append_log_entry(
   103→        plan,
   104→        "commit_record",
   105→        finding_ids=record["finding_ids"],
   106→        actor="user",
   107→        note=note,
   108→        detail={"sha": sha, "branch": branch},
   109→    )
   110→    save_plan(plan)
   111→
   112→    recorded = len(record["finding_ids"])
   113→    print(colorize(f"  Recorded commit {sha} with {recorded} finding(s).", "green"))
   114→    if note:
   115→        print(colorize(f"  Note: {note}", "dim"))
   116→
   117→    # Update PR if configured
   118→    from desloppify.core.config import load_config
   119→
   120→    config = load_config()
   121→    pr_number = config.get("commit_pr", 0)
   122→    if pr_number:
   123→        try:
   124→            from desloppify import state as state_mod
   125→
   126→            state = state_mod.load_state()
   127→            body = generate_pr_body(plan, state)
   128→            ok = update_pr_body(pr_number, body)
   129→            if ok:
   130→                print(colorize(f"  PR #{pr_number} description updated.", "green"))
   131→            else:
   132→                print(colorize(f"  Could not update PR #{pr_number} (gh may not be available).", "yellow"))
   133→        except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError) as exc:
   134→            print(
   135→                colorize(f"  Warning: PR update skipped ({exc}).", "yellow"),
   136→                file=sys.stderr,
   137→            )
   138→
   139→
   140→def _cmd_commit_log_history(args: argparse.Namespace, plan: dict) -> None:
   141→    """Show commit records."""
   142→    top = getattr(args, "top", 10)
   143→    commit_log = plan.get("commit_log", [])
   144→
   145→    if not commit_log:
   146→        print(colorize("  No commits recorded yet.", "dim"))
   147→        return
   148→
   149→    print(colorize("  Commit History", "bold"))
   150→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
   151→
   152→    shown = commit_log[-top:] if top else commit_log
   153→    for record in reversed(shown):
   154→        sha = record.get("sha", "?")[:7]
   155→        branch = record.get("branch", "")
   156→        finding_ids = record.get("finding_ids", [])
   157→        note = record.get("note", "")
   158→        recorded_at = record.get("recorded_at", "")
   159→
   160→        header = f"  {sha}"
   161→        if branch:
   162→            header += f" ({branch})"
   163→        header += f" — {len(finding_ids)} finding(s)"
   164→        if recorded_at:
   165→            header += f"  [{recorded_at[:16]}]"
   166→        print(header)
   167→        if note:
   168→            print(colorize(f"    Note: {note}", "dim"))
   169→        for fid in finding_ids[:5]:
   170→            print(f"    - {fid}")
   171→        if len(finding_ids) > 5:
   172→            print(f"    ... and {len(finding_ids) - 5} more")
   173→
   174→
   175→def _cmd_commit_log_pr(plan: dict) -> None:
   176→    """Print PR body markdown to stdout (dry run)."""
   177→    try:
   178→        from desloppify import state as state_mod
   179→
   180→        state = state_mod.load_state()
   181→    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
   182→        state = {"findings": {}}
   183→
   184→    body = generate_pr_body(plan, state)
   185→    print(body)
   186→
   187→
   188→def cmd_commit_log_dispatch(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   189→    """Route commit-log subcommands."""
   190→    from desloppify.core.config import load_config
   191→
   192→    config = load_config()
   193→    if not config.get("commit_tracking_enabled", True):
   194→        print(colorize("  Commit tracking is disabled. Enable with: desloppify config set commit_tracking_enabled true", "yellow"))
   195→        return
   196→
   197→    plan = load_plan()
   198→    action = getattr(args, "commit_log_action", None)
   199→
   200→    if action == "record":
   201→        _cmd_commit_log_record(args, plan)
   202→    elif action == "history":
   203→        _cmd_commit_log_history(args, plan)
   204→    elif action == "pr":
   205→        _cmd_commit_log_pr(plan)
   206→    else:
   207→        _cmd_commit_log_status(plan)
   208→
   209→
   210→__all__ = ["cmd_commit_log_dispatch"]
   211→
```

```
    76→def _clear_needs_rescan_flag(config: dict[str, object]) -> None:
    77→    """Best-effort clear for config.needs_rescan after a successful scan merge."""

    79→        return
    80→    try:
    81→        config["needs_rescan"] = False
    82→        _save_config(config)
    83→    except OSError as exc:
    84→        _ = exc
    85→        config["needs_rescan"] = True

    88→def _plan_has_user_content(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
    89→    """Return True when the living plan has any user-managed queue metadata."""
    90→    return bool(
    91→        plan.get("queue_order")
    92→        or plan.get("overrides")
    93→        or plan.get("clusters")
    94→        or plan.get("skipped")
    95→    )

    98→def _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, reconcile_fn) -> bool:
    99→    """Apply standard post-scan plan reconciliation when user content exists."""
   100→    if not _plan_has_user_content(plan):
   101→        return False
   102→    recon = reconcile_fn(plan, state)
   103→    if recon.resurfaced:
   104→        print(
   105→            colorize(
   106→                f"  Plan: {len(recon.resurfaced)} skipped item(s) re-surfaced after review period.",
   107→                "cyan",
   108→            )
   109→        )
   110→    return bool(recon.changes)
   111→

   163→def _seed_plan_start_scores(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   164→    """Set plan_start_scores when beginning a new queue cycle."""
   165→    existing = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   166→    if existing and not isinstance(existing, dict):
   167→        return False
   168→    # Seed when empty OR when it's the reset sentinel ({"reset": True})
   169→    if existing and not existing.get("reset"):
   170→        return False
   171→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   172→    if scores.strict is None:
   173→        return False
   174→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
   175→        "strict": scores.strict,
   176→        "overall": scores.overall,
   177→        "objective": scores.objective,
   178→        "verified": scores.verified,
   179→    }
   180→    return True
   181→
   182→
   183→def _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(
   184→    state: state_mod.StateModel, plan: dict[str, object]
   185→) -> bool:
   186→    """Clear plan-start score snapshot once the queue is fully drained."""
   187→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
   188→        return False
   189→
   190→    try:
   191→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import plan_aware_queue_count
   192→
   193→        queue_empty = plan_aware_queue_count(state, plan) == 0
   194→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   195→        logging.debug("Plan operation skipped: %s", exc)
   196→        return False
   197→    if not queue_empty:
   198→        return False
   199→    state["_plan_start_scores_for_reveal"] = dict(plan["plan_start_scores"])
   200→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
   201→    return True

   204→def _reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: "ScanRuntime") -> None:
   205→    """Reconcile plan queue metadata and stale subjective review dimensions."""
   206→    try:
   207→        plan_path = runtime.state_path.parent / "plan.json" if runtime.state_path else None
   208→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   209→        dirty = False
   210→
   211→        if _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan, runtime.state, reconcile_plan_after_scan):
   212→            dirty = True
   213→
   214→        unscored_changed = _sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, runtime.state, sync_unscored_dimensions)
   215→        if unscored_changed:
   216→            dirty = True
   217→            append_log_entry(plan, "sync_unscored", actor="system",
   218→                             detail={"changes": True})
   219→
   220→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score import target_strict_score_from_config
   221→        _target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   222→
   223→        # Compute subjective visibility policy once for consistent gating
   224→        from desloppify.engine.plan import compute_subjective_visibility
   225→        _policy = compute_subjective_visibility(runtime.state, target_strict=_target_strict)
   226→
   227→        # Detect cycle completion: plan_start_scores is empty when the
   228→        # previous cycle drained the queue and revealed scores.  In that
   229→        # case stale subjective dimensions should be prioritized over new
   230→        # objective findings so the user reviews before a new cycle begins.
   231→        _cycle_just_completed = not plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   232→
   233→        stale_changed = _sync_stale_dimensions(
   234→            plan, runtime.state,
   235→            lambda p, s: sync_stale_dimensions(
   236→                p, s, policy=_policy, cycle_just_completed=_cycle_just_completed,
   237→            ),
   238→        )
   239→        if stale_changed:
   240→            dirty = True
   241→            append_log_entry(plan, "sync_stale", actor="system",
   242→                             detail={"changes": True})
   243→
   244→        auto_changed = _sync_auto_clusters(
   245→            plan, runtime.state, target_strict=_target_strict, policy=_policy,
   246→            cycle_just_completed=_cycle_just_completed,
   247→        )
   248→        if auto_changed:
   249→            dirty = True
   250→            append_log_entry(plan, "auto_cluster", actor="system",
   251→                             detail={"changes": True})
   252→
   253→        triage_sync = sync_triage_needed(plan, runtime.state)
   254→        if triage_sync.changes:
   255→            dirty = True
   256→            if triage_sync.injected:
   257→                print(
   258→                    colorize(
   259→                        "  Plan: planning mode needed — review findings changed since last triage.",
   260→                        "cyan",
   261→                    )
   262→                )
   263→                append_log_entry(plan, "sync_triage", actor="system",
   264→                                 detail={"injected": True})
   265→
   266→        checkpoint_sync = sync_score_checkpoint_needed(plan, runtime.state, policy=_policy)
   267→        if checkpoint_sync.changes:
   268→            dirty = True
   269→            append_log_entry(plan, "sync_score_checkpoint", actor="system",
   270→                             detail={"injected": True})
   271→
   272→        create_plan_sync = sync_create_plan_needed(plan, runtime.state, policy=_policy)
   273→        if create_plan_sync.changes:
   274→            dirty = True
   275→            if create_plan_sync.injected:
   276→                print(
   277→                    colorize(
   278→                        "  Plan: reviews complete — `workflow::create-plan` queued.",
   279→                        "cyan",
   280→                    )
   281→                )
   282→                append_log_entry(plan, "sync_create_plan", actor="system",
   283→                                 detail={"injected": True})
   284→
   285→        seeded = _seed_plan_start_scores(plan, runtime.state)
   286→        if seeded:
   287→            dirty = True
   288→            append_log_entry(plan, "seed_start_scores", actor="system",
   289→                             detail={})
   290→        # Only clear scores that existed before this reconcile pass —
   291→        # never clear scores we just seeded in the same scan.
   292→        if not seeded and _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(runtime.state, plan):
   293→            dirty = True
   294→            append_log_entry(plan, "clear_start_scores", actor="system",
   295→                             detail={})
   296→
   297→        if dirty:
   298→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   299→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   300→        logging.debug("Plan operation skipped: %s", exc)

   303→def _state_subjective_assessments(
   304→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   305→) -> dict[str, object]:
   306→    """Return normalized subjective assessment store from state."""
   307→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
   308→    if isinstance(assessments, dict):
   309→        return assessments
   310→    raise ScanStateContractError(
   311→        "state.subjective_assessments must be an object; rerun with a valid state file"
   312→    )
   313→
   314→
   315→def _state_lang_capabilities(
   316→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   317→) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
   318→    """Return normalized language capability map from state."""
   319→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities")
   320→    if capabilities is None:
   321→        normalized: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {}
   322→        state["lang_capabilities"] = normalized
   323→        return normalized
   324→    if isinstance(capabilities, dict):
   325→        return capabilities
   326→    raise ScanStateContractError(
   327→        "state.lang_capabilities must be an object when present"
   328→    )
   329→
   330→
   331→def _state_findings(state: state_mod.StateModel) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
   332→    """Return normalized finding map from state."""
   333→    findings = state.get("findings")
   334→    if isinstance(findings, dict):
   335→        return findings
   336→    raise ScanStateContractError(
   337→        "state.findings must be an object; state file appears corrupted"

   341→def _subjective_reset_dimensions(*, lang_name: str | None = None) -> tuple[str, ...]:

   346→@dataclass
   347→class ScanRuntime:

   349→

   380→
```
