   387→
   388→def _configure_lang_runtime(
   389→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   390→    config: dict[str, object],
   391→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   392→    lang: LangRun | None,
   393→) -> LangRun | None:
   394→    """Populate runtime context and threshold overrides for a selected language."""
   395→    if not lang:
   396→        return None
   397→
   398→    lang_options = resolve_lang_runtime_options(args, lang)
   399→    lang_settings = resolve_lang_settings(config, lang)
   400→    runtime_lang = make_lang_run(
   401→        lang,
   402→        overrides=LangRunOverrides(
   403→            review_cache=state.get("review_cache", {}),
   404→            review_max_age_days=config.get("review_max_age_days", 30),
   405→            runtime_settings=lang_settings,
   406→            runtime_options=lang_options,
   407→            large_threshold_override=config.get("large_files_threshold", 0),
   408→            props_threshold_override=config.get("props_threshold", 0),
   409→        ),
   410→    )
   411→
   412→    lang_capabilities = _state_lang_capabilities(state)
   413→    lang_capabilities[runtime_lang.name] = {
   414→        "fixers": sorted(runtime_lang.fixers.keys()),
   415→        "typecheck_cmd": runtime_lang.typecheck_cmd,
   416→    }
   417→    return runtime_lang
   418→
   419→
   420→def _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   421→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   422→    *,
   423→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   424→) -> int:
   425→    """Reset known subjective dimensions to 0 so the next scan starts fresh."""
   426→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   427→
   428→    reset_keys = {
   429→        key.strip()
   430→        for key in assessments
   431→        if isinstance(key, str) and key.strip()
   432→    }
   433→    reset_keys.update(_subjective_reset_dimensions(lang_name=lang_name))
   434→
   435→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   436→    for key in sorted(reset_keys):
   437→        payload = assessments.get(key)
   438→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
   439→            payload["score"] = 0.0
   440→            payload["source"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   441→            payload["assessed_at"] = now
   442→            payload["reset_by"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   443→            payload["placeholder"] = True
   444→            payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   445→            payload.pop("components", None)
   446→            payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   447→            continue
   448→        assessments[key] = {
   449→            "score": 0.0,
   450→            "source": "scan_reset_subjective",
   451→            "assessed_at": now,
   452→            "reset_by": "scan_reset_subjective",
   453→            "placeholder": True,
   454→        }
   455→    return len(reset_keys)
   456→
   457→
   458→def _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   459→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   460→) -> int:
   461→    """Expire provisional manual-override assessments at scan start."""
   462→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   463→
   464→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   465→    expired = 0
   466→    for payload in assessments.values():
   467→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   468→            continue
   469→        if payload.get("provisional_override") is not True:
   470→            continue
   471→        payload["score"] = 0.0
   472→        payload["source"] = "manual_override_expired"
   473→        payload["assessed_at"] = now
   474→        payload["reset_by"] = "manual_override_expired"
   475→        payload["placeholder"] = True
   476→        payload.pop("provisional_override", None)
   477→        payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)
   478→        payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   479→        payload.pop("components", None)
   480→        payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   481→        expired += 1
   482→    return expired
   483→
   484→
   485→def prepare_scan_runtime(args) -> ScanRuntime:
   486→    """Resolve state/config/language and apply scan-time runtime settings."""
   487→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   488→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   489→    state = runtime.state if isinstance(runtime.state, dict) else {}
   490→    state_mod.ensure_state_defaults(state)
   491→    path = Path(args.path)
   492→    config = runtime.config if isinstance(runtime.config, dict) else {}
   493→    lang_config = resolve_lang(args)
   494→    reset_subjective_count = 0
   495→    expired_manual_override_count = _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   496→        state
   497→    )
   498→    if getattr(args, "reset_subjective", False):
   499→        reset_subjective_count = _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   500→            state,
   501→            lang_name=getattr(lang_config, "name", None),
   502→        )
   503→
   504→    include_slow = not getattr(args, "skip_slow", False)
   505→    profile = _resolve_scan_profile(getattr(args, "profile", None), lang_config)
   506→    effective_include_slow = _effective_include_slow(include_slow, profile)
   507→
   508→    lang = _configure_lang_runtime(args, config, state, lang_config)
   509→    coverage_warnings = _seed_runtime_coverage_warnings(lang)
   510→    zone_overrides_raw = config.get("zone_overrides")
   511→    zone_overrides = zone_overrides_raw if isinstance(zone_overrides_raw, dict) else None
   512→
   513→    return ScanRuntime(
   514→        args=args,
   515→        state_path=state_file,
   516→        state=state,
   517→        path=path,
   518→        config=config,
   519→        lang=lang,
   520→        lang_label=f" ({lang.name})" if lang else "",
   521→        profile=profile,
   522→        effective_include_slow=effective_include_slow,
   523→        zone_overrides=zone_overrides,
   524→        reset_subjective_count=reset_subjective_count,
   525→        expired_manual_override_count=expired_manual_override_count,
   526→        coverage_warnings=coverage_warnings,
   527→    )
   528→
   529→
   530→def _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(
   531→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   532→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   533→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   534→    """Append stale exclude findings when excluded dirs are unreferenced."""
   535→    extra_exclusions = get_exclusions()
   536→    if not (extra_exclusions and runtime.lang and runtime.lang.file_finder):
   537→        return findings
   538→
   539→    scanned_files = runtime.lang.file_finder(runtime.path)
   540→    stale = _audit_excluded_dirs(
   541→        extra_exclusions, scanned_files, PROJECT_ROOT
   542→    )
   543→    if not stale:
   544→        return findings
   545→
   546→    augmented = list(findings)
   547→    augmented.extend(stale)
   548→    for stale_finding in stale:
   549→        print(colorize(f"  ℹ {stale_finding['summary']}", "dim"))
   550→    return augmented
   551→
   552→
   553→def _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   554→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   555→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   556→    *,
   557→    decay_scans: int,
   558→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], int]:
   559→    """Append re-triage findings for stale/worsening wontfix debt."""
   560→    return _augment_stale_wontfix_impl(
   561→        findings,
   562→        state=runtime.state,
   563→        scan_path=runtime.path,
   564→        project_root=PROJECT_ROOT,
   565→        decay_scans=decay_scans,
   566→    )
   567→
   568→
   569→def run_scan_generation(
   570→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   571→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, object], dict[str, object] | None]:
   572→    """Run detector pipeline and return findings, potentials, and codebase metrics."""
   573→    enable_file_cache()
   574→    enable_parse_cache()
   575→    try:
   576→        findings, potentials = plan_mod.generate_findings(
   577→            runtime.path,
   578→            lang=runtime.lang,
   579→            options=PlanScanOptions(
   580→                include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   581→                zone_overrides=runtime.zone_overrides,
   582→                profile=runtime.profile,
   583→            ),
   584→        )
   585→    finally:
   586→        disable_parse_cache()
   587→        disable_file_cache()
   588→
   589→    codebase_metrics = _collect_codebase_metrics(runtime.lang, runtime.path)
   590→    _warn_explicit_lang_with_no_files(
   591→        runtime.args, runtime.lang, runtime.path, codebase_metrics
   592→    )
   593→    findings = _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(findings, runtime)
   594→    decay_scans = _coerce_int(
   595→        runtime.config.get("wontfix_decay_scans"),
   596→        default=_WONTFIX_DECAY_SCANS_DEFAULT,
   597→    )
   598→    findings, monitored_wontfix = _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   599→        findings,
   600→        runtime,
   601→        decay_scans=max(decay_scans, 0),
   602→    )
   603→    potentials["stale_wontfix"] = monitored_wontfix
   604→    return findings, potentials, codebase_metrics
   605→
   606→
   607→def merge_scan_results(
   608→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   609→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   610→    potentials: dict[str, object],
   611→    codebase_metrics: dict[str, object] | None,
   612→) -> ScanMergeResult:
   613→    """Merge findings into persistent state and return diff + previous score snapshot."""
   614→    scan_path_rel = rel(str(runtime.path))
   615→    prev_scan_path = runtime.state.get("scan_path")
   616→    path_changed = prev_scan_path is not None and prev_scan_path != scan_path_rel
   617→
   618→    if not path_changed:
   619→        prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(runtime.state)
   620→    else:
   621→        prev = state_mod.ScoreSnapshot(None, None, None, None)
   622→    prev_dim_scores = (
   623→        runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {}) if not path_changed else {}
   624→    )
   625→
   626→    if runtime.lang and runtime.lang.zone_map is not None:
   627→        runtime.state["zone_distribution"] = runtime.lang.zone_map.counts()
   628→    _persist_scan_coverage(runtime.state, runtime.lang)
   629→
   630→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   631→
   632→    diff = state_mod.merge_scan(
   633→        runtime.state,
   634→        findings,
   635→        options=state_mod.MergeScanOptions(
   636→            lang=runtime.lang.name if runtime.lang else None,
   637→            scan_path=scan_path_rel,
   638→            force_resolve=getattr(runtime.args, "force_resolve", False),
   639→            exclude=get_exclusions(),
   640→            potentials=potentials,
   641→            codebase_metrics=codebase_metrics,
   642→            include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   643→            ignore=runtime.config.get("ignore", []),
   644→            subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   645→        ),
   646→    )
   647→
   648→    issues_mod.expire_stale_holistic(
   649→        runtime.state, runtime.config.get("holistic_max_age_days", 30)
   650→    )
   651→    state_mod.save_state(
   652→        runtime.state,
   653→        runtime.state_path,
   654→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   655→    )
   656→
   657→    _clear_needs_rescan_flag(runtime.config)
   658→    _reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime)
   659→
   660→    return ScanMergeResult(
   661→        diff=diff,
   662→        prev_overall=prev.overall,
   663→        prev_objective=prev.objective,
   664→        prev_strict=prev.strict,
   665→        prev_verified=prev.verified,
   666→        prev_dim_scores=prev_dim_scores,
   667→    )
   668→
   669→
   670→def resolve_noise_snapshot(
   671→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   672→    config: dict[str, object],
   673→) -> ScanNoiseSnapshot:
   674→    """Resolve noise budget settings and hidden finding counters."""
   675→    noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning = (
   676→        state_mod.resolve_finding_noise_settings(config)
   677→    )
   678→    findings_by_id = _state_findings(state)
   679→    open_findings = [
   680→        finding
   681→        for finding in state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   682→            findings_by_id, state.get("scan_path")
   683→        ).values()
   684→        if finding.get("status") == "open"
   685→    ]
   686→    _, hidden_by_detector = state_mod.apply_finding_noise_budget(
   687→        open_findings,
   688→        budget=noise_budget,
   689→        global_budget=global_noise_budget,
   690→    )
   691→
   692→    return ScanNoiseSnapshot(
   693→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   694→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   695→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   696→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   697→        hidden_total=sum(hidden_by_detector.values()),
   698→    )
   699→
   700→
   701→def persist_reminder_history(
   702→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   703→    narrative: dict[str, object],
   704→) -> None:
   705→    """Persist reminder history emitted by narrative computation."""
   706→    if not (narrative and "reminder_history" in narrative):
   707→        return
   708→
   709→    runtime.state["reminder_history"] = narrative["reminder_history"]
   710→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   711→    state_mod.save_state(
   712→        runtime.state,
   713→        runtime.state_path,
   714→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   715→    )
   716→
   717→
   718→__all__ = [
   719→    "ScanStateContractError",
   720→    "ScanMergeResult",
   721→    "ScanNoiseSnapshot",
   722→    "ScanRuntime",
   723→    "merge_scan_results",
   724→    "persist_reminder_history",
   725→    "prepare_scan_runtime",
   726→    "resolve_noise_snapshot",
   727→    "run_scan_generation",
   728→]
   729→

1→"""Scan lifecycle orchestration service.
     2→
     3→Keeps scan command flow explicit while delegating stage implementations to
     4→scan_workflow helpers.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations

    13→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_workflow import (

    24→@dataclass
    25→class ScanOrchestrator:
    26→    """Compose scan lifecycle stages around a resolved ScanRuntime."""
    27→
    28→    runtime: ScanRuntime
    29→    run_scan_generation_fn: Callable[
    30→        [ScanRuntime],

    41→    persist_reminder_history_fn: Callable[
    42→        [ScanRuntime, dict[str, object]],

    44→    ] = persist_reminder_history
    45→
    46→    def generate(
    47→        self,
    48→    ) -> tuple[list[dict[str, object]], dict[str, object], dict[str, object] | None]:
    49→        """Run detector generation and lifecycle augmenters."""
    50→        return self.run_scan_generation_fn(self.runtime)
    51→
    52→    def merge(
    53→        self,
    54→        findings: list[dict[str, object]],
    55→        potentials: dict[str, object],
    56→        codebase_metrics: dict[str, object] | None,
    57→    ) -> ScanMergeResult:
    58→        """Merge generated findings and persist scan state updates."""
    59→        return self.merge_scan_results_fn(
    60→            self.runtime,
    61→            findings,
    62→            potentials,
    63→            codebase_metrics,
    64→        )
    65→
    66→    def noise_snapshot(self) -> ScanNoiseSnapshot:
    67→        """Resolve effective noise budgets for display/query payloads."""
    68→        return self.resolve_noise_snapshot_fn(self.runtime.state, self.runtime.config)
    69→
    70→    def persist_reminders(self, narrative: dict[str, object]) -> None:
    71→        """Persist reminder history emitted by post-scan narrative analysis."""
    72→        self.persist_reminder_history_fn(self.runtime, narrative)
    73→
    74→
    75→__all__ = ["ScanOrchestrator"]

1→"""Queue-order enforcement helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     6→from desloppify.engine.plan import has_living_plan, load_plan
     7→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
     8→
     9→
    10→def _check_queue_order_guard(
    11→    state: dict,
    12→    patterns: list[str],
    13→    status: str,
    14→) -> bool:
    15→    """Warn and block if resolving items not at the front of the plan queue."""
    16→    if status != "fixed":
    17→        return False
    18→    try:
    19→        if not has_living_plan():
    20→            return False
    21→        plan = load_plan()
    22→        queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    23→        if not queue_order:
    24→            return False
    25→
    26→        result = build_work_queue(
    27→            state,
    28→            options=QueueBuildOptions(
    29→                plan=plan,
    30→                count=1,
    31→                collapse_clusters=True,
    32→                include_subjective=True,
    33→            ),
    34→        )
    35→        if not result["items"]:
    36→            return False
    37→
    38→        front_item = result["items"][0]
    39→        front_id = front_item["id"]
    40→
    41→        front_ids: set[str] = set()
    42→        if front_item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    43→            for member in front_item.get("members", []):
    44→                front_ids.add(member["id"])
    45→            front_ids.add(front_id)
    46→        else:
    47→            front_ids.add(front_id)
    48→
    49→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    50→        resolved_ids: set[str] = set()
    51→        for pattern in patterns:
    52→            if pattern in clusters:
    53→                resolved_ids.update(clusters[pattern].get("finding_ids", []))
    54→                resolved_ids.add(pattern)
    55→            else:
    56→                resolved_ids.add(pattern)
    57→
    58→        findings = state.get("findings", {})
    59→        resolved_ids = {
    60→            finding_id
    61→            for finding_id in resolved_ids
    62→            if finding_id in clusters
    63→            or (finding_id in findings and findings[finding_id].get("status") == "open")
    64→        }
    65→        if not resolved_ids:
    66→            return False
    67→
    68→        out_of_order = resolved_ids - front_ids
    69→        for cluster_id in list(out_of_order):
    70→            if cluster_id in clusters:
    71→                alive_members = {
    72→                    finding_id
    73→                    for finding_id in clusters[cluster_id].get("finding_ids", [])
    74→                    if finding_id in findings and findings[finding_id].get("status") == "open"
    75→                }
    76→                if alive_members and alive_members <= front_ids:
    77→                    out_of_order.discard(cluster_id)
    78→        if not out_of_order:
    79→            return False
    80→
    81→        print(colorize("\n  Queue order violation: these items are not next in the plan queue:\n", "yellow"))
    82→        for finding_id in sorted(out_of_order):
    83→            print(f"    {finding_id}")
    84→        print(colorize(f"\n  The current next item is: {front_id}", "dim"))
    85→        print(colorize("  Items must be resolved in plan order. If you need to reprioritize:", "dim"))
    86→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan move <pattern> --position top    # move to front", "dim"))
    87→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan skip <pattern> --reason '...'    # skip for now", "dim"))
    88→        print(colorize("    desloppify next                                  # see what's next\n", "dim"))
    89→        return True
    90→    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
    91→        return False
    92→
    93→
    94→__all__ = ["_check_queue_order_guard"]
    95→

   182→        help=(
   183→            "Submit external reviewer JSON via a started session; "
   184→            "CLI injects canonical provenance before import"
   185→        ),
   186→    )
   187→    g_external.add_argument(
   188→        "--session-id",
   189→        type=str,
   190→        default=None,
   191→        help="External review session id for --external-submit",
   192→    )
   193→    g_external.add_argument(
   194→        "--external-runner",
   195→        choices=["claude"],
   196→        default="claude",
   197→        help="External reviewer runner for --external-start (default: claude)",
   198→    )
   199→    g_external.add_argument(
   200→        "--session-ttl-hours",
   201→        type=int,
   202→        default=24,
   203→        help="External review session expiration in hours (default: 24)",
   204→    )
   205→
   206→    # -- batch execution --
   207→    g_batch = p_review.add_argument_group("batch execution")
   208→    g_batch.add_argument(
   209→        "--run-batches",
   210→        action="store_true",
   211→        help="Run holistic investigation batches with subagents and merge/import output",
   212→    )
   213→    g_batch.add_argument(
   214→        "--runner",
   215→        choices=["codex"],
   216→        default="codex",
   217→        help="Subagent runner backend (default: codex)",
   218→    )
   219→    g_batch.add_argument(
   220→        "--parallel", action="store_true", help="Run selected batches in parallel"
   221→    )
   222→    g_batch.add_argument(
   223→        "--max-parallel-batches",
   224→        type=int,
   225→        default=3,
   226→        help=(
   227→            "Max concurrent subagent batches when --parallel is enabled "
   228→            "(default: 3)"
   229→        ),
   230→    )
   231→    g_batch.add_argument(
   232→        "--batch-timeout-seconds",
   233→        type=int,
   234→        default=20 * 60,
   235→        help="Per-batch runner timeout in seconds (default: 1200)",
   236→    )
   237→    g_batch.add_argument(
   238→        "--batch-max-retries",
   239→        type=int,
   240→        default=1,
   241→        help=(
   242→            "Retries per failed batch for transient runner/network errors "
   243→            "(default: 1)"
   244→        ),
   245→    )
   246→    g_batch.add_argument(
   247→        "--batch-retry-backoff-seconds",
   248→        type=float,
   249→        default=2.0,
   250→        help=(
   251→            "Base backoff delay for transient batch retries in seconds "
   252→            "(default: 2.0)"
   253→        ),
   254→    )
   255→    g_batch.add_argument(
   256→        "--batch-heartbeat-seconds",
   257→        type=float,
   258→        default=15.0,
   259→        help=(
   260→            "Progress heartbeat interval during parallel batch runs in seconds "
   261→            "(default: 15.0)"
   262→        ),
   263→    )
   264→    g_batch.add_argument(
   265→        "--batch-stall-warning-seconds",
   266→        type=int,
   267→        default=0,
   268→        help=(
   269→            "Emit warning when a running batch exceeds this elapsed time "
   270→            "(0 disables warnings; does not terminate the batch)"
   271→        ),
   272→    )
   273→    g_batch.add_argument(
   274→        "--batch-stall-kill-seconds",
   275→        type=int,
   276→        default=120,
   277→        help=(
   278→            "Terminate a batch when output state is unchanged and runner streams are idle "
   279→            "for this many seconds (default: 120; 0 disables kill recovery)"
   280→        ),
   281→    )
   282→    g_batch.add_argument(
   283→        "--dry-run",
   284→        action="store_true",
   285→        help="Generate packet/prompts only (skip runner/import)",
   286→    )
   287→    g_batch.add_argument(
   288→        "--run-log-file",
   289→        type=str,
   290→        default=None,
   291→        help=(
   292→            "Optional explicit path for live run log output "
   293→            "(overrides default run artifacts path)"
   294→        ),
   295→    )
   296→    g_batch.add_argument(
   297→        "--packet",
   298→        type=str,
   299→        default=None,
   300→        help="Use an existing immutable packet JSON instead of preparing a new one",
   301→    )
   302→    g_batch.add_argument(
   303→        "--only-batches",
   304→        type=str,
   305→        default=None,
   306→        help="Comma-separated 1-based batch indexes to run (e.g. 1,3,5)",
   307→    )
   308→    g_batch.add_argument(
   309→        "--scan-after-import",
   310→        action="store_true",
   311→        help="Run `scan` after successful merged import",
   312→    )
   313→    g_batch.add_argument(
   314→        "--import-run",
   315→        dest="import_run_dir",
   316→        type=str,
   317→        metavar="DIR",
   318→        default=None,
   319→        help=(
   320→            "Re-import results from a completed run directory "
   321→            "(replays merge+import when the original pipeline was interrupted)"
   322→        ),
   323→    )
   324→
   325→    # -- trust & attestation --
   326→    g_trust = p_review.add_argument_group("trust & attestation")
   327→    g_trust.add_argument(
   328→        "--manual-override",
   329→        action="store_true",
   330→        help=(
   331→            "Allow untrusted assessment score imports. Findings always import; "
   332→            "scores require trusted blind provenance unless this override is set."
   333→        ),
   334→    )
   335→    g_trust.add_argument(
   336→        "--attested-external",
   337→        action="store_true",
   338→        help=(
   339→            "Accept external blind-run assessments as durable scores when "
   340→            "paired with --attest and valid blind packet provenance "
   341→            "(intended for cloud Claude subagent workflows)."
   342→        ),
   343→    )
   344→    g_trust.add_argument(
   345→        "--attest",
   346→        type=str,
   347→        default=None,
   348→        help=(
   349→            "Required with --manual-override or --attested-external. "
   350→            "For attested external imports include both phrases "
   351→            "'without awareness' and 'unbiased'."
   352→        ),
   353→    )
   354→
   355→    # -- post-processing --
   356→    g_post = p_review.add_argument_group("post-processing")
   357→    g_post.add_argument(
   358→        "--merge",
   359→        action="store_true",
   360→        help="Merge conceptually duplicate open review findings",
   361→    )
   362→    g_post.add_argument(
   363→        "--similarity",
   364→        type=float,
   365→        default=0.8,
   366→        help="Summary similarity threshold for merge (0-1, default: 0.8)",
   367→    )
   368→
   369→    # -- deprecated --
   370→    g_deprecated = p_review.add_argument_group("deprecated")
   371→    g_deprecated.add_argument(
   372→        "--max-age",
   373→        type=int,
   374→        default=None,
   375→        action=_DeprecatedAction,
   376→        help="Deprecated in holistic-only mode (ignored)",
   377→    )
   378→    g_deprecated.add_argument(
   379→        "--max-files",
   380→        type=int,
   381→        default=None,
   382→        action=_DeprecatedAction,
   383→        help="Deprecated in holistic-only mode (ignored)",
   384→    )
   385→    g_deprecated.add_argument(
   386→        "--refresh",
   387→        action=_DeprecatedBoolAction,
   388→        help="Deprecated in holistic-only mode (ignored)",
   389→    )
   390→    g_deprecated.add_argument(
   391→        "--holistic",
   392→        action=_DeprecatedBoolAction,
   393→        help="Deprecated: holistic is now the only review mode",
   394→    )
   395→    g_deprecated.add_argument(
   396→        "--save-run-log",
   397→        action="store_true",
   398→        help=(
   399→            "Deprecated no-op: run logs are now always saved while running "
   400→            "(default location: run artifacts dir)"
   401→        ),
   402→    )

   405→def _add_zone_parser(sub) -> None:
   406→    p_zone = sub.add_parser("zone", help="Show/set/clear zone classifications")
   407→    p_zone.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None, help="Project root directory (default: auto-detected)")
   408→    p_zone.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None, help="Path to state file")
   409→    zone_sub = p_zone.add_subparsers(dest="zone_action")
   410→    zone_sub.add_parser("show", help="Show zone classifications for all files")
   411→    z_set = zone_sub.add_parser("set", help="Override zone for a file")
   412→    z_set.add_argument("zone_path", type=str, help="Relative file path")
   413→    z_set.add_argument(
   414→        "zone_value",
   415→        type=str,
   416→        help="Zone (production, test, config, generated, script, vendor)",
   417→    )
   418→    z_clear = zone_sub.add_parser("clear", help="Remove zone override for a file")
   419→    z_clear.add_argument("zone_path", type=str, help="Relative file path")
   420→
   421→
   422→def _add_config_parser(sub) -> None:
   423→    p_config = sub.add_parser("config", help="Show/set/unset project configuration")
   424→    config_sub = p_config.add_subparsers(dest="config_action")
   425→    config_sub.add_parser("show", help="Show all config values")
   426→    c_set = config_sub.add_parser("set", help="Set a config value")
   427→    c_set.add_argument("config_key", type=str, help="Config key name")
   428→    c_set.add_argument("config_value", type=str, help="Value to set")
   429→    c_unset = config_sub.add_parser("unset", help="Reset a config key to default")
   430→    c_unset.add_argument("config_key", type=str, help="Config key name")
   431→
   432→
   433→def _fixer_help_lines(langs: list[str]) -> list[str]:
   434→    fixer_help_lines: list[str] = []
   435→    for lang_name in langs:
   436→        try:
   437→            fixer_names = sorted(get_lang(lang_name).fixers.keys())
   438→        except (ImportError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError):
   439→            fixer_names = []
   440→        fixer_list = ", ".join(fixer_names) if fixer_names else "none yet"
   441→        fixer_help_lines.append(f"fixers ({lang_name}): {fixer_list}")
   442→    fixer_help_lines.append("special: review — prepare structured review data")
   443→    return fixer_help_lines
   444→
   445→
   446→def _add_fix_parser(sub, langs: list[str]) -> None:
   447→    p_fix = sub.add_parser(
   448→        "autofix",
   449→        help="Auto-fix mechanical issues",
   450→        epilog="\n".join(_fixer_help_lines(langs)),
   451→    )
   452→    p_fix.add_argument("fixer", type=str, help="What to fix")
   453→    p_fix.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None, help="Project root directory (default: auto-detected)")
   454→    p_fix.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None, help="Path to state file")
   455→    p_fix.add_argument(
   456→        "--dry-run",
   457→        action="store_true",
   458→        help="Show what would change without modifying files",
   459→    )
   460→
   461→
   462→def _add_plan_parser(sub) -> None:
   463→    p_plan = sub.add_parser(
   464→        "plan",
   465→        help="Living plan: generate, reorder, cluster, skip, note",
   466→        description="""\
   467→Manage the living plan — a persistent layer on top of the work queue.
   468→Track custom ordering, clusters, skips, and per-finding annotations.
   469→Run with no subcommand to generate a full prioritized markdown plan.""",
   470→        epilog="""\
   471→typical workflow:
   472→  desloppify scan                       # detect findings
   473→  desloppify plan                       # full prioritized markdown
   474→  desloppify plan queue                 # compact table of all items
   475→  desloppify plan cluster create ...    # group related findings
   476→  desloppify plan focus <cluster>       # narrow scope
   477→  desloppify next                       # work on the next item
   478→  desloppify plan done <id> --attest .. # mark as fixed
   479→
   480→patterns (used by move, skip, done, describe, note, etc.):
   481→  Patterns match findings by detector, file, ID prefix, glob, or name.
   482→  Cluster names also work as patterns — they expand to all member IDs.
   483→  Examples: "security", "src/foo.py", "unused::*React*", "my-cluster"
   484→
   485→subcommands:
   486→  show       Show plan metadata summary
   487→  queue      Compact table of upcoming queue items
   488→  reset      Reset plan to empty
   489→  move       Move findings or clusters in the queue
   490→  done       Mark findings as fixed (score movement + next-step)
   491→  describe   Set augmented description
   492→  note       Set note on findings
   493→  skip       Skip findings (temporary/permanent/false_positive)
   494→  unskip     Bring skipped findings back to queue
   495→  reopen     Reopen resolved findings
   496→  focus      Set or clear active cluster focus
   497→  cluster    Manage finding clusters
   498→  triage     Staged triage workflow (after review)""",
   499→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
   500→    )
   501→    p_plan.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None, help="Path to state file")
   502→    p_plan.add_argument(
   503→        "--output", type=str, metavar="FILE", help="Write to file instead of stdout"
   504→    )
   505→
   506→    plan_sub = p_plan.add_subparsers(dest="plan_action")
   507→
   508→    # plan show
   509→    plan_sub.add_parser("show", help="Show plan metadata summary")
   510→
   511→    # plan queue
   512→    p_queue = plan_sub.add_parser("queue", help="Compact table of upcoming queue items")
   513→    p_queue.add_argument("--top", type=int, default=30, help="Max items (default: 30, 0=all)")
   514→    p_queue.add_argument("--cluster", type=str, default=None, metavar="NAME",
   515→                         help="Filter to a specific cluster")
   516→    p_queue.add_argument("--include-skipped", action="store_true",
   517→                         help="Include skipped items at end")
   518→    p_queue.add_argument("--sort", choices=["priority", "recent"], default="priority",
   519→                         help="Sort order (default: priority)")
   520→
   521→    # plan reset
   522→    plan_sub.add_parser("reset", help="Reset plan to empty")
   523→
   524→    # plan move <patterns> <position> [--target TARGET]
   525→    p_move = plan_sub.add_parser(
   526→        "move",
   527→        help="Move findings in the queue",
   528→        epilog="""\
   529→patterns accept finding IDs, detector names, file paths, globs, or cluster names.
   530→cluster names expand to all member IDs automatically.
   531→
   532→examples:
   533→  desloppify plan move security top                         # all findings from detector
   534→  desloppify plan move "unused::src/foo.ts::*" top          # glob pattern
   535→  desloppify plan move smells bottom                        # deprioritize
   536→  desloppify plan move my-cluster top                       # cluster members
   537→  desloppify plan move my-cluster unused top                # mix clusters + findings
   538→  desloppify plan move unused before -t security            # before a finding/cluster
   539→  desloppify plan move smells after -t my-cluster           # after a cluster
   540→  desloppify plan move security up -t 3                     # shift up 3 positions""",
   541→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
   542→    )
   543→    p_move.add_argument(
   544→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   545→        help="Finding ID(s), detector, file path, glob, or cluster name",
   546→    )
   547→    p_move.add_argument(
   548→        "position", choices=["top", "bottom", "before", "after", "up", "down"],
   549→        help="Where to move",
   550→    )
   551→    p_move.add_argument(
   552→        "-t", "--target", default=None,
   553→        help="Required for before/after (finding ID or cluster name) and up/down (integer offset)",
   554→    )
   555→
   556→    # plan describe <patterns> "<text>"
   557→    p_describe = plan_sub.add_parser("describe", help="Set augmented description")
   558→    p_describe.add_argument(
   559→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   560→        help="Finding ID(s), detector, file path, glob, or cluster name",
   561→    )
   562→    p_describe.add_argument("text", type=str, help="Description text")
   563→
   564→    # plan note <patterns> "<text>"
   565→    p_note = plan_sub.add_parser("note", help="Set note on findings")
   566→    p_note.add_argument(
   567→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   568→        help="Finding ID(s), detector, file path, glob, or cluster name",
   569→    )
   570→    p_note.add_argument("text", type=str, help="Note text")
   571→
   572→    # plan skip <patterns> [--reason] [--review-after N] [--permanent] [--false-positive] [--note] [--attest]
   573→    p_skip = plan_sub.add_parser(
   574→        "skip",
   575→        help="Skip findings: temporary (default), --permanent (wontfix), or --false-positive",
   576→    )
   577→    p_skip.add_argument(
   578→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   579→        help="Finding ID(s), detector, file path, glob, or cluster name",
   580→    )
   581→    p_skip.add_argument("--reason", type=str, default=None, help="Why this is being skipped")
   582→    p_skip.add_argument(
   583→        "--review-after", type=int, default=None, metavar="N",
   584→        help="Re-surface after N scans (temporary only)",
   585→    )
   586→    p_skip.add_argument(
   587→        "--permanent", action="store_true",
   588→        help="Mark as wontfix (score-affecting, requires --note and --attest)",
   589→    )
   590→    p_skip.add_argument(
   591→        "--false-positive", action="store_true",
   592→        help="Mark as false positive (requires --attest)",
   593→    )
   594→    p_skip.add_argument("--note", type=str, default=None, help="Explanation (required for --permanent)")
   595→    p_skip.add_argument(
   596→        "--attest", type=str, default=None,
   597→        help="Attestation (required for --permanent and --false-positive)",
   598→    )
   599→
   600→    # plan unskip <patterns>
   601→    p_unskip = plan_sub.add_parser(
   602→        "unskip", help="Bring skipped findings back to queue (reopens permanent/fp in state)"
   603→    )
   604→    p_unskip.add_argument(
   605→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   606→        help="Finding ID(s), detector, file path, glob, or cluster name",
   607→    )
   608→
   609→    # plan reopen <patterns>
   610→    p_reopen = plan_sub.add_parser(
   611→        "reopen", help="Reopen resolved findings and move back to queue"
   612→    )
   613→    p_reopen.add_argument(
   614→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   615→        help="Finding ID(s), detector, file path, glob, or cluster name",
   616→    )
   617→
   618→    # plan done <patterns> --attest [--note]
   619→    p_done = plan_sub.add_parser(
   620→        "done",
   621→        help="Mark findings as fixed (shows score movement + next step)",
   622→        epilog="""\
   623→examples:
   624→  desloppify plan done "unused::src/foo.tsx::React" \\
   625→    --attest "I have actually removed the import and I am not gaming the score."
   626→  desloppify plan done security --note "patched XSS" \\
   627→    --attest "I have actually ..."  """,
   628→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
   629→    )
   630→    p_done.add_argument(
   631→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   632→        help="Finding ID(s), detector, file path, glob, or cluster name",
   633→    )
   634→    p_done.add_argument(
   635→        "--note", type=str, default=None, help="Explanation of the fix"
   636→    )
   637→    p_done.add_argument(
   638→        "--attest",
   639→        type=str,
   640→        default=None,
   641→        help=(
   642→            "Required anti-gaming attestation. Must include BOTH keywords "
   643→            "'I have actually' and 'not gaming'."
   644→        ),
   645→    )
   646→    p_done.add_argument(
   647→        "--confirm",
   648→        action="store_true",
   649→        default=False,
   650→        help="Auto-generate attestation from --note (requires --note)",
   651→    )
   652→    p_done.add_argument(
   653→        "--force-resolve",
   654→        action="store_true",
   655→        default=False,
   656→        dest="force_resolve",
   657→        help="Bypass triage guardrail when new findings are pending triage",
   658→    )
   659→
   660→    # plan focus <cluster> | --clear
   661→    p_focus = plan_sub.add_parser("focus", help="Set or clear active cluster focus")
   662→    p_focus.add_argument("cluster_name", nargs="?", default=None, help="Cluster name")
   663→    p_focus.add_argument("--clear", action="store_true", help="Clear focus")
   664→
   665→    # plan cluster ...
   666→    p_cluster = plan_sub.add_parser(
   667→        "cluster",
   668→        help="Manage finding clusters",
   669→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
   670→    )
   671→    cluster_sub = p_cluster.add_subparsers(dest="cluster_action")
   672→
   673→    # plan cluster create <name> [--description "..."] [--action "..."]
   674→    p_cc = cluster_sub.add_parser("create", help="Create a cluster")
   675→    p_cc.add_argument("cluster_name", type=str, help="Cluster name (slug)")
   676→    p_cc.add_argument("--description", type=str, default=None, help="Cluster description")
   677→    p_cc.add_argument("--action", type=str, default=None, help="Primary action/command for this cluster")
   678→
   679→    # plan cluster add <cluster> <patterns...>
   680→    p_ca = cluster_sub.add_parser("add", help="Add findings to a cluster")
   681→    p_ca.add_argument("cluster_name", type=str, help="Cluster name")
   682→    p_ca.add_argument("patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN", help="Finding ID(s), detector, file path, glob, or cluster name")
   683→
   684→    # plan cluster remove <cluster> <patterns...>
   685→    p_cr = cluster_sub.add_parser("remove", help="Remove findings from a cluster")
   686→    p_cr.add_argument("cluster_name", type=str, help="Cluster name")
   687→    p_cr.add_argument("patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN", help="Finding ID(s), detector, file path, glob, or cluster name")
   688→
   689→    # plan cluster delete <name>
   690→    p_cd = cluster_sub.add_parser("delete", help="Delete a cluster")
   691→    p_cd.add_argument("cluster_name", type=str, help="Cluster name")
   692→
   693→    # plan cluster move <cluster[,cluster…]> <position> [target]
   694→    p_cm = cluster_sub.add_parser("move", help="Move cluster(s) as a block")
   695→    p_cm.add_argument("cluster_names", type=str, help="Cluster name(s), comma-separated for multiple")
   696→    p_cm.add_argument(
   697→        "position", choices=["top", "bottom", "before", "after", "up", "down"],
   698→        help="Where to move",
   699→    )
   700→    p_cm.add_argument("target", nargs="?", default=None, help="Target finding/cluster (before/after) or integer offset (up/down)")
   701→
   702→    # plan cluster show <name>
   703→    p_cs = cluster_sub.add_parser("show", help="Show cluster details and members")
   704→    p_cs.add_argument("cluster_name", type=str, help="Cluster name")
   705→
   706→    # plan cluster list
   707→    cluster_sub.add_parser("list", help="List all clusters")
   708→
   709→    # plan cluster merge <source> <target>
   710→    p_cmerge = cluster_sub.add_parser("merge", help="Merge source cluster into target (moves findings, deletes source)")
   711→    p_cmerge.add_argument("source", type=str, help="Source cluster name (will be deleted)")
   712→    p_cmerge.add_argument("target", type=str, help="Target cluster name (receives findings)")
   713→

1→"""Scan preflight guard: warn and gate scan when queue has unfinished items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→import sys
     7→
     8→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import plan_aware_queue_count
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
    11→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS, CommandError
    12→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    13→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan, save_plan
    14→
    15→
    16→def scan_queue_preflight(args) -> None:
    17→    """Warn and gate scan when queue has unfinished items."""
    18→    # CI profile always passes
    19→    if getattr(args, "profile", None) == "ci":
    20→        return
    21→
    22→    # --force-rescan with valid attestation bypasses
    23→    if getattr(args, "force_rescan", False):
    24→        attest = getattr(args, "attest", None) or ""
    25→        if "i understand" not in attest.lower():
    26→            print(
    27→                colorize(
    28→                    '  --force-rescan requires --attest "I understand this is not '
    29→                    "the intended workflow and I am intentionally skipping queue "
    30→                    'completion"',
    31→                    "red",
    32→                ),
    33→                file=sys.stderr,
    34→            )
    35→            sys.exit(1)
    36→        print(
    37→            colorize(
    38→                "  --force-rescan: bypassing queue completion check. "
    39→                "Plan-start score will be reset.",
    40→                "yellow",
    41→            )
    42→        )
    43→        # Clear plan_start_scores
    44→        try:
    45→            plan = load_plan()
    46→            if plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    47→                plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
    48→                save_plan(plan)
    49→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
    50→            logging.debug("Plan score cleanup skipped: %s", exc)
    51→        return
    52→
    53→    # No plan = no gate (first scan, or user never uses plan)
    54→    try:
    55→        plan = load_plan()
    56→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    57→        return
    58→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    59→        return  # No active cycle
    60→
    61→    # Count plan-aware remaining items
    62→    try:
    63→        state = state_mod.load_state(state_path(args))
    64→        remaining = plan_aware_queue_count(state, plan)
    65→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    66→        return
    67→    if remaining == 0:
    68→        return  # Queue clear, scan allowed
    69→
    70→    # GATE
    71→    print(
    72→        colorize(
    73→            f"\n  WARNING: {remaining} item{'s' if remaining != 1 else ''}"
    74→            " remaining in your queue.",
    75→            "red",
    76→        ),
    77→        file=sys.stderr,
    78→    )
    79→    print(
    80→        colorize(
    81→            "  The intended workflow is to complete the queue before scanning.",
    82→            "red",
    83→        ),
    84→        file=sys.stderr,
    85→    )
    86→    print(
    87→        colorize(
    88→            "  Work through items with `desloppify next`, then scan when clear.",
    89→            "dim",
    90→        ),
    91→        file=sys.stderr,
    92→    )
    93→    print(
    94→        colorize(
    95→            "\n  To force a rescan (resets your plan-start score):",
    96→            "dim",
    97→        ),
    98→        file=sys.stderr,
    99→    )
   100→    print(
   101→        colorize(
   102→            '    desloppify scan --force-rescan --attest "I understand this is not '
   103→            "the intended workflow and I am intentionally skipping queue "
   104→            'completion"',
   105→            "yellow",
   106→        ),
   107→        file=sys.stderr,
   108→    )
   109→    sys.exit(1)
   110→
   111→
   112→__all__ = ["scan_queue_preflight"]
   113→

1→"""Scope and queue helpers for show command selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify import scoring as scoring_mod
     8→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     9→from desloppify.core import registry as registry_mod
    10→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
    11→    QueueBuildOptions,
    12→    build_work_queue,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    15→
    16→
    17→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    18→class ResolvedEntity:
    19→    """Classifies what the user's show pattern refers to."""
    20→
    21→    kind: str  # "dimension", "special_view", "file_or_pattern"
    22→    pattern: str  # Original user pattern
    23→    display_name: str  # Human-readable label
    24→    detectors: tuple[str, ...] = ()  # Detector names (for dimension kind)
    25→    is_subjective: bool = False  # Whether this is a subjective dimension
    26→
    27→
    28→def _resolve_special_view(pattern: str, lowered: str) -> ResolvedEntity | None:
    29→    if lowered not in ("concerns", "subjective", "subjective_review"):
    30→        return None
    31→    return ResolvedEntity(
    32→        kind="special_view",
    33→        pattern=pattern,
    34→        display_name=pattern,
    35→    )
    36→
    37→
    38→def _resolve_mechanical_dimension(pattern: str, lowered: str) -> ResolvedEntity | None:
    39→    lookup = _build_dimension_lookup()
    40→    detectors = lookup.get(lowered) or lookup.get(pattern.lower())
    41→    if not detectors:
    42→        return None
    43→    dim_display = pattern
    44→    for dim in scoring_mod.DIMENSIONS:
    45→        dim_lowered = dim.name.lower().replace(" ", "_")
    46→        if dim.name.lower() == lowered or dim_lowered == lowered:
    47→            dim_display = dim.name
    48→            break
    49→    return ResolvedEntity(
    50→        kind="dimension",
    51→        pattern=pattern,
    52→        display_name=dim_display,
    53→        detectors=tuple(detectors),
    54→        is_subjective=False,
    55→    )
    56→
    57→
    58→def _resolve_subjective_dimension(
    59→    pattern: str,
    60→    lowered: str,
    61→    state: dict,
    62→) -> ResolvedEntity | None:
    63→    display_name = scoring_mod.DISPLAY_NAMES.get(lowered)
    64→    if not display_name:
    65→        for key in state.get("dimension_scores") or {}:
    66→            if key.lower().replace(" ", "_") == lowered:
    67→                display_name = key
    68→                break
    69→    if not display_name:
    70→        return None
    71→    dim_data, display_name = _lookup_dimension_score(state, display_name)
    72→    is_subj = "subjective_assessment" in (
    73→        dim_data.get("detectors", {}) if isinstance(dim_data, dict) else {}
    74→    )
    75→    return ResolvedEntity(
    76→        kind="dimension",
    77→        pattern=pattern,
    78→        display_name=display_name,
    79→        detectors=(),
    80→        is_subjective=is_subj,
    81→    )
    82→
    83→
    84→def resolve_entity(pattern: str, state: dict) -> ResolvedEntity:
    85→    """Classify a user pattern as a dimension, special view, or passthrough.
    86→
    87→    Resolution priority:
    88→    1. Special views: "concerns", "subjective", "subjective_review"
    89→    2. Mechanical dimension name (from DIMENSIONS)
    90→    3. Subjective dimension name (from DISPLAY_NAMES / dimension_scores)
    91→    4. Everything else: file_or_pattern passthrough
    92→    """
    93→    lowered = pattern.strip().lower().replace(" ", "_")
    94→
    95→    # 1. Special views
    96→    special_view = _resolve_special_view(pattern, lowered)
    97→    if special_view is not None:
    98→        return special_view
    99→
   100→    # 2. Mechanical dimension (via DIMENSIONS list)
   101→    mechanical = _resolve_mechanical_dimension(pattern, lowered)
   102→    if mechanical is not None:
   103→        return mechanical
   104→
   105→    # 3. Subjective dimension (via DISPLAY_NAMES or dimension_scores)
   106→    subjective = _resolve_subjective_dimension(pattern, lowered, state)
   107→    if subjective is not None:
   108→        return subjective
   109→
   110→    # 4. Everything else
   111→    return ResolvedEntity(
   112→        kind="file_or_pattern",
   113→        pattern=pattern,
   114→        display_name=pattern,
   115→    )
   116→
   117→
   118→def _build_dimension_lookup() -> dict[str, list[str]]:
   119→    """Build a map from dimension name/key (lowered) to detector names."""
   120→    lookup: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   121→    for dim in scoring_mod.DIMENSIONS:
   122→        detectors = list(dim.detectors) if hasattr(dim, "detectors") else []
   123→        lookup[dim.name.lower()] = detectors
   124→        # Also index by underscore key: "file_health" -> detectors
   125→        key = dim.name.lower().replace(" ", "_")
   126→        if key not in lookup:
   127→            lookup[key] = detectors
   128→    # Also add DISPLAY_NAMES reverse lookup (e.g. "abstraction_fit" -> "Abstraction Fit")
   129→    for key, display in scoring_mod.DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   130→        normalized_key = key.lower().replace(" ", "_")
   131→        normalized_display = display.lower()
   132→        # Find which dimension this belongs to via get_dimension_for_detector or direct name match
   133→        for dim in scoring_mod.DIMENSIONS:
   134→            dim_lower = dim.name.lower()
   135→            if normalized_display == dim_lower or normalized_key == dim_lower.replace(" ", "_"):
   136→                detectors = list(dim.detectors) if hasattr(dim, "detectors") else []
   137→                if normalized_key not in lookup:
   138→                    lookup[normalized_key] = detectors
   139→                if normalized_display not in lookup:
   140→                    lookup[normalized_display] = detectors
   141→    return lookup
   142→
   143→
   144→def _lookup_dimension_score(
   145→    state: dict, display_name: str,
   146→) -> tuple[dict, str]:
   147→    """Find dimension_scores entry with case-insensitive fallback.
   148→
   149→    Returns (dim_data_dict, resolved_display_name).
   150→    """
   151→    lowered = display_name.lower().replace(" ", "_")
   152→    dim_data = (state.get("dimension_scores") or {}).get(display_name, {})
   153→    if not dim_data:
   154→        for ds_key, ds_val in (state.get("dimension_scores") or {}).items():
   155→            if ds_key.lower().replace(" ", "_") == lowered:
   156→                dim_data = ds_val
   157→                display_name = ds_key
   158→                break
   159→    return dim_data, display_name
   160→
   161→
   162→def _detector_names_hint() -> str:
   163→    """Return a compact list of detector names for the help message."""
   164→    names = getattr(registry_mod, "DISPLAY_ORDER", [])
   165→    if names:
   166→        return ", ".join(names[:10]) + (", ..." if len(names) > 10 else "")
   167→    return "smells, structural, security, review, ..."
   168→
   169→
   170→def resolve_show_scope(args) -> tuple[bool, str | None, str, str | None]:
   171→    """Resolve scope/pattern/status for a show invocation."""
   172→    chronic = getattr(args, "chronic", False)
   173→    pattern = args.pattern
   174→    status_filter = "open" if chronic else getattr(args, "status", "open")
   175→    if chronic:
   176→        scope = pattern
   177→        pattern = pattern or "<chronic>"
   178→        return True, pattern, status_filter, scope
   179→    if not pattern:
   180→        print(
   181→            colorize(
   182→                "Pattern required (or use --chronic). Try: desloppify show --help",
   183→                "yellow",
   184→            )
   185→        )
   186→        return False, None, status_filter, ""
   187→    return True, pattern, status_filter, pattern
   188→
   189→
   190→def load_matches(
   191→    state: dict,
   192→    *,
   193→    scope: str | None,
   194→    status_filter: str,
   195→    chronic: bool,
   196→) -> list[dict]:
   197→    """Load matching findings from the ranked queue."""
   198→    queue = build_work_queue(
   199→        state,
   200→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   201→            count=None,
   202→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   203→            scope=scope,
   204→            status=status_filter,
   205→            include_subjective=False,
   206→            chronic=chronic,
   207→        ),
   208→    )
   209→    return [item for item in queue.get("items", []) if item.get("kind") == "finding"]
   210→
   211→
   212→def resolve_noise(config: dict, matches: list[dict], *, no_budget: bool = False):
   213→    """Apply detector/global noise budget to show matches.
   214→
   215→    When *no_budget* is True, all matches are surfaced (nothing hidden).
   216→    """
   217→    if no_budget:
   218→        return (
   219→            matches,
   220→            {},
   221→            0,
   222→            0,
   223→            None,
   224→        )
   225→    noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning = (
   226→        state_mod.resolve_finding_noise_settings(config)
   227→    )
   228→    surfaced_matches, hidden_by_detector = state_mod.apply_finding_noise_budget(
   229→        matches,
   230→        budget=noise_budget,
   231→        global_budget=global_noise_budget,
   232→    )
   233→    return (
   234→        surfaced_matches,
   235→        hidden_by_detector,
   236→        noise_budget,
   237→        global_noise_budget,
   238→        budget_warning,
   239→    )
   240→
   241→
   242→__all__ = [
   243→    "ResolvedEntity",
   244→    "_detector_names_hint",
   245→    "_lookup_dimension_score",
   246→    "load_matches",
   247→    "resolve_entity",
   248→    "resolve_noise",
   249→    "resolve_show_scope",
   250→]
   251→
```

1→"""Fix command option and fixer resolution helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import dataclasses
     6→from collections.abc import Callable
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
     9→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import CommandError
    10→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import FixerConfig, LangConfig
    11→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    12→
    13→_COMMAND_POST_FIX: dict[str, Callable[..., None]] = {}
    14→
    15→
    16→def _load_fixer(args, fixer_name: str) -> tuple[LangConfig, FixerConfig]:
    17→    """Resolve fixer from language plugin registry, or exit."""
    18→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    19→    if not lang:
    20→        raise CommandError("Could not detect language. Use --lang to specify.")
    21→    if not lang.fixers:
    22→        raise CommandError(f"No auto-fixers available for {lang.name}.")
    23→    if fixer_name not in lang.fixers:
    24→        available = ", ".join(sorted(lang.fixers.keys()))
    25→        raise CommandError(
    26→            f"Unknown fixer: {fixer_name}\n  Available: {available}"
    27→        )
    28→    fc = lang.fixers[fixer_name]
    29→    if fixer_name in _COMMAND_POST_FIX and not fc.post_fix:
    30→        fc = dataclasses.replace(fc, post_fix=_COMMAND_POST_FIX[fixer_name])
    31→    return lang, fc
    32→
```
